# ============================================================================
# DGTL version 2.2.0  |  amdgpu_core.dgtl_isel  (AMD ROCm / GCN)
# Architecture:  AMD GCN / CDNA / RDNA (gfx90a, gfx942, gfx1100)
# Model:         Wavefront Execution (Wave32 / Wave64)
# ============================================================================

include "common_runtime_forms.dgtl_isel"
include "core_base.dgtl_isel"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# AMDGCN PEEPHOLE REWRITE & FUSED VECTOR INSTRUCTIONS
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# V_FMA_F32 / V_FMA_F64 native GCN instructions
rule amdgcn_fma_f32:              AddF32(MulF32($a, $b), $c) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=rocm|amdgcn|hip, benefit=100]
rule amdgcn_fma_f32_rev:          AddF32($c, MulF32($a, $b)) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=rocm|amdgcn|hip, benefit=100]
rule amdgcn_fma_f64:              AddF64(MulF64($a, $b), $c) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=rocm|amdgcn|hip, benefit=100]

# V_CNDMASK_B32 (conditional move from EXEC mask)
rule amdgcn_fold_cndmask:         Select(CmpEqI32($a, $b), $t, $f) -> SELECT($a, $t, $f) [targets=rocm|amdgcn|hip, benefit=30]

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# AMD GCN form override
# GCN/CDNA/RDNA all have native V_FMA_F32 and V_FMA_F64.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form fma_f32:                     opcode=FMA_F32,               machine_form=FloatTernaryFma32

# ============================================================================
# DGTL version 2.2.0  |  arm64_core.dgtl_isel  (AArch64 / ARMv8-A / ARMv9-A)
# Architecture:  ARMv8-A / ARMv9-A â€” NEON, SVE, SVE2, LSE Atomics
# ABI:           AAPCS64 / Apple Silicon AArch64
# ============================================================================

include "common_runtime_forms.dgtl_isel"
include "core_base.dgtl_isel"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# AArch64 PEEPHOLE REWRITE & FUSION RULES
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Shifted-register addressing (ADD Xd, Xn, Xm, LSL #shift)
rule arm64_fold_lsl_add:          AddI64($a, ShlI64($b, $shift)) -> ADD_I64($a, $b) [targets=arm64|aarch64, benefit=35]
rule arm64_fold_lsr_sub:          SubI64($a, ShrI64($b, $shift)) -> SUB_I64($a, $b) [targets=arm64|aarch64, benefit=35]

# NEON / Advanced SIMD FMA (FMLA Vd.4S, Vn.4S, Vm.S[lane])
rule arm64_neon_fma_f32:          AddF32(MulF32($a, $b), $c) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=arm64|aarch64, benefit=80]

# Conditional select (CSEL Xd, Xn, Xm, cond)
rule arm64_fold_csel:             Select(CmpEqI32($a, $b), $t, $f) -> SELECT($a, $t, $f) [targets=arm64|aarch64, benefit=25]

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# AArch64-specific form override
# AArch64 supports native hardware FMA via FMADD / FMLA instructions.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form fma_f32:                     opcode=FMA_F32,               machine_form=FloatTernaryFma32

# ============================================================================
# DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer) version 2.2.0
# COMPONENT: core_base.dgtl_isel
# PURPOSE:   Universal form baseline â€” included by ALL target ISel files.
#            Contains every form that maps identically on every target.
#            DO NOT add target-specific overrides or rules here.
# MAINTENANCE: When adding a new canonical opcode to phase1_core_ops.dgtl_def,
#            add the base form here first; only add a target-file override when
#            the mapping genuinely differs from this baseline.
# ============================================================================

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. CONTROL FLOW â€” identical on all 8 targets
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form nop:                         opcode=NOP,                   machine_form=NoOp
form label:                       opcode=LABEL,                 machine_form=NoOp
form region_begin:                opcode=REGION_BEGIN,          machine_form=NoOp
form region_end:                  opcode=REGION_END,            machine_form=NoOp
form fusion_group:                opcode=FUSION_GROUP,          machine_form=NoOp
form assume:                      opcode=ASSUME,                machine_form=NoOp
form undef:                       opcode=UNDEF,                 machine_form=NoOp

form goto:                        opcode=GOTO,                  machine_form=Goto
form branch:                      opcode=BRANCH,                machine_form=Goto
form branch_cond:                 opcode=BRANCH_COND,           machine_form=BranchCond
form branch_likely:               opcode=BRANCH_LIKELY,         machine_form=BranchCond
form branch_unlikely:             opcode=BRANCH_UNLIKELY,       machine_form=BranchCond
form branch_cmp:                  opcode=BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED,  machine_form=BranchCompare

form ret:                         opcode=RET,                   machine_form=Return
form return_alias:                opcode=RETURN,                machine_form=Return

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. REGISTERS, MEMORY & ADDRESSING â€” identical on all 8 targets
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form load:                        opcode=LOAD,                  machine_form=LoadStack
form volatile_load:               opcode=VOLATILE_LOAD,         machine_form=LoadStack
form store:                       opcode=STORE,                 machine_form=StoreStack
form volatile_store:              opcode=VOLATILE_STORE,        machine_form=StoreStack
form reg_move:                    opcode=REG_MOVE,              machine_form=Move
form mov_alias:                   opcode=MOV,                   machine_form=Move

form load_imm:                    opcode=LOAD_IMM,              machine_form=LoadImmediate
form const_alias:                 opcode=CONST,                 machine_form=LoadImmediate
form immediate:                   opcode=IMMEDIATE,             machine_form=LoadImmediate
form string_const:                opcode=STRING_CONST,          machine_form=LoadImmediate

form gep:                         opcode=GEP,                   machine_form=Move
form lea:                         opcode=LEA,                   machine_form=Move
form ptr_diff:                    opcode=PTR_DIFF,              machine_form=IntegerBinary64, kind=Sub
form ptr_to_int:                  opcode=PTR_TO_INT,            machine_form=Move
form int_to_ptr:                  opcode=INT_TO_PTR,            machine_form=Move
form ptr_cast:                    opcode=PTR_CAST,              machine_form=Move
form select:                      opcode=SELECT,                machine_form=Move
form bool_select:                 opcode=BOOL_SELECT,           machine_form=Move

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. 32-BIT INTEGER ARITHMETIC & BITWISE â€” identical on all 8 targets
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form add_i32:                     opcode=ADD_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Add
form op_add:                      opcode=OP_ADD,                machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Add
form add_i8:                      opcode=ADD_I8,                machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Add
form sub_i32:                     opcode=SUB_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Sub
form mul_i32:                     opcode=MUL_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Mul
form div_i32:                     opcode=DIV_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Div
form mod_i32:                     opcode=MOD_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Mod

form and_i32:                     opcode=AND_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=And
form or_i32:                      opcode=OR_I32,                machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Or
form xor_i32:                     opcode=XOR_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Xor
form shl_i32:                     opcode=SHL_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Shl
form shr_i32:                     opcode=SHR_I32,               machine_form=IntegerBinary32, kind=Shr

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. 64-BIT INTEGER ARITHMETIC & BITWISE â€” identical on all 8 targets
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# 5. RELATIONAL COMPARISONS â€” identical on all 8 targets

form cmp_ge_i32:                  opcode=CMP_GE_I32,            machine_form=Compare32,       kind=Any

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. FLOATING-POINT SCALARS â€” identical on all 8 targets
#    EXCEPTION: fma_f32 is NOT declared here.
#    Reason: WASM uses Call (software emulation, no native fused-multiply-add).
#            All other 7 targets use FloatTernaryFma32 (hardware FMA).
#    Each target file must declare fma_f32 explicitly and correctly.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form add_f32:                     opcode=ADD_F32,               machine_form=FloatBinary32,   kind=Add
form add_f8:                      opcode=ADD_F8,                machine_form=FloatBinary32,   kind=Add

form sub_f32:                     opcode=SUB_F32,               machine_form=FloatBinary32,   kind=Sub
form mul_f32:                     opcode=MUL_F32,               machine_form=FloatBinary32,   kind=Mul
form div_f32:                     opcode=DIV_F32,               machine_form=FloatBinary32,   kind=Div

form add_f64:                     opcode=ADD_F64,               machine_form=FloatBinary64,   kind=Add
form sub_f64:                     opcode=SUB_F64,               machine_form=FloatBinary64,   kind=Sub
form mul_f64:                     opcode=MUL_F64,               machine_form=FloatBinary64,   kind=Mul
form div_f64:                     opcode=DIV_F64,               machine_form=FloatBinary64,   kind=Div

form fast_inv_sqrt:               opcode=FAST_INV_SQRT,         machine_form=FloatBinary32,   kind=Div, semantic=FloatRsqrt

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. VECTOR PIPELINE â€” identical on all 8 targets
#    Target lowering handles the actual encoding; MachineForm is arch-neutral.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form vec_add_f32_512:             opcode=VEC_ADD_F32_512,       machine_form=VectorBinary512, kind=Add
form vec_mul_f32_512:             opcode=VEC_MUL_F32_512,       machine_form=VectorBinary512, kind=Mul
form store_v512:                  opcode=STORE_V512,            machine_form=VectorStore512
form gather_f32_512:              opcode=GATHER_F32_512,        machine_form=VectorGather512
form gather_16:                   opcode=GATHER_16,             machine_form=VectorGather512
form neon_vadd_q:                 opcode=NEON_VADD_Q,           machine_form=VectorBinary512, kind=Add

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. ATOMICS, MEMORY FENCES & HARDWARE PRIMITIVES â€” identical on all 8 targets
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form atomic_load:                 opcode=ATOMIC_LOAD,           machine_form=Move
form atomic_store:                opcode=ATOMIC_STORE,          machine_form=StoreStack

# DGTL canonical MachineForm contract.
# This is the sole source of truth for the target-independent ISel shape ABI.
# Values are stable and must never be renumbered once published.
version: 1

form Unhandled: value=0
form NoOp: value=1, semantic=Other, allowed_semantics=Other|LoopPreheader|LoopExit|LoopStep
form Move: value=2, semantic=Move, allowed_semantics=Move
form LoadImmediate: value=3, semantic=LoadImmediate, allowed_semantics=LoadImmediate
form LoadStack: value=4, semantic=LoadStack, allowed_semantics=LoadStack
form StoreStack: value=5, semantic=StoreStack, allowed_semantics=StoreStack
form IntegerBinary32: value=6, kinds=Add|Sub|Mul|Div|Mod|And|Or|Xor|Shl|Shr, kind_semantics=Add:IntegerAdd|Sub:IntegerSub|Mul:IntegerMul|Div:IntegerDiv|Mod:IntegerMod|And:IntegerAnd|Or:IntegerOr|Xor:IntegerXor|Shl:IntegerShl|Shr:IntegerShr, allowed_semantics=IntegerAdd|IntegerSub|IntegerMul|IntegerDiv|IntegerMod|IntegerAnd|IntegerOr|IntegerXor|IntegerShl|IntegerShr|IntegerAbs|IntegerNeg|IntegerMin|IntegerMax|IntegerClamp|IntegerPopcount|IntegerClz|IntegerCtz
form IntegerBinary64: value=7, kinds=Add|Sub|Mul|Div|Mod|And|Or|Xor|Shl|Shr, kind_semantics=Add:IntegerAdd|Sub:IntegerSub|Mul:IntegerMul|Div:IntegerDiv|Mod:IntegerMod|And:IntegerAnd|Or:IntegerOr|Xor:IntegerXor|Shl:IntegerShl|Shr:IntegerShr, allowed_semantics=IntegerAdd|IntegerSub|IntegerMul|IntegerDiv|IntegerMod|IntegerAnd|IntegerOr|IntegerXor|IntegerShl|IntegerShr|IntegerAbs|IntegerNeg|IntegerMin|IntegerMax|IntegerClamp|IntegerPopcount|IntegerClz|IntegerCtz
form FloatBinary32: value=8, kinds=Add|Sub|Mul|Div, kind_semantics=Add:FloatAdd|Sub:FloatSub|Mul:FloatMul|Div:FloatDiv, allowed_semantics=FloatAdd|FloatSub|FloatMul|FloatDiv|FloatFma|FloatSqrt|FloatAbs|FloatNeg|FloatMin|FloatMax|FloatClamp|FloatPow|FloatRsqrt|FloatCbrt|FloatHypot|FloatExp|FloatExp2|FloatExp10|FloatExpm1|FloatLog|FloatLog2|FloatLog10|FloatLog1p|FloatSin|FloatCos|FloatTan|FloatAsin|FloatAcos|FloatAtan|FloatAtan2|FloatSinh|FloatCosh|FloatTanh
form FloatBinary64: value=9, kinds=Add|Sub|Mul|Div, kind_semantics=Add:FloatAdd|Sub:FloatSub|Mul:FloatMul|Div:FloatDiv, allowed_semantics=FloatAdd|FloatSub|FloatMul|FloatDiv|FloatFma|FloatSqrt|FloatAbs|FloatNeg|FloatMin|FloatMax|FloatClamp|FloatPow|FloatRsqrt|FloatCbrt|FloatHypot|FloatExp|FloatExp2|FloatExp10|FloatExpm1|FloatLog|FloatLog2|FloatLog10|FloatLog1p|FloatSin|FloatCos|FloatTan|FloatAsin|FloatAcos|FloatAtan|FloatAtan2|FloatSinh|FloatCosh|FloatTanh
form FloatTernaryFma32: value=10, semantic=FloatFma, allowed_semantics=FloatFma|FloatClamp, requires=fma
form Compare32: value=11, kinds=Any, kind_semantics=Any:Compare, allowed_semantics=Compare
form VectorBinary512: value=12, kinds=Add|Mul, kind_semantics=Add:VectorAdd|Mul:VectorMul, allowed_semantics=VectorAdd|VectorMul, requires=vector

# ============================================================================
# DGTL version 2.2.0  |  ptx_core.dgtl_isel  (NVIDIA PTX / CUDA)
# Architecture:  NVIDIA GPU Virtual Architecture (sm_70 through sm_90a)
# Model:         SIMT (Single Instruction Multiple Threads)

include "core_base.dgtl_isel"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# PTX PEEPHOLE REWRITE & FMA RULES
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# FMA.RN.F32 / FMA.RN.F64 native PTX instructions (all SM >= 2.0)
rule ptx_fma_f32:                 AddF32(MulF32($a, $b), $c) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=cuda|ptx|nvptx, benefit=100]
rule ptx_fma_f32_rev:             AddF32($c, MulF32($a, $b)) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=cuda|ptx|nvptx, benefit=100]
rule ptx_fma_f64:                 AddF64(MulF64($a, $b), $c) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=cuda|ptx|nvptx, benefit=100]

# SELP instruction (predicated selection)
rule ptx_fold_select_eq:          Select(CmpEqI32($a, $b), $t, $f) -> SELECT($a, $t, $f) [targets=cuda|ptx|nvptx, benefit=30]

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CUDA/PTX form override
# PTX supports native FMA via FMA.RN.F32 on all Volta+ devices.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form fma_f32:                     opcode=FMA_F32,               machine_form=FloatTernaryFma32

rule rv64_fuse_branch_ge:         BranchCond(CmpGeI32($a, $b), $t) -> BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED($a, $b, $t) [targets=riscv64|rv64, benefit=25]

# ============================================================================
# DGTL version 2.2.0  |  wasm_core.dgtl_isel  (WebAssembly)
# Architecture:  Wasm32 / Wasm64, SIMD128, Bulk Memory, Threads
# ============================================================================
#
# NOTE ON fma_f32:
#   WebAssembly does NOT have a native fused-multiply-add instruction in the
#   base spec or SIMD128 extension (as of Wasm 2.0). FMA must be emitted as
#   a software call. Therefore fma_f32 -> Call here, not FloatTernaryFma32.
#   This is the ONLY form that differs between WASM and all other targets.

include "common_runtime_forms.dgtl_isel"
include "core_base.dgtl_isel"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# No target-specific peephole rules for WASM.
# WASM is a stack machine; branch fusion and GEP folding are handled by
# the WASM structured-control-flow lowering pass, not by ISel rules.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# WASM-specific form override â€” the only divergence from core_base.
# WebAssembly has no native FMA instruction; lower to a runtime call.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form fma_f32:                     opcode=FMA_F32,               machine_form=Call

# ============================================================================
# DGTL version 2.2.0  |  x64_core.dgtl_isel  (AMD64 / x86-64)
# Architecture:  AMD64, Intel 64 â€” AVX-512, AVX2, FMA3, BMI2, Atomics
# ABI:           Microsoft x64 / System V AMD64
# ============================================================================
#
# This file contains ONLY what is genuinely x64-specific:
#   - Target peephole / fusion rules (tagged targets=x86_64_*)
#   - fma_f32 form override (FloatTernaryFma32 â€” native hardware FMA)
#   - x64-only operand-strength-reduction rules (pow2â†’shl, gep folding)
#
# All universal formâ†’MachineForm mappings are in:
#   core_base.dgtl_isel      (included via common_runtime_forms below)
#   common_runtime_forms.dgtl_isel

include "common_runtime_forms.dgtl_isel"
include "core_base.dgtl_isel"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# x64 PEEPHOLE REWRITE & FUSION RULES
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Fused-multiply-add (VFMADD* instruction family)
rule x64_fma_f32_standard:        AddF32(MulF32($a, $b), $c) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=100, require_fma=true]
rule x64_fma_f32_commutative:     AddF32($c, MulF32($a, $b)) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=100, require_fma=true]
rule x64_fma_f64_standard:        AddF64(MulF64($a, $b), $c) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=100, require_fma=true]
rule x64_fma_f64_commutative:     AddF64($c, MulF64($a, $b)) -> FMA_F32($a, $b, $c) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=100, require_fma=true]

# AVX-512 vector FMA
rule x64_vec_fma_512:             VecAddF32512(VecMulF32512($a, $b), $c) -> VEC_ADD_F32_512($a, $b, $c) [targets=x86_64_avx512, benefit=30]
rule x64_vec_fma_512_rev:         VecAddF32512($c, VecMulF32512($a, $b)) -> VEC_ADD_F32_512($a, $b, $c) [targets=x86_64_avx512, benefit=30]

# CMP+branch fusion (TEST/CMP+Jcc)
rule x64_fuse_branch_eq:          BranchCond(CmpEqI32($a, $b), $t) -> BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED($a, $b, $t) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=20]
rule x64_fuse_branch_ne:          BranchCond(CmpNeI32($a, $b), $t) -> BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED($a, $b, $t) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=20]
rule x64_fuse_branch_lt:          BranchCond(CmpLtI32($a, $b), $t) -> BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED($a, $b, $t) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=20]
rule x64_fuse_branch_le:          BranchCond(CmpLeI32($a, $b), $t) -> BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED($a, $b, $t) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=20]
rule x64_fuse_branch_gt:          BranchCond(CmpGtI32($a, $b), $t) -> BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED($a, $b, $t) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=20]
rule x64_fuse_branch_ge:          BranchCond(CmpGeI32($a, $b), $t) -> BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED($a, $b, $t) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=20]

# GEP folding (LEA with scaled index)
rule x64_fold_gep_shl:            AddI64($base, ShlI64($index, $scale)) -> GEP($base, $index) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=40]
rule x64_fold_gep_shl_rev:        AddI64(ShlI64($index, $scale), $base) -> GEP($base, $index) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=40]
rule x64_fold_gep_add:            AddI64($base, $index) -> GEP($base, $index) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=15]

# Strength reduction (IMULâ†’SHL, IDIVâ†’SHR, IMODâ†’AND for powers-of-2)
rule x64_mul_pow2_to_shl:         MulI64($val, Const($imm)) -> ShlI64($val, $imm) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=25]
rule x64_div_pow2_to_shr:         DivI64($val, Const($imm)) -> ShrI64($val, $imm) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=50]
rule x64_mod_pow2_to_and:         ModI64($val, Const($imm)) -> AndI64($val, $imm) [targets=x86_64_avx2|x86_64_avx512, benefit=50]

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# x64-specific form override
# x64 supports native 3-operand FMA (VFMADD132/213/231PS/PD).
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

form fma_f32:                     opcode=FMA_F32,               machine_form=FloatTernaryFma32

/*

 *
 * Project:     DGTL (Deep General-purpose Transformation Layer)
 * Owner:       Divyanshu Tata

module;

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <limits>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <vector>

export module dgtl.mir.machine_ir:types;

import dgtl.mir.isel_gen;

export namespace dgtl::machine {

/* ============================================================================
 * Core identifiers
 * ========================================================================== */

using MachineId   = std::uint32_t;
using BlockId     = std::uint32_t;
using InstrId     = std::uint32_t;
using RegId       = std::uint32_t;
using StackSlotId = std::uint32_t;
using SymbolId    = std::uint32_t;

inline constexpr MachineId INVALID_MACHINE_ID = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr BlockId INVALID_BLOCK_ID = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr InstrId INVALID_INSTR_ID = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr RegId INVALID_REG = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr StackSlotId INVALID_STACK_SLOT = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
inline constexpr SymbolId INVALID_SYMBOL = 0xFFFFFFFFu;

/* ============================================================================
 * Machine IR state
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class MachinePhase : std::uint8_t {
    Selected,
    Legalized,
    PreRegAlloc,
    PostRegAlloc,
    FrameLowered,
    ABILowered,
    EmissionReady
};

enum class RegisterKind : std::uint8_t {
    Virtual,
    Physical
};

enum class RegisterClassKind : std::uint8_t {
    Invalid,
    General,
    Integer,
    FloatingPoint,
    Vector,
    Predicate,
    Mask,
    Address,
    Special,
    Custom
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Register model
 * ========================================================================== */

struct MachineRegister {
    RegId id = INVALID_REG;
    RegisterKind kind = RegisterKind::Virtual;
    RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::Invalid;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool valid() const noexcept {
        return id != INVALID_REG;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_virtual() const noexcept {
        return kind == RegisterKind::Virtual;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_physical() const noexcept {
        return kind == RegisterKind::Physical;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr MachineRegister virtual_reg(
        RegId reg_id,
        RegisterClassKind cls = RegisterClassKind::General) noexcept {
        MachineRegister r;
        r.id = reg_id;
        r.kind = RegisterKind::Virtual;
        r.reg_class = cls;
        return r;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr MachineRegister physical_reg(
        RegId reg_id,
        RegisterClassKind cls = RegisterClassKind::General) noexcept {
        MachineRegister r;
        r.id = reg_id;
        r.kind = RegisterKind::Physical;
        r.reg_class = cls;
        return r;
    }

    friend constexpr bool operator==(const MachineRegister&,
                                     const MachineRegister&) noexcept = default;
};

struct RegisterMask {
    std::vector<std::uint64_t> words;

    void resize(std::size_t bit_count) {
        words.resize((bit_count + 63u) / 64u);
    }

    void clear() noexcept {
        std::fill(words.begin(), words.end(), 0u);
    }

    void set(std::size_t bit) {
        const std::size_t word = bit / 64u;
        if (word >= words.size()) {
            words.resize(word + 1u);
        }
        words[word] |= (std::uint64_t{1} << (bit % 64u));
    }

    void reset(std::size_t bit) noexcept {
        const std::size_t word = bit / 64u;
        if (word < words.size()) {
            words[word] &= ~(std::uint64_t{1} << (bit % 64u));
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool test(std::size_t bit) const noexcept {
        const std::size_t word = bit / 64u;
        return word < words.size() &&
               (words[word] & (std::uint64_t{1} << (bit % 64u))) != 0;
    }
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Machine operands
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class MachineOperandKind : std::uint8_t {
    Invalid,
    Register,
    Immediate,
    FloatingImmediate,
    Memory,
    BasicBlock,
    Symbol,
    StackSlot,
    ConstantPoolIndex,
    JumpTableIndex,
    RegisterMask,
    Metadata
};

enum class OperandRole : std::uint8_t {
    Use,
    Def,
    UseDef,
    Kill,
    Dead,
    ImplicitUse,
    ImplicitDef,
    Debug
};

enum class RelocationKind : std::uint8_t {
    None,
    Absolute,
    PCRelative,
    GOT,
    PLT,
    TLS,
    TargetSpecific
};

struct MachineMemoryOperand {
    MachineRegister base = {};
    MachineRegister index = {};
    std::int32_t scale = 1;
    std::int64_t displacement = 0;
    std::uint32_t address_space = 0;
    std::uint32_t size_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 1;

    bool load = false;
    bool store = false;
    bool volatile_access = false;
    bool atomic = false;
    bool invariant = false;
};

enum class SubRegIndex : std::uint8_t {
    None = 0,
    SubLow32 = 1,
    SubHigh32 = 2,
    SubLow16 = 3,
    SubHigh16 = 4,
    SubLow8 = 5,
    SubHigh8 = 6,
    SubLow64 = 7,
    SubHigh64 = 8,
    SubLow128 = 9,
    SubHigh128 = 10,
    SubLow256 = 11,
    SubHigh256 = 12
};

using LaneBitmask = std::uint64_t;
inline constexpr LaneBitmask kAllLanes = ~0ULL;

struct alignas(16) MachineOperand {
    MachineOperandKind kind = MachineOperandKind::Invalid;
    OperandRole role = OperandRole::Use;

    MachineRegister reg = {};
    std::int64_t immediate = 0;
    double floating_immediate = 0.0;

    MachineMemoryOperand memory = {};
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    SymbolId symbol = INVALID_SYMBOL;
    StackSlotId stack_slot = INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    std::uint32_t constant_pool_index = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
    std::uint32_t jump_table_index = 0xFFFFFFFFu;

    RelocationKind relocation = RelocationKind::None;

    std::uint32_t subregister = 0;
    SubRegIndex sub_reg_index = SubRegIndex::None;
    LaneBitmask lane_mask = kAllLanes;
    std::uint32_t flags = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_subregister() const noexcept {
        return sub_reg_index != SubRegIndex::None || subregister != 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand use(MachineRegister r) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::Register;
        op.role = OperandRole::Use;
        op.reg = r;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand def(MachineRegister r) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::Register;
        op.role = OperandRole::Def;
        op.reg = r;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand imm(std::int64_t value) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::Immediate;
        op.immediate = value;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand block_ref(BlockId id) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock;
        op.block = id;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand stack_ref(StackSlotId id) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::StackSlot;
        op.stack_slot = id;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand cpi(std::uint32_t idx) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::ConstantPoolIndex;
        op.constant_pool_index = idx;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand jti(std::uint32_t idx) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex;
        op.jump_table_index = idx;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineOperand memory_ref(
        const MachineMemoryOperand& mem_op,
        OperandRole r = OperandRole::Use) noexcept {
        MachineOperand op;
        op.kind = MachineOperandKind::Memory;
        op.role = r;
        op.memory = mem_op;
        return op;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_register() const noexcept {
        return kind == MachineOperandKind::Register;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_memory() const noexcept {
        return kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_immediate() const noexcept {
        return kind == MachineOperandKind::Immediate;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_def() const noexcept {
        return role == OperandRole::Def || role == OperandRole::UseDef ||
               role == OperandRole::ImplicitDef;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_use() const noexcept {
        return role == OperandRole::Use || role == OperandRole::UseDef ||
               role == OperandRole::ImplicitUse;
    }
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Industry-Grade Machine Opcode Architecture
 * ========================================================================== */

using MachineOpcode = std::uint16_t;

inline constexpr MachineOpcode INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE = 0xFFFFu;
inline constexpr MachineOpcode GENERIC_OPCODE_START   = 0x0001u;
inline constexpr MachineOpcode GENERIC_OPCODE_END     = 0x7FFFu;
inline constexpr MachineOpcode TARGET_OPCODE_START    = 0x8000u;

enum GenericMachineOpcode : MachineOpcode {
    GM_INVALID = 0,
    GM_NOOP = 1,
    GM_COPY,
    GM_IMMEDIATE,
    GM_LOAD_STACK,
    GM_STORE_STACK,

    // Integer Binary 32-bit
    GM_IADD32, GM_ISUB32, GM_IMUL32, GM_IDIV32, GM_IREM32,
    GM_IAND32, GM_IOR32,  GM_IXOR32, GM_ISHL32, GM_ISHR32,

    // Integer Binary 64-bit
    GM_IADD64, GM_ISUB64, GM_IMUL64, GM_IDIV64, GM_IREM64,
    GM_IAND64, GM_IOR64,  GM_IXOR64, GM_ISHL64, GM_ISHR64,

    // Floating-Point
    GM_FADD32, GM_FSUB32, GM_FMUL32, GM_FDIV32,
    GM_FADD64, GM_FSUB64, GM_FMUL64, GM_FDIV64,
    GM_FFMA32, GM_FFMA64, GM_FAST_INV_SQRT,

    // Comparison & Control Flow
    GM_ICMP32, GM_ICMP64, GM_FCMP32, GM_FCMP64,
    GM_GOTO, GM_BR_COND, GM_BR_COMPARE, GM_BR_TABLE,
    GM_CALL, GM_CALL_VARIADIC, GM_RETURN, GM_SYSCALL,

    // Memory
    GM_LOAD, GM_STORE, GM_VEC_LOAD, GM_VEC_STORE,
    GM_VEC_GATHER, GM_VEC_SCATTER,

    // Concurrency / Atomics
    GM_FENCE, GM_ACQUIRE_FENCE, GM_RELEASE_FENCE, GM_ATOMIC_FENCE,
    GM_ATOMIC_LOAD, GM_ATOMIC_STORE, GM_ATOMIC_SNAPSHOT,
    GM_LOCK_ACQUIRE, GM_LOCK_RELEASE, GM_SPIN_ACQUIRE,
    GM_BARRIER_SYNC, GM_SYNC_BARRIER,

    // Async / Coroutines / Fibers
    GM_ASYNC_AWAIT, GM_ASYNC_LAUNCH, GM_ASYNC_SPAWN,
    GM_FIBER_YIELD, GM_FIBER_YIELD_LOOP, GM_SUSPEND_CTX, GM_RESUME_CTX,
    GM_THREAD_SPAWN,

    // Memory & Address Math
    GM_LEA, GM_GEP, GM_PTR_DIFF, GM_PTR_TO_INT, GM_INT_TO_PTR,
    GM_PREFETCH, GM_PREFETCH_T0, GM_CLFLUSH, GM_READ_TSC,

    // String Primitives
    GM_STRING_LENGTH, GM_STRING_COMPARE, GM_STRING_CONCAT,
    GM_STRING_SUBSTRING, GM_STRING_COPY, GM_STRING_EQUALS,
    GM_STRING_FIND, GM_STRING_HASH,

    // Dynamic Allocations / Data Structures
    GM_DSA_ALLOCATE, GM_DSA_DEALLOCATE,
    GM_RING_PUSH_FREE, GM_DEOPT_TRIGGER,
    GM_TREE_BUILD, GM_TREE_TRAVERSE,
    GM_TRIE_BUILD, GM_TRIE_INSERT, GM_TRIE_LOOKUP,
    GM_GRAPH_BUILD, GM_GRAPH_TRAVERSE,
    GM_TUPLE_PACK, GM_TUPLE_UNPACK,

    // Tensor & Accelerator Kernels
    GM_TENSOR_OP, GM_TENSOR_BROADCAST, GM_TENSOR_CONCAT, GM_TENSOR_CONV,
    GM_TENSOR_GEMM, GM_TENSOR_GATHER, GM_TENSOR_POOL, GM_TENSOR_REDUCE,
    GM_TENSOR_RESHAPE, GM_TENSOR_SCATTER_D, GM_TENSOR_SLICE,
    GM_TENSOR_SOFTMAX, GM_TENSOR_TRANSPOSE,
    GM_MATMUL_BF16, GM_MATMUL_FP8, GM_SDPA_F16,
    GM_SOFTMAX_FAST, GM_LAYER_NORM, GM_ROPE_EMBED,
    GM_MAT4_MUL, GM_QUAT_SLERP, GM_VEC3_CROSS, GM_VEC4_DOT, GM_RAY_AABB_ISECT,
    GM_GPU_LAUNCH, GM_WASM_INVOKE,

    GM_MAX_GENERIC = GENERIC_OPCODE_END
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_generic_opcode(MachineOpcode op) noexcept {
    return op >= GENERIC_OPCODE_START && op <= GENERIC_OPCODE_END;
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_target_opcode(MachineOpcode op) noexcept {
    return op >= TARGET_OPCODE_START && op != INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
}

/* ============================================================================
 * Canonical MachineForm and MachineSemanticKind
 * ========================================================================== */

using MachineForm = dgtl::mir::MachineForm;
using MachineSemanticKind = dgtl::mir::MachineSemanticKind;

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool
semantic_kind_matches_form(MachineForm form, MachineSemanticKind kind) noexcept {
    return dgtl::mir::generated::semantic_kind_matches_form(form, kind);
}

[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool
requires_semantic_kind(MachineForm form) noexcept {
    return form != MachineForm::Unhandled;
}

enum class MachineInstrFlag : std::uint32_t {
    None            = 0,
    Terminator      = 1u << 0,
    Branch          = 1u << 1,
    Call            = 1u << 2,
    Return          = 1u << 3,
    Load            = 1u << 4,
    Store           = 1u << 5,
    Atomic          = 1u << 6,
    Barrier         = 1u << 7,
    SideEffect      = 1u << 8,
    Commutable      = 1u << 9,
    MayLoad         = 1u << 10,
    MayStore        = 1u << 11,
    HasUnmodeledSideEffect = 1u << 12,
    Pseudo          = 1u << 13,
    Debug           = 1u << 14,
    FrameSetup      = 1u << 15,
    FrameDestroy    = 1u << 16,
    CFI             = 1u << 17
};

constexpr MachineInstrFlag operator|(MachineInstrFlag a, MachineInstrFlag b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineInstrFlag>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(a) | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(b));
}

constexpr MachineInstrFlag operator&(MachineInstrFlag a, MachineInstrFlag b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineInstrFlag>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(a) & static_cast<std::uint32_t>(b));
}

enum class MachineTrait : std::uint64_t {
    None                = 0ULL,
    Commutative         = 1ULL << 0,
    HasNoSideEffects    = 1ULL << 1,
    IsTerminator        = 1ULL << 2,
    IsMemoryRead        = 1ULL << 3,
    IsMemoryWrite       = 1ULL << 4,
    IsMemoryOp          = 1ULL << 5,
    IsVectorizable      = 1ULL << 6,
    IsFusible           = 1ULL << 7,
    IsSpeculative       = 1ULL << 8,
    IsGuard             = 1ULL << 9,
    IsGPU               = 1ULL << 10,
    IsTensor            = 1ULL << 11,
    IsGraph             = 1ULL << 12,
    IsAsyncSpawnable    = 1ULL << 13,
    IsLatencyCritical   = 1ULL << 14,
    IsHardwareIntrinsic = 1ULL << 15,
    Associative         = 1ULL << 16,
    ConstantFoldable    = 1ULL << 17,
    MemoryBarrier       = 1ULL << 18,
    SingleDefUse        = 1ULL << 19,
    RequiresSpill       = 1ULL << 20,
    ClobbersFlags       = 1ULL << 21,
    TwoAddress          = 1ULL << 22,
    BranchDelaySlot     = 1ULL << 23
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineTrait operator|(MachineTrait a, MachineTrait b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineTrait>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) | static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineTrait operator&(MachineTrait a, MachineTrait b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineTrait>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) & static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineTrait operator~(MachineTrait a) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineTrait>(~static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a));
}

enum class MachineDomain : std::uint64_t {
    None        = 0ULL,
    Arithmetic  = 1ULL << 0,
    Memory      = 1ULL << 1,
    ControlFlow = 1ULL << 2,
    Terminator  = 1ULL << 3,
    Comparison  = 1ULL << 4,
    BitwiseOp   = 1ULL << 5,
    SIMD        = 1ULL << 6,
    GPU         = 1ULL << 7,
    Tensor      = 1ULL << 8,
    Concurrency = 1ULL << 9,
    DSA         = 1ULL << 10,
    Ownership   = 1ULL << 11,
    PointerMath = 1ULL << 12,
    NeuralNet   = 1ULL << 13,
    SpatialMath = 1ULL << 14
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineDomain operator|(MachineDomain a, MachineDomain b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineDomain>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) | static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineDomain operator&(MachineDomain a, MachineDomain b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineDomain>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) & static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}

enum class MachineAttribute : std::uint64_t {
    None             = 0ULL,
    NoWrap           = 1ULL << 0,
    Exact            = 1ULL << 1,
    FastMath         = 1ULL << 2,
    Aligned          = 1ULL << 3,
    NoAlias          = 1ULL << 4,
    AddrSpaceGeneric = 1ULL << 5,
    Predicated       = 1ULL << 6,
    Relaxed          = 1ULL << 7,
    Acquire          = 1ULL << 8,
    Release          = 1ULL << 9,
    SeqCst           = 1ULL << 10,
    HotPath          = 1ULL << 11,
    ColdPath         = 1ULL << 12,
    LockFree         = 1ULL << 13,
    Variadic         = 1ULL << 14,
    MultiResult      = 1ULL << 15,
    Volatile         = 1ULL << 16
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineAttribute operator|(MachineAttribute a, MachineAttribute b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineAttribute>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) | static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}
[[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineAttribute operator&(MachineAttribute a, MachineAttribute b) noexcept {
    return static_cast<MachineAttribute>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a) & static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
}

enum class MachineCFIKind : std::uint8_t {
    None = 0,
    DefCFA = 1,
    DefCFAOffset = 2,
    DefCFARegister = 3,
    Offset = 4,
    Restore = 5,
    RememberState = 6,
    RestoreState = 7
};

struct MachineCFIData {
    MachineCFIKind kind = MachineCFIKind::None;
    MachineRegister reg = {};
    std::int32_t offset = 0;
};

struct alignas(64) MachineInstruction {
    InstrId id = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    MachineForm form = MachineForm::Unhandled;
    MachineSemanticKind semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::None;
    MachineOpcode opcode = INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
    MachineInstrFlag flags = MachineInstrFlag::None;
    MachineCFIData cfi_data{};

    BlockId parent_block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    InstrId prev_instr   = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    InstrId next_instr   = INVALID_INSTR_ID;

    std::vector<MachineOperand> operands;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_cfi_instruction() const noexcept {
        return cfi_data.kind != MachineCFIKind::None || (flags & MachineInstrFlag::CFI) != MachineInstrFlag::None;
    }

    std::uint32_t semantic_provenance = 0;
    std::uint32_t debug_id = 0;

    MachineTrait traits = MachineTrait::None;
    MachineDomain domains = MachineDomain::None;
    MachineAttribute attributes = MachineAttribute::None;
    std::uint64_t intent_hash = 0ULL;
    std::uint32_t epoch = 0;
    std::uint16_t latency_cycles = 1;
    std::uint16_t throughput_reciprocal = 1;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_trait(MachineTrait t) const noexcept {
        return (traits & t) != MachineTrait::None;
    }

    void add_trait(MachineTrait t) noexcept {
        traits = traits | t;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool in_domain(MachineDomain d) const noexcept {
        return (domains & d) != MachineDomain::None;
    }

    void add_domain(MachineDomain d) noexcept {
        domains = domains | d;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_attribute(MachineAttribute a) const noexcept {
        return (attributes & a) != MachineAttribute::None;
    }

    void add_attribute(MachineAttribute a) noexcept {
        attributes = attributes | a;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_target_opcode() const noexcept {
        return is_target_opcode(opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_opcode() const noexcept {
        return opcode != INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool assign_target_opcode_once(MachineOpcode target_opcode) noexcept {
        if (!is_target_opcode(target_opcode) || has_target_opcode()) {
            return false;
        }
        opcode = target_opcode;
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_semantic_kind() const noexcept {
        return semantic_kind != MachineSemanticKind::None;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_flag(MachineInstrFlag flag) const noexcept {
        return (flags & flag) != MachineInstrFlag::None;
    }

    void add_flag(MachineInstrFlag flag) noexcept {
        flags = flags | flag;
    }

    void add_operand(MachineOperand operand) {
        operands.push_back(std::move(operand));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_terminator() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_branch() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Branch);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_call() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Call);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_return() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Return);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_load() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Load);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_store() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Store);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_barrier() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Barrier);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_atomic() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Atomic);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool may_load() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::MayLoad) || has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Load);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool may_store() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::MayStore) || has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Store);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_side_effects() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::SideEffect) || has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::HasUnmodeledSideEffect);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_commutable() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Commutable);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_pseudo() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Pseudo);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_frame_setup() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::FrameSetup);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_frame_destroy() const noexcept {
        return has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::FrameDestroy);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_phi() const noexcept {
        if (operands.size() >= 3 && (operands.size() % 2 == 1)) {
            if (operands[0].is_def()) {
                for (std::size_t i = 1; i < operands.size(); i += 2) {
                    if (operands[i].kind != MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine

module;

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <limits>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

export module dgtl.mir.machine_ir:function;

import dgtl.lut_fsm;
import :types;
import :target;

export namespace dgtl::machine {

class MachineDominatorTree;
class MachineDominanceFrontier;
class MachineLoopInfo;

struct MachineBasicBlock {
    BlockId id = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;

    InstrId head_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    InstrId tail_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    std::size_t instr_count = 0;

    std::vector<InstrId> instructions;
    std::vector<BlockId> predecessors;
    std::vector<BlockId> successors;

    std::vector<MachineRegister> live_ins;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> live_outs;

    bool entry = false;
    bool exit = false;
    bool cold = false;
    bool address_taken = false;

    [[nodiscard]] bool valid() const noexcept {
        return id != INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    }

    void add_instruction(InstrId instr) {
        instructions.push_back(instr);
    }

    void add_successor(BlockId b) {
        if (std::find(successors.begin(), successors.end(), b) == successors.end()) {
            successors.push_back(b);
        }
    }

    void remove_successor(BlockId b) {
        auto it = std::find(successors.begin(), successors.end(), b);
        if (it != successors.end()) {
            successors.erase(it);
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_successor(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        return std::find(successors.begin(), successors.end(), b) != successors.end();
    }

    void add_predecessor(BlockId b) {
        if (std::find(predecessors.begin(), predecessors.end(), b) == predecessors.end()) {
            predecessors.push_back(b);
        }
    }

    void remove_predecessor(BlockId b) {
        auto it = std::find(predecessors.begin(), predecessors.end(), b);
        if (it != predecessors.end()) {
            predecessors.erase(it);
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_predecessor(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        return std::find(predecessors.begin(), predecessors.end(), b) != predecessors.end();
    }
};

enum class StackSlotKind : std::uint8_t {
    Spill,
    Local,
    IncomingArgument,
    OutgoingArgument,
    CalleeSaved,
    VariableSized,
    Alignment,
    Temporary
};

struct MachineStackSlot {
    StackSlotId id = INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    StackSlotKind kind = StackSlotKind::Temporary;

    std::uint32_t size_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 1;

    std::int64_t offset = 0;
    bool fixed_offset = false;
    bool is_dead = false;
};

struct MachineFrameInfo {
    std::vector<MachineStackSlot> slots;

    std::uint32_t stack_alignment = 1;
    std::uint32_t outgoing_argument_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t local_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t spill_bytes = 0;
    std::uint32_t callee_saved_bytes = 0;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> callee_saved_registers;
    std::uint32_t alignment_padding = 0;
    std::uint32_t total_frame_bytes = 0;

    bool has_calls = false;
    bool has_variable_sized_objects = false;
    bool has_stack_protector = false;

    [[nodiscard]] StackSlotId create_slot(StackSlotKind kind,
                                          std::uint32_t size,
                                          std::uint32_t alignment) {
        const StackSlotId id = static_cast<StackSlotId>(slots.size());
        slots.push_back({
            id, kind, size, std::max<std::uint32_t>(alignment, 1u),
            0, false, false
        });
        return id;
    }
};

struct MachineProfile {
    std::uint32_t spill_count = 0;
    std::uint32_t reload_count = 0;
    std::uint32_t peak_register_pressure = 0;
    std::uint32_t estimated_cycle_cost = 0;
    std::uint32_t vector_lane_utilization = 0;
    std::uint32_t branch_mispredict_weight = 0;
    bool contains_atomic_fences = false;
    bool contains_accelerator_calls = false;
};

struct MachineConstantPoolEntry {
    std::uint32_t id = 0;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> data;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 4;
    std::uint32_t offset = 0;
    bool is_machine_specific = false;
};

class MachineConstantPool {
    std::vector<MachineConstantPoolEntry> entries_;
    std::uint32_t total_size_ = 0;
    std::uint32_t max_alignment_ = 1;

public:
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t add_entry(std::span<const std::uint8_t> bytes, std::uint32_t alignment = 4) {
        for (const auto& entry : entries_) {
            if (entry.alignment == alignment && entry.data.size() == bytes.size()) {
                if (std::equal(entry.data.begin(), entry.data.end(), bytes.begin())) {
                    return entry.id;
                }
            }
        }
        const std::uint32_t id = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(entries_.size());
        MachineConstantPoolEntry entry{};
        entry.id = id;
        entry.data.assign(bytes.begin(), bytes.end());
        entry.alignment = std::max(alignment, 1u);
        max_alignment_ = std::max(max_alignment_, entry.alignment);
        
        const std::uint32_t rem = total_size_ % entry.alignment;
        if (rem != 0) {
            total_size_ += (entry.alignment - rem);
        }
        entry.offset = total_size_;
        total_size_ += static_cast<std::uint32_t>(entry.data.size());

        entries_.push_back(std::move(entry));
        return id;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t add_float(float val) {
        const auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(&val);
        return add_entry(std::span<const std::uint8_t>(bytes, sizeof(float)), alignof(float));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t add_double(double val) {
        const auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(&val);
        return add_entry(std::span<const std::uint8_t>(bytes, sizeof(double)), alignof(double));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t add_uint64(std::uint64_t val) {
        const auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(&val);
        return add_entry(std::span<const std::uint8_t>(bytes, sizeof(std::uint64_t)), alignof(std::uint64_t));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineConstantPoolEntry>& entries() const noexcept {
        return entries_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineConstantPoolEntry* entry(std::uint32_t id) const noexcept {
        if (id >= entries_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &entries_[id];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t total_size() const noexcept { return total_size_; }
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t max_alignment() const noexcept { return max_alignment_; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept { return entries_.empty(); }
    void clear() noexcept { entries_.clear(); total_size_ = 0; max_alignment_ = 1; }
};

struct MachineJumpTableEntry {
    std::uint32_t id = 0;
    std::vector<BlockId> targets;
    std::uint32_t alignment = 8;
    std::uint32_t offset = 0;
};

class MachineJumpTableInfo {
    std::vector<MachineJumpTableEntry> entries_;

public:
    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t create_jump_table(std::vector<BlockId> targets, std::uint32_t alignment = 8) {
        const std::uint32_t id = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(entries_.size());
        MachineJumpTableEntry entry{};
        entry.id = id;
        entry.targets = std::move(targets);
        entry.alignment = std::max(alignment, 1u);
        entries_.push_back(std::move(entry));
        return id;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineJumpTableEntry>& entries() const noexcept {
        return entries_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineJumpTableEntry* entry(std::uint32_t id) const noexcept {
        if (id >= entries_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &entries_[id];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept { return entries_.empty(); }
    void clear() noexcept { entries_.clear(); }
};

enum class BlockBranchTopology : std::uint8_t {
    EmptyOrFallthrough = 0,
    UnconditionalBranch = 1,
    ConditionalBranchWithFallthrough = 2,
    ConditionalAndUnconditionalBranch = 3,
    Return = 4,
    SyscallOrTrap = 5,
    IndirectBranch = 6,
    Malformed = 7
};

struct MachineFunctionProperties {
    bool is_ssa = true;
    bool tracks_liveness = false;
    bool registers_allocated = false;
    bool frame_lowered = false;
    bool abi_lowered = false;
    bool contains_calls = false;
    bool contains_dynamic_alloca = false;
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<8, 8> build_phase_transition_lut() noexcept {
    dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<8, 8> lut{};
    for (std::size_t from = 0; from < 7; ++from) {
        for (std::size_t to = from + 1; to < 7; ++to) {
            lut.set(from, to, true);
        }
    }
    return lut;
}

inline constexpr auto kPhaseTransitionMatrix = build_phase_transition_lut();

class MachineFunction {
public:
    explicit MachineFunction(std::string_view name = {})
        : name_(name) {}

    [[nodiscard]] std::string_view name() const noexcept {
        return name_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachinePhase phase() const noexcept {
        return phase_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr bool is_legal_phase_transition(MachinePhase from, MachinePhase to) noexcept {
        const auto f = static_cast<std::size_t>(from);
        const auto t = static_cast<std::size_t>(to);
        if (f >= 8 || t >= 8) return false;
        return kPhaseTransitionMatrix.get(f, t);
    }

    void set_phase(MachinePhase phase) noexcept {
        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase) >= static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase_)) {
            phase_ = phase;
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool advance_phase(MachinePhase expected_current,
                                     MachinePhase next) noexcept {
        if (phase_ != expected_current) {
            return false;
        }
        if (!is_legal_phase_transition(expected_current, next)) {
            return false;
        }
        phase_ = next;
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineFunctionProperties& properties() noexcept {
        return properties_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineFunctionProperties&
    properties() const noexcept {
        return properties_;
    }

    void invalidate_analysis_caches() noexcept;

    MachineBasicBlock& create_block(bool entry = false) {
        invalidate_analysis_caches();
        if (blocks_.size() >= static_cast<std::size_t>(INVALID_BLOCK_ID)) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MachineFunction block id exhausted");
        }
        const BlockId id = static_cast<BlockId>(blocks_.size());
        blocks_.push_back({});
        blocks_.back().id = id;
        blocks_.back().entry = entry;
        if (entry) {
            if (entry_block_ != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) {
                throw std::logic_error("MachineFunction cannot have multiple entry blocks");
            }
            entry_block_ = id;
        }
        return blocks_.back();
    }

    MachineBasicBlock* block(BlockId id) noexcept {
        if (id >= blocks_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &blocks_[id];
    }

    const MachineBasicBlock* block(BlockId id) const noexcept {
        if (id >= blocks_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &blocks_[id];
    }

    bool connect_blocks(BlockId from, BlockId to) {
        MachineBasicBlock* source = block(from);
        MachineBasicBlock* target = block(to);
        if (source == nullptr || target == nullptr) {
            return false;
        }
        invalidate_analysis_caches();
        source->add_successor(to);
        target->add_predecessor(from);
        return true;
    }

    bool disconnect_blocks(BlockId from, BlockId to) {
        MachineBasicBlock* source = block(from);
        MachineBasicBlock* target = block(to);
        if (source == nullptr || target == nullptr) {
            return false;
        }
        invalidate_analysis_caches();
        source->remove_successor(to);
        target->remove_predecessor(from);
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<MachineBasicBlock> blocks() noexcept {
        return blocks_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const MachineBasicBlock> blocks() const noexcept {
        return blocks_;
    }

    MachineInstruction& create_instruction() {
        return create_instruction(INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE);
    }

    MachineInstruction& create_instruction(MachineOpcode opcode) {
        if (instructions_.size() >= static_cast<std::size_t>(INVALID_INSTR_ID)) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MachineFunction instruction id exhausted");
        }
        const InstrId id = static_cast<InstrId>(instructions_.size());
        instructions_.push_back({});
        instructions_.back().id = id;
        instructions_.back().opcode = opcode;
        return instructions_.back();
    }

    void insert_instruction_before(BlockId block_id, InstrId cursor_id, InstrId new_instr_id);
    void remove_instruction(InstrId instr_id);
    void splice_instructions(BlockId dest_block_id, InstrId cursor_id,
                             BlockId src_block_id, InstrId first_instr_id, InstrId last_instr_id);

    MachineInstruction* instruction(InstrId id) noexcept {
        if (id >= instructions_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &instructions_[id];
    }

    const MachineInstruction* instruction(InstrId id) const noexcept {
        if (id >= instructions_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &instructions_[id];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<MachineInstruction> instructions() noexcept {
        return instructions_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const MachineInstruction> instructions() const noexcept {
        return instructions_;
    }

    MachineRegister create_virtual_register(
        RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        if (next_virtual_reg_ == INVALID_REG) {
            throw std::overflow_error("MachineFunction virtual register id exhausted");
        }
        const RegId id = next_virtual_reg_++;
        return MachineRegister{id, RegisterKind::Virtual, reg_class};
    }

    void add_stack_slot(MachineStackSlot slot) {
        if (slot.id == INVALID_STACK_SLOT) {
            slot.id = static_cast<StackSlotId>(frame_.slots.size());
        }
        frame_.slots.push_back(slot);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineFrameInfo& frame_info() noexcept {
        return frame_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineFrameInfo& frame_info() const noexcept {
        return frame_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId entry_block() const noexcept {
        return entry_block_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t virtual_register_count() const noexcept {
        return next_virtual_reg_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool assign_target_opcode(
        InstrId instruction_id, MachineOpcode target_opcode) noexcept {
        MachineInstruction* inst = instruction(instruction_id);
        return inst != nullptr && inst->assign_target_opcode_once(target_opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_target_opcodes() const noexcept {
        for (const MachineInstruction& inst : instructions_) {
            if (!inst.has_target_opcode()) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase phase) const noexcept {
        return static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase_) >= static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_virtual_registers() const noexcept {
        for (const MachineInstruction& inst : instructions_) {
            for (const MachineOperand& op : inst.operands) {
                if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                    return true;
                }
                if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) {
                    if (op.memory.base.is_virtual() || op.memory.index.is_virtual()) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool replace_virtual_register(
        MachineRegister virtual_reg,
        MachineRegister physical_reg) noexcept {
        if (!virtual_reg.is_virtual() || !physical_reg.is_physical()) {
            return false;
        }

        bool replaced = false;
        for (MachineInstruction& inst : instructions_) {
            for (MachineOperand& op : inst.operands) {
                if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register && op.reg == virtual_reg) {
                    op.reg = physical_reg;
                    replaced = true;
                }

                if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) {
                    if (op.memory.base == virtual_reg) {
                        op.memory.base = physical_reg;
                        replaced = true;
                    }
                    if (op.memory.index == virtual_reg) {
                        op.memory.index = physical_reg;
                        replaced = true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return replaced;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool mark_target_lowered() noexcept {
        if (!has_target_opcodes()) {
            return false;
        }
        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(phase_) < static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc)) {
            phase_ = MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc;
        }
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool mark_frame_lowered() noexcept {
        if (!properties_.registers_allocated || has_virtual_registers()) {
            return false;
        }
        properties_.frame_lowered = true;
        phase_ = MachinePhase::FrameLowered;
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool mark_abi_lowered() noexcept {
        if (!properties_.frame_lowered) {
            return false;
        }
        properties_.abi_lowered = true;
        phase_ = MachinePhase::ABILowered;
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool mark_emission_ready() noexcept {
        if (!properties_.abi_lowered || has_virtual_registers() || !has_target_opcodes()) {
            return false;
        }
        phase_ = MachinePhase::EmissionReady;
        return true;
    }

    void mark_registers_allocated() noexcept {
        properties_.registers_allocated = has_target_opcodes() && !has_virtual_registers();
        if (properties_.registers_allocated && is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc)) {
            phase_ = MachinePhase::PostRegAlloc;
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineConstantPool& constant_pool() noexcept {
        return constant_pool_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineConstantPool& constant_pool() const noexcept {
        return constant_pool_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineJumpTableInfo& jump_table_info() noexcept {
        return jump_table_info_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineJumpTableInfo& jump_table_info() const noexcept {
        return jump_table_info_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineProfile& profile() noexcept {
        return profile_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineProfile& profile() const noexcept {
        return profile_;
    }

    void recompute_dominators() const;
    [[nodiscard]] const MachineDominatorTree& dominators() const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool strictly_dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool instruction_dominates(InstrId a, InstrId b) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool dominates(const MachineInstruction& a, const MachineInstruction& b) const;
    [[nodiscard]] BlockId nearest_common_dominator(BlockId a, BlockId b) const;

    void recompute_dominance_frontier() const;
    [[nodiscard]] const MachineDominanceFrontier& dominance_frontier() const;

    void recompute_loop_info() const;
    [[nodiscard]] MachineLoopInfo& loop_info() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] const MachineLoopInfo& loop_info() const noexcept;

    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> traversal_preorder() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> traversal_postorder() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> traversal_reverse_postorder() const;
    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> traversal_breadth_first() const;

    [[nodiscard]] bool verify_cfg_integrity(std::string* out_error = nullptr) const;
    [[nodiscard]] bool verify_ssa_dominance(std::string* out_error = nullptr) const;
    [[nodiscard]] BlockBranchTopology block_branch_topology(BlockId block_id) const noexcept;

private:
    std::string name_;
    MachinePhase phase_ = MachinePhase::Selected;
    MachineFunctionProperties properties_{};

    std::vector<MachineBasicBlock> blocks_;
    std::vector<MachineInstruction> instructions_;

    MachineFrameInfo frame_{};
    MachineConstantPool constant_pool_{};
    MachineJumpTableInfo jump_table_info_{};
    MachineProfile profile_{};

    mutable bool dom_tree_valid_ = false;
    mutable void* dom_tree_cache_ptr_ = nullptr;
    mutable bool dom_frontier_valid_ = false;
    mutable void* dom_frontier_cache_ptr_ = nullptr;
    mutable bool loop_info_valid_ = false;
    mutable void* loop_info_cache_ptr_ = nullptr;

    RegId next_virtual_reg_ = 0;
    BlockId entry_block_ = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineSelection Bridge & Builder
 * ========================================================================== */

enum class MachineSelectionOperandKind : std::uint8_t {
    Invalid,
    Value,
    Immediate,
    FloatingImmediate,
    Memory,
    BasicBlock,
    Symbol,
    StackSlot,
    Register
};

struct MachineSelectionOperand {
    MachineSelectionOperandKind kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::Invalid;
    std::uint32_t value = INVALID_MACHINE_ID;
    std::int64_t immediate = 0;
    double floating_immediate = 0.0;
    MachineMemoryOperand memory = {};
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    SymbolId symbol = INVALID_SYMBOL;
    StackSlotId stack_slot = INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    MachineRegister reg = {};
    RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::Invalid;
    OperandRole role = OperandRole::Use;

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineSelectionOperand value_ref(
        std::uint32_t id,
        OperandRole role = OperandRole::Use) noexcept {
        MachineSelectionOperand operand;
        operand.kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::Value;
        operand.value = id;
        operand.role = role;
        return operand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineSelectionOperand imm(
        std::int64_t v) noexcept {
        MachineSelectionOperand operand;
        operand.kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::Immediate;
        operand.immediate = v;
        return operand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineSelectionOperand block_ref(
        BlockId id) noexcept {
        MachineSelectionOperand operand;
        operand.kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::BasicBlock;
        operand.block = id;
        return operand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static MachineSelectionOperand memory_ref(
        const MachineMemoryOperand& mem,
        OperandRole role = OperandRole::Use) noexcept {
        MachineSelectionOperand operand;
        operand.kind = MachineSelectionOperandKind::Memory;
        operand.memory = mem;
        operand.role = role;
        return operand;
    }
};

struct MachineSelection {
    MachineForm form = MachineForm::Unhandled;
    MachineSemanticKind semantic_kind = MachineSemanticKind::None;
    MachineOpcode opcode = GM_INVALID;
    std::uint32_t semantic_provenance = 0;
    std::uint32_t debug_id = 0;
    std::uint32_t result_value = INVALID_MACHINE_ID;
    RegisterClassKind result_class = RegisterClassKind::General;

    std::vector<MachineSelectionOperand> operands;

    MachineInstrFlag flags = MachineInstrFlag::None;

    MachineTrait traits = MachineTrait::None;
    MachineDomain domains = MachineDomain::None;
    MachineAttribute attributes = MachineAttribute::None;
    std::uint64_t intent_hash = 0ULL;
    std::uint32_t epoch = 0;
    std::uint16_t latency_cycles = 1;
    std::uint16_t throughput_reciprocal = 1;
};

class MachineFunctionBuilder {
public:
    explicit MachineFunctionBuilder(MachineFunction& function)
        : function_(function) {}

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister value_register(
        std::uint32_t value_id,
        RegisterClassKind reg_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        if (value_id == INVALID_MACHINE_ID) {
            return {};
        }
        if (value_id >= value_registers_.size()) {
            value_registers_.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(value_id) + 1u);
        }
        MachineRegister& cached = value_registers_[value_id];
        if (!cached.valid()) {
            cached = function_.create_virtual_register(reg_class);
        }
        return cached;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineInstruction& append(
        BlockId block,
        const MachineSelection& selection,
        RegisterClassKind result_class = RegisterClassKind::General) {
        MachineBasicBlock* bb = function_.block(block);
        if (bb == nullptr) {
            throw std::out_of_range("invalid MachineFunction block");
        }

        if (selection.form == MachineForm::Unhandled) {
            throw std::invalid_argument("cannot append unhandled MachineForm");
        }

        MachineInstruction& instruction =
            function_.create_instruction();
        instruction.form = selection.form;
        instruction.semantic_kind = selection.semantic_kind;
        instruction.opcode = selection.opcode != GM_INVALID ? selection.opcode : GM_NOOP;
        instruction.semantic_provenance = selection.semantic_provenance;
        instruction.debug_id = selection.debug_id;
        instruction.flags = selection.flags;
        instruction.traits = selection.traits;
        instruction.domains = selection.domains;
        instruction.attributes = selection.attributes;
        instruction.intent_hash = selection.intent_hash;
        instruction.epoch = selection.epoch;
        instruction.latency_cycles = selection.latency_cycles;
        instruction.throughput_reciprocal = selection.throughput_reciprocal;
        instruction.parent_block = block;

        const RegisterClassKind effective_result_class =
            selection.result_class == RegisterClassKind::Invalid
                ? result_class
                : selection.result_class;

        if (selection.result_value != INVALID_MACHINE_ID) {
            instruction.add_operand(
                MachineOperand::def(
                    value_register(selection.result_value, effective_result_class)));
        }

        if (!selection.operands.empty()) {
            for (const MachineSelectionOperand& source : selection.operands) {
                switch (source.kind) {
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Value:
                    if (source.value != INVALID_MACHINE_ID) {
                        const RegisterClassKind operand_class =
                            source.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Invalid
                                ? RegisterClassKind::General
                                : source.reg_class;
                        MachineRegister value_reg =
                            value_register(source.value, operand_class);
                        if (source.role == OperandRole::Def) {
                            instruction.add_operand(MachineOperand::def(value_reg));
                        } else {
                            MachineOperand operand = MachineOperand::use(value_reg);
                            operand.role = source.role;
                            instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                        }
                    }
                    break;
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Immediate:
                    instruction.add_operand(MachineOperand::imm(source.immediate));
                    break;
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::FloatingImmediate: {
                    MachineOperand operand;
                    operand.kind = MachineOperandKind::FloatingImmediate;
                    operand.role = source.role;
                    operand.floating_immediate = source.floating_immediate;
                    instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                    break;
                }
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Memory: {
                    MachineOperand operand;
                    operand.kind = MachineOperandKind::Memory;
                    operand.role = source.role;
                    operand.memory = source.memory;
                    if (operand.memory.base.valid() && operand.memory.base.is_virtual()) {
                        operand.memory.base = value_register(operand.memory.base.id, RegisterClassKind::General);
                    }
                    if (operand.memory.index.valid() && operand.memory.index.is_virtual()) {
                        operand.memory.index = value_register(operand.memory.index.id, RegisterClassKind::General);
                    }
                    instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                    break;
                }
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::BasicBlock:
                    instruction.add_operand(MachineOperand::block_ref(source.block));
                    break;
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Symbol: {
                    MachineOperand operand;
                    operand.kind = MachineOperandKind::Symbol;
                    operand.role = source.role;
                    operand.symbol = source.symbol;
                    instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                    break;
                }
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::StackSlot:
                    instruction.add_operand(MachineOperand::stack_ref(source.stack_slot));
                    break;
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Register: {
                    MachineOperand operand;
                    operand.kind = MachineOperandKind::Register;
                    operand.role = source.role;
                    operand.reg = source.reg;
                    instruction.add_operand(std::move(operand));
                    break;
                }
                case MachineSelectionOperandKind::Invalid:
                    throw std::invalid_argument("invalid MachineSelection operand");
                }
            }
        }

        function_.insert_instruction_before(block, INVALID_INSTR_ID, instruction.id);

        if (instruction.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Call)) {
            function_.properties().contains_calls = true;
            function_.frame_info().has_calls = true;
        }
        return instruction;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineBasicBlock& create_block(bool entry = false) {
        return function_.create_block(entry);
    }

private:
    MachineFunction& function_;
    std::vector<MachineRegister> value_registers_;
};

enum class EmissionKind : std::uint8_t {
    Assembly,
    Object,
    MachineCode,
    Intermediate
};

struct EmissionResult {
    bool success = false;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> bytes;
    std::string_view diagnostic = {};
};

class MachineEmitter {
public:
    virtual ~MachineEmitter() = default;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual EmissionResult
    emit(const MachineFunction& function) = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] virtual EmissionKind kind() const noexcept = 0;
};

struct MIR {
    MachineFunction function;

    explicit MIR(std::string_view name = {})
        : function(name) {}

    [[nodiscard]] MachineFunction& machine_function() noexcept {
        return function;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineFunction&
    machine_function() const noexcept {
        return function;
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine
