inline const MachineOperand kInvalidOperand{};

/* ============================================================================
 * Concepts
 * ========================================================================== */

template <typename T>
concept MachineInstructionLike = requires(const T& t) {
    { t.operands } -> std::same_as<const std::vector<MachineOperand>&>;
    { t.form } -> std::same_as<const MachineForm&>;
    { t.opcode } -> std::same_as<const MachineOpcode&>;
    { t.traits } -> std::same_as<const MachineTrait&>;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineBinaryOpView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineBinaryOpView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineBinaryOpView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& dst() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& lhs() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& rhs() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 2) ? inst_.operands[2] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineForm form() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr MachineOpcode opcode() const noexcept {
        return inst_.opcode;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_generic() const noexcept {
        return is_generic_opcode(inst_.opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_target() const noexcept {
        return is_target_opcode(inst_.opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_commutative() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_trait(MachineTrait::Commutative);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_no_wrap() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::NoWrap);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_exact() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Exact);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_fast_math() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::FastMath);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint16_t latency_cycles() const noexcept {
        return inst_.latency_cycles;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineLoadView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineLoadView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineLoadView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& dst() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& address_operand() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister base() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) return op.memory.base;
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) return op.reg;
        return {};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister index() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.index : MachineRegister{};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::int32_t scale() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.scale : 1;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::int64_t displacement() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) return op.memory.displacement;
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Immediate) return op.immediate;
        return 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t alignment() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.alignment : 1u;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] StackSlotId stack_slot() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot) ? op.stack_slot : INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_stack_load() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::LoadStack || address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_volatile() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Volatile) ||
               (address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory && address_operand().memory.volatile_access);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_atomic() const noexcept {
        return inst_.is_atomic() ||
               (address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory && address_operand().memory.atomic);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineStoreView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineStoreView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineStoreView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& val() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0) ? inst_.operands[0] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& address_operand() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? inst_.operands[1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister base() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) return op.memory.base;
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) return op.reg;
        return {};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister index() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.index : MachineRegister{};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::int32_t scale() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.scale : 1;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::int64_t displacement() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) return op.memory.displacement;
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Immediate) return op.immediate;
        return 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t alignment() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory) ? op.memory.alignment : 1u;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] StackSlotId stack_slot() const noexcept {
        const auto& op = address_operand();
        return (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot) ? op.stack_slot : INVALID_STACK_SLOT;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_stack_store() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::StoreStack || address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_volatile() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Volatile) ||
               (address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory && address_operand().memory.volatile_access);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_atomic() const noexcept {
        return inst_.is_atomic() ||
               (address_operand().kind == MachineOperandKind::Memory && address_operand().memory.atomic);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineBranchView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineBranchView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineBranchView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId target_block() const noexcept {
        for (const auto& op : inst_.operands) {
            if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock) {
                return op.block;
            }
        }
        return INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& condition() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 0 && inst_.operands[0].kind != MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock)
            ? inst_.operands[0]
            : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_conditional() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::BranchCond || inst_.form == MachineForm::BranchCompare;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_fused_compare() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::BranchCompare;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_likely() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::HotPath);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_unlikely() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::ColdPath);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

/* ============================================================================
 * MachineCallView
 * ========================================================================== */

class MachineCallView {
public:
    explicit constexpr MachineCallView(const MachineInstruction& inst) noexcept
        : inst_(inst) {}

    [[nodiscard]] SymbolId callee_symbol() const noexcept {
        if (!inst_.operands.empty() && inst_.operands[0].kind == MachineOperandKind::Symbol) {
            return inst_.operands[0].symbol;
        }
        return INVALID_SYMBOL;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] MachineRegister callee_reg() const noexcept {
        if (!inst_.operands.empty() && inst_.operands[0].kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) {
            return inst_.operands[0].reg;
        }
        return {};
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t arg_count() const noexcept {
        return (inst_.operands.size() > 1) ? (inst_.operands.size() - 1) : 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineOperand& arg(std::size_t index) const noexcept {
        return (index + 1 < inst_.operands.size()) ? inst_.operands[index + 1] : kInvalidOperand;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_indirect() const noexcept {
        return !inst_.operands.empty() && inst_.operands[0].kind == MachineOperandKind::Register;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_syscall() const noexcept {
        return inst_.form == MachineForm::Syscall;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_variadic() const noexcept {
        return inst_.has_attribute(MachineAttribute::Variadic);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr const MachineInstruction& raw_instruction() const noexcept {
        return inst_;
    }

private:
    const MachineInstruction& inst_;
};

#include <cstddef>

#include <vector>

enum class MachineVerifyError : std::uint8_t {
    None,
    InvalidBlock,
    InvalidInstruction,
    InvalidOperand,
    InvalidRegister,
    MissingTerminator,
    InvalidCFGEdge,
    DuplicateBlock,
    DuplicateInstruction,
    InvalidStackSlot,
    InvalidMemoryOperand,
    InvalidOperandRole,
    MissingSemanticKind,
    MissingTargetOpcode,
    InvalidTargetOpcodeState,
    InvalidPhaseTransition,
    MissingEntryBlock,
    PhaseViolation,
    CFGIntegrityViolation,
    SSADominanceViolation
};

struct MachineVerifyResult {
    MachineVerifyError error = MachineVerifyError::None;
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    InstrId instruction = INVALID_INSTR_ID;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool ok() const noexcept {
        return error == MachineVerifyError::None;
    }
};

[[nodiscard]] constexpr dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<16, 16> build_operand_role_legality_lut() noexcept {
    dgtl::utils::BitMatrixLUT<16, 16> lut{};
    for (std::size_t r = 0; r <= 7; ++r) {
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(MachineOperandKind::Register), r, true);
    }
    auto set_read_only = [&](MachineOperandKind k) {
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(k), static_cast<std::size_t>(OperandRole::Use), true);
        lut.set(static_cast<std::size_t>(k), static_cast<std::size_t>(OperandRole::Debug), true);
    };
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Immediate);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::FloatingImmediate);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Symbol);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::StackSlot);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::ConstantPoolIndex);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::RegisterMask);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Metadata);
    set_read_only(MachineOperandKind::Memory);
    return lut;
}

inline constexpr auto kOperandRoleLegalityLUT = build_operand_role_legality_lut();

inline bool MachineFunction::verify_cfg_integrity(std::string* out_error) const {
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && root >= blocks().size()) {
        if (out_error) *out_error = "Entry block ID is out of range.";
        return false;
    }
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < blocks().size(); ++i) {
        const auto* bb_ptr = block(static_cast<BlockId>(i));
        if (!bb_ptr) continue;
        const auto& bb = *bb_ptr;
        if (bb.id != static_cast<BlockId>(i)) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Block id does not match vector index at " + std::to_string(i);
            return false;
        }
        for (BlockId succ_id : bb.successors) {
            if (succ_id >= blocks().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Successor " + std::to_string(succ_id) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* succ_bb = block(succ_id);
            if (!succ_bb || std::find(succ_bb->predecessors.begin(), succ_bb->predecessors.end(), bb.id) == succ_bb->predecessors.end()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "CFG asymmetry: block " + std::to_string(bb.id) + " has successor " + std::to_string(succ_id) + " but successor lacks predecessor back-edge.";
                return false;
            }
        }
        for (BlockId pred_id : bb.predecessors) {
            if (pred_id >= blocks().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Predecessor " + std::to_string(pred_id) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* pred_bb = block(pred_id);
            if (!pred_bb || std::find(pred_bb->successors.begin(), pred_bb->successors.end(), bb.id) == pred_bb->successors.end()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "CFG asymmetry: block " + std::to_string(bb.id) + " has predecessor " + std::to_string(pred_id) + " but predecessor lacks successor forward-edge.";
                return false;
            }
        }
        InstrId curr = bb.head_instr;
        InstrId prev = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        std::size_t counted = 0;
        while (curr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            if (curr >= instructions().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction ID " + std::to_string(curr) + " out of range in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            const auto* inst = instruction(curr);
            if (!inst || inst->parent_block != bb.id) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction " + std::to_string(curr) + " parent_block mismatch in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
            if (inst->prev_instr != prev) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Instruction chain broken: prev_instr mismatch at instr " + std::to_string(curr);
                return false;
            }
            prev = curr;
            curr = inst->next_instr;
            counted++;
            if (counted > instructions().size()) {
                if (out_error) *out_error = "Cycle detected in instruction intrusive list of block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                return false;
            }
        }
        if (prev != bb.tail_instr) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Tail instruction pointer mismatch in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
            return false;
        }
        if (counted != bb.instructions.size()) {
            if (out_error) *out_error = "Intrusive chain count " + std::to_string(counted) + " does not match instructions vector size " + std::to_string(bb.instructions.size()) + " in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

inline bool MachineFunction::verify_ssa_dominance(std::string* out_error) const {
    if (!properties().is_ssa) {
        return true;
    }

    struct DefLocation {
        InstrId instr_id = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        BlockId block_id = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        std::size_t instr_index_in_block = 0;
    };

    std::unordered_map<RegId, DefLocation> vreg_defs;

    for (const auto& bb : blocks()) {
        for (std::size_t idx = 0; idx < bb.instructions.size(); ++idx) {
            const InstrId iid = bb.instructions[idx];
            const auto* inst = instruction(iid);
            if (!inst) continue;

            for (const auto& op : inst->operands) {
                if (op.is_register() && op.is_def() && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                    const RegId r = op.reg.id;
                    if (vreg_defs.find(r) != vreg_defs.end()) {
                        if (out_error) {
                            *out_error = "SSA violation: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                         " defined multiple times (first in instr " +
                                         std::to_string(vreg_defs[r].instr_id) + ", then in instr " +
                                         std::to_string(iid) + ").";
                        }
                        return false;
                    }
                    vreg_defs[r] = DefLocation{iid, bb.id, idx};
                }
            }
        }
    }

    const auto& dom = dominators();

    for (const auto& bb : blocks()) {
        for (std::size_t idx = 0; idx < bb.instructions.size(); ++idx) {
            const InstrId iid = bb.instructions[idx];
            const auto* inst = instruction(iid);
            if (!inst) continue;

            const bool is_phi = inst->is_phi();

            if (is_phi) {
                for (std::size_t op_idx = 1; op_idx + 1 < inst->operands.size(); op_idx += 2) {
                    const auto& block_op = inst->operands[op_idx];
                    const auto& val_op = inst->operands[op_idx + 1];
                    if (block_op.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock &&
                        val_op.is_register() && val_op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                        const RegId r = val_op.reg.id;
                        auto def_it = vreg_defs.find(r);
                        if (def_it == vreg_defs.end()) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA use without def: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " used in PHI instruction " + std::to_string(iid) + " has no definition.";
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                        const BlockId pred_block = block_op.block;
                        const DefLocation& def_loc = def_it->second;
                        if (!dom.dominates(def_loc.block_id, pred_block)) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA dominance violation: definition of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " in block " + std::to_string(def_loc.block_id) +
                                             " does not dominate PHI incoming predecessor block " +
                                             std::to_string(pred_block) + " in block " + std::to_string(bb.id);
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                for (const auto& op : inst->operands) {
                    if (op.is_register() && op.is_use() && op.reg.is_virtual()) {
                        const RegId r = op.reg.id;
                        auto def_it = vreg_defs.find(r);
                        if (def_it == vreg_defs.end()) {
                            if (out_error) {
                                *out_error = "SSA use without def: virtual register v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                             " used in instruction " + std::to_string(iid) + " has no definition.";
                            }
                            return false;
                        }
                        const DefLocation& def_loc = def_it->second;
                        if (def_loc.block_id == bb.id) {
                            if (def_loc.instr_index_in_block >= idx) {
                                if (out_error) {
                                    *out_error = "SSA intra-block dominance violation: use of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                                 " at instr " + std::to_string(iid) + " precedes its definition at instr " +
                                                 std::to_string(def_loc.instr_id);
                                }
                                return false;
                            }
                        } else {
                            if (!dom.dominates(def_loc.block_id, bb.id)) {
                                if (out_error) {
                                    *out_error = "SSA dominance violation: definition of v" + std::to_string(r) +
                                                 " in block " + std::to_string(def_loc.block_id) +
                                                 " does not dominate use block " + std::to_string(bb.id) +
                                                 " at instr " + std::to_string(iid);
                                }
                                return false;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

inline BlockBranchTopology MachineFunction::block_branch_topology(BlockId block_id) const noexcept {
    const auto* bb = block(block_id);
    if (!bb) return BlockBranchTopology::Malformed;
    if (bb->instructions.empty() || bb->tail_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::EmptyOrFallthrough;
    }
    const auto* tail = instruction(bb->tail_instr);
    if (!tail) return BlockBranchTopology::Malformed;

    if (!tail->has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator)) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::EmptyOrFallthrough;
    }

    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Return || tail->has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Return)) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::Return;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Syscall) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::SyscallOrTrap;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::Goto) {
        if (tail->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            const auto* prev = instruction(tail->prev_instr);
            if (prev && (prev->form == MachineForm::BranchCond || prev->form == MachineForm::BranchCompare)) {
                return BlockBranchTopology::ConditionalAndUnconditionalBranch;
            }
        }
        return BlockBranchTopology::UnconditionalBranch;
    }
    if (tail->form == MachineForm::BranchCond || tail->form == MachineForm::BranchCompare) {
        return BlockBranchTopology::ConditionalBranchWithFallthrough;
    }
    for (const auto& op : tail->operands) {
        if (op.kind == MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex) {
            return BlockBranchTopology::IndirectBranch;
        }
    }
    return BlockBranchTopology::UnconditionalBranch;
}

class MachineVerifier {
public:
    [[nodiscard]] static MachineVerifyResult
    verify(const MachineFunction& function) noexcept {
        if (!function.verify_cfg_integrity()) {
            return {MachineVerifyError::CFGIntegrityViolation, INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
        }
        if (function.properties().is_ssa && !function.verify_ssa_dominance()) {
            return {MachineVerifyError::SSADominanceViolation, INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
        }

        const auto blocks = function.blocks();
        const auto instructions = function.instructions();

        std::vector<std::uint32_t> instruction_membership(instructions.size(), 0u);

        if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc)) {
            if (blocks.empty() || function.entry_block() == INVALID_BLOCK_ID ||
                function.entry_block() >= blocks.size() ||
                !blocks[function.entry_block()].entry) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::MissingEntryBlock,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
        }

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < blocks.size(); ++i) {
            if (blocks[i].id != static_cast<BlockId>(i)) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::DuplicateBlock,
                    blocks[i].id,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }

            if (blocks[i].instr_count != blocks[i].instructions.size()) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (blocks[i].instructions.empty()) {
                if (blocks[i].head_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                    blocks[i].tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
            } else if (blocks[i].head_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                       blocks[i].tail_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            bool seen_terminator = false;
            for (std::size_t position = 0; position < blocks[i].instructions.size(); ++position) {
                const InstrId id = blocks[i].instructions[position];
                if (id >= instructions.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }
                if (++instruction_membership[id] != 1u) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }

                const MachineInstruction& block_instr = instructions[id];

                if (block_instr.parent_block != blocks[i].id) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (block_instr.form == MachineForm::Unhandled) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (requires_semantic_kind(block_instr.form) &&
                    !block_instr.has_semantic_kind()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::MissingSemanticKind, blocks[i].id, id};
                }
                if (block_instr.has_semantic_kind() &&
                    !semantic_kind_matches_form(block_instr.form, block_instr.semantic_kind)) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                const bool emission_phase =
                    function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::FrameLowered);
                if (emission_phase && !block_instr.has_target_opcode()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::MissingTargetOpcode, blocks[i].id, id};
                }

                if (seen_terminator) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        id
                    };
                }
                if (block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Terminator)) {
                    seen_terminator = true;
                    if (position + 1u != blocks[i].instructions.size()) {
                        return {
                            MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            blocks[i].id,
                            id
                        };
                    }
                }

                for (const MachineOperand& operand : block_instr.operands) {
                    switch (operand.kind) {
                    case MachineOperandKind::Register:
                        if (!operand.reg.valid() ||
                            operand.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Invalid) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock:
                        if (operand.block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || operand.block >= blocks.size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::StackSlot:
                        if (operand.stack_slot == INVALID_STACK_SLOT ||
                            operand.stack_slot >= function.frame_info().slots.size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::ConstantPoolIndex:
                        if (operand.constant_pool_index >= function.constant_pool().entries().size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::JumpTableIndex:
                        if (operand.jump_table_index >= function.jump_table_info().entries().size()) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::Memory:
                        if (operand.memory.scale != 1 && operand.memory.scale != 2 &&
                            operand.memory.scale != 4 && operand.memory.scale != 8) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        if (operand.memory.alignment == 0) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        if (!operand.memory.load && !operand.memory.store &&
                            (block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Load) ||
                             block_instr.has_flag(MachineInstrFlag::Store))) {
                            return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidMemoryOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                        }
                        break;
                    case MachineOperandKind::Invalid:
                        return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperand, blocks[i].id, id};
                    default:
                        break;
                    }

                    if (!kOperandRoleLegalityLUT.get(static_cast<std::size_t>(operand.kind),
                                                     static_cast<std::size_t>(operand.role))) {
                        return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidOperandRole, blocks[i].id, id};
                    }
                }
            }

            if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc) &&
                !blocks[i].instructions.empty() && !seen_terminator) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::MissingTerminator,
                        blocks[i].id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            for (BlockId successor : blocks[i].successors) {
                if (successor >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }

            for (BlockId predecessor : blocks[i].predecessors) {
                if (predecessor >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        blocks[i].id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (const MachineBasicBlock& block : blocks) {
            InstrId cursor = block.head_instr;
            InstrId previous = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            std::size_t chain_count = 0;
            while (cursor != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                if (cursor >= instructions.size() || chain_count > block.instructions.size()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                const MachineInstruction& chained = instructions[cursor];
                if (chained.parent_block != block.id ||
                    chained.prev_instr != previous) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                if (std::find(block.instructions.begin(),
                              block.instructions.end(), cursor) == block.instructions.end()) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                            block.id, cursor};
                }
                previous = cursor;
                cursor = chained.next_instr;
                ++chain_count;
            }
            if (chain_count != block.instructions.size() ||
                previous != block.tail_instr) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        block.id, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (block.tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID &&
                instructions[block.tail_instr].next_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                        block.id, block.tail_instr};
            }

            for (BlockId successor : block.successors) {
                const MachineBasicBlock& target = blocks[successor];
                if (std::find(target.predecessors.begin(),
                              target.predecessors.end(),
                              block.id) == target.predecessors.end()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        block.id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
            for (BlockId predecessor : block.predecessors) {
                const MachineBasicBlock& source = blocks[predecessor];
                if (std::find(source.successors.begin(),
                              source.successors.end(),
                              block.id) == source.successors.end()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidCFGEdge,
                        block.id,
                        INVALID_INSTR_ID
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < instruction_membership.size(); ++i) {
            if (instruction_membership[i] != 1u) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::DuplicateInstruction,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    static_cast<InstrId>(i)
                };
            }
        }

        for (const MachineInstruction& instr : instructions) {
            if (instr.id == INVALID_INSTR_ID ||
                instr.form == MachineForm::Unhandled) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidInstruction,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    instr.id
                };
            }

            const bool before_target_lowering =
                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) <
                static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc);
            if (before_target_lowering) {
                if (is_target_opcode(instr.opcode)) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::PhaseViolation,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            } else {
                if (!instr.has_target_opcode()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::MissingTargetOpcode,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            }

            for (const MachineOperand& operand : instr.operands) {
                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                    !operand.reg.valid()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }

                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock &&
                    operand.block >= blocks.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidBlock,
                        operand.block,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }

                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::StackSlot &&
                    operand.stack_slot >= function.frame_info().slots.size()) {
                    return {
                        MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                        instr.id
                    };
                }
            }
        }

        for (const MachineStackSlot& slot : function.frame_info().slots) {
            if (slot.id == INVALID_STACK_SLOT) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
            if (slot.alignment == 0u || slot.size_bytes == 0u) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
        }

        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) >=
            static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::PostRegAlloc)) {
            if (function.has_virtual_registers() ||
                !function.properties().registers_allocated) {
                return {
                    MachineVerifyError::InvalidRegister,
                    INVALID_BLOCK_ID,
                    INVALID_INSTR_ID
                };
            }
        }

        if (static_cast<std::uint8_t>(function.phase()) >=
            static_cast<std::uint8_t>(MachinePhase::FrameLowered)) {
            const auto& frame = function.frame_info();

            if (frame.stack_alignment == 0u) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }

            for (const auto& slot : frame.slots) {
                if (!slot.fixed_offset || slot.alignment == 0u ||
                    slot.size_bytes == 0u) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                            INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
                const auto alignment = static_cast<std::int64_t>(slot.alignment);
                if (slot.offset % alignment != 0) {
                    return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidStackSlot,
                            INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
                }
            }
        }

        if (function.is_phase_at_least(MachinePhase::EmissionReady)) {
            if (!function.has_target_opcodes() || function.has_virtual_registers()) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::InvalidTargetOpcodeState,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
            if (!function.properties().frame_lowered ||
                !function.properties().abi_lowered ||
                !function.properties().registers_allocated) {
                return {MachineVerifyError::PhaseViolation,
                        INVALID_BLOCK_ID, INVALID_INSTR_ID};
            }
        }

        return {};
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::machine

module;

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>

export module dgtl.mir.machine_ir:analysis;

import :types;
import :target;
import :function;

export namespace dgtl::machine {

enum class MachineLoopKind : std::uint8_t {
    For,
    While,
    DoWhile,
    Infinite
};

struct MachineInductionVariable {
    MachineRegister iv_reg{};
    std::int64_t init_value = 0;
    std::int64_t step_value = 1;
    std::int64_t limit_value = 0;
    MachineSemanticKind compare_kind = MachineSemanticKind::Compare;
    bool is_constant_trip_count = false;
    std::uint64_t trip_count = 0;
};

class MachineLoop {
public:
    BlockId header = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    BlockId preheader = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    BlockId latch = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    std::vector<BlockId> latches;
    std::vector<BlockId> blocks;
    std::vector<BlockId> exit_blocks;
    std::vector<BlockId> exiting_blocks;
    MachineLoopKind kind = MachineLoopKind::While;
    std::optional<MachineInductionVariable> induction_var;
    std::uint32_t depth = 1;

    [[nodiscard]] bool contains(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return std::find(blocks.begin(), blocks.end(), bb) != blocks.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool contains(const MachineLoop& subloop) const noexcept {
        for (BlockId b : subloop.blocks) {
            if (!contains(b)) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_header(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return header == bb;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_latch(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return latch == bb || std::find(latches.begin(), latches.end(), bb) != latches.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_exiting(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return std::find(exiting_blocks.begin(), exiting_blocks.end(), bb) != exiting_blocks.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_exit(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        return std::find(exit_blocks.begin(), exit_blocks.end(), bb) != exit_blocks.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_for_loop() const noexcept { return kind == MachineLoopKind::For; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool is_while_loop() const noexcept { return kind == MachineLoopKind::While; }
    [[nodiscard]] bool is_do_while_loop() const noexcept { return kind == MachineLoopKind::DoWhile; }

    [[nodiscard]] bool has_constant_trip_count() const noexcept {
        return induction_var && induction_var->is_constant_trip_count;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t trip_count() const noexcept {
        return induction_var ? induction_var->trip_count : 0;
    }
};

struct MachineDomTreeNode {
    BlockId block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    BlockId idom = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    std::vector<BlockId> children;
    std::uint32_t dfs_in = 0;
    std::uint32_t dfs_out = 0;
    std::uint32_t level = 0;
};

class MachineDominatorTree {
    BlockId root_ = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    std::vector<MachineDomTreeNode> nodes_;

public:
    void clear() noexcept {
        root_ = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        nodes_.clear();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept {
        return root_ == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || nodes_.empty();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId root() const noexcept {
        return root_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const noexcept {
        return nodes_.size();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineDomTreeNode>& nodes() const noexcept {
        return nodes_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineDomTreeNode* node(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return nullptr;
        return &nodes_[b];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId idom(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        return nodes_[b].idom;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_reachable(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return false;
        return nodes_[b].dfs_in > 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const BlockId> children(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return {};
        return nodes_[b].children;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t level(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= nodes_.size()) return 0;
        return nodes_[b].level;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (a == b) return true;
        if (a >= nodes_.size() || b >= nodes_.size()) return false;
        const auto& na = nodes_[a];
        const auto& nb = nodes_[b];
        if (na.dfs_in == 0 || nb.dfs_in == 0) return false;
        return (na.dfs_in <= nb.dfs_in) && (na.dfs_out >= nb.dfs_out);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool strictly_dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const noexcept {
        return (a != b) && dominates(a, b);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] BlockId nearest_common_dominator(BlockId a, BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (a == b) return a;
        if (a >= nodes_.size() || b >= nodes_.size()) return INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        if (dominates(a, b)) return a;
        if (dominates(b, a)) return b;

        BlockId curr_a = a;
        BlockId curr_b = b;
        while (curr_a != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && curr_b != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && curr_a != curr_b) {
            if (nodes_[curr_a].level > nodes_[curr_b].level) {
                curr_a = nodes_[curr_a].idom;
            } else if (nodes_[curr_b].level > nodes_[curr_a].level) {
                curr_b = nodes_[curr_b].idom;
            } else {
                curr_a = nodes_[curr_a].idom;
                curr_b = nodes_[curr_b].idom;
            }
        }
        return curr_a;
    }

    void compute(const MachineFunction& mf);
};

class MachineDominanceFrontier {
    std::vector<std::vector<BlockId>> frontiers_;

public:
    void clear() noexcept {
        frontiers_.clear();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept {
        return frontiers_.empty();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::span<const BlockId> get_frontier(BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (b >= frontiers_.size()) return {};
        return frontiers_[b];
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool in_frontier(BlockId a, BlockId b) const noexcept {
        if (a >= frontiers_.size()) return false;
        const auto& f = frontiers_[a];
        return std::find(f.begin(), f.end(), b) != f.end();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::vector<BlockId> compute_iterated_dominance_frontier(
        std::span<const BlockId> def_blocks) const;

    void compute(const MachineFunction& mf, const MachineDominatorTree& dom_tree);
};

class MachineLoopInfo {
    std::vector<MachineLoop> loops_;
    std::unordered_map<BlockId, std::size_t> block_to_loop_;

public:
    void clear() noexcept {
        loops_.clear();
        block_to_loop_.clear();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<MachineLoop>& loops() const noexcept {
        return loops_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const MachineLoop* get_loop_for(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        auto it = block_to_loop_.find(bb);
        if (it != block_to_loop_.end() && it->second < loops_.size()) {
            return &loops_[it->second];
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t get_loop_depth(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        const auto* l = get_loop_for(bb);
        return l ? l->depth : 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool is_loop_header(BlockId bb) const noexcept {
        const auto* l = get_loop_for(bb);
        return l && l->header == bb;
    }

    void add_loop(MachineLoop loop) {
        const std::size_t idx = loops_.size();
        for (BlockId bb : loop.blocks) {
            block_to_loop_[bb] = idx;
        }
        loops_.push_back(std::move(loop));
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool empty() const noexcept { return loops_.empty(); }
    [[nodiscard]] std::size_t size() const noexcept { return loops_.size(); }

    void compute(const MachineFunction& mf, const MachineDominatorTree& dom_tree);
};

/* ============================================================================
 * Algorithm Implementations
 * ========================================================================== */

inline void MachineDominatorTree::compute(const MachineFunction& mf) {
    clear();
    const auto block_span = mf.blocks();
    const std::size_t n = block_span.size();
    if (n == 0) return;

    root_ = mf.entry_block();
    if (root_ == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || root_ >= n) return;

    nodes_.resize(n);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        nodes_[i].block = static_cast<BlockId>(i);
        nodes_[i].idom = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
        nodes_[i].children.clear();
        nodes_[i].dfs_in = 0;
        nodes_[i].dfs_out = 0;
        nodes_[i].level = 0;
    }

    std::vector<bool> visited(n, false);
    std::vector<BlockId> post_order;
    post_order.reserve(n);
    std::vector<std::int32_t> post_order_num(n, -1);

    std::vector<std::pair<BlockId, std::size_t>> dfs_stack;
    visited[root_] = true;
    dfs_stack.push_back({root_, 0});

    while (!dfs_stack.empty()) {
        auto& [u, succ_idx] = dfs_stack.back();
        const auto* bb = mf.block(u);
        if (bb && succ_idx < bb->successors.size()) {
            BlockId succ = bb->successors[succ_idx++];
            if (succ < n && !visited[succ]) {
                visited[succ] = true;
                dfs_stack.push_back({succ, 0});
            }
        } else {
            post_order_num[u] = static_cast<std::int32_t>(post_order.size());
            post_order.push_back(u);
            dfs_stack.pop_back();
        }
    }

    std::vector<BlockId> idoms(n, INVALID_BLOCK_ID);
    idoms[root_] = root_;

    auto intersect = [&](BlockId b1, BlockId b2) -> BlockId {
        BlockId finger1 = b1;
        BlockId finger2 = b2;
        while (finger1 != finger2) {
            while (finger1 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && finger2 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID &&
                   post_order_num[finger1] < post_order_num[finger2]) {
                finger1 = idoms[finger1];
            }
            while (finger1 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && finger2 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID &&
                   post_order_num[finger2] < post_order_num[finger1]) {
                finger2 = idoms[finger2];
            }
            if (finger1 == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || finger2 == INVALID_BLOCK_ID) {
                break;
            }
        }
        return (finger1 != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) ? finger1 : finger2;
    };

    bool changed = true;
    while (changed) {
        changed = false;
        for (auto it = post_order.rbegin(); it != post_order.rend(); ++it) {
            const BlockId b = *it;
            if (b == root_) continue;
            const auto* bb = mf.block(b);
            if (!bb) continue;

            BlockId new_idom = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
            for (BlockId p : bb->predecessors) {
                if (p < n && idoms[p] != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) {
                    new_idom = p;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (new_idom == INVALID_BLOCK_ID) continue;

            for (BlockId p : bb->predecessors) {
                if (p < n && p != new_idom && idoms[p] != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) {
                    new_idom = intersect(p, new_idom);
                }
            }

            if (idoms[b] != new_idom) {
                idoms[b] = new_idom;
                changed = true;
            }
        }
    }
    idoms[root_] = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        nodes_[i].idom = idoms[i];
        if (idoms[i] != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && idoms[i] < n) {
            nodes_[idoms[i]].children.push_back(static_cast<BlockId>(i));
        }
    }

    std::uint32_t timestamp = 0;
    nodes_[root_].level = 0;
    nodes_[root_].dfs_in = ++timestamp;

    std::vector<std::pair<BlockId, std::size_t>> dom_stack;
    dom_stack.push_back({root_, 0});

    while (!dom_stack.empty()) {
        auto& [u, child_idx] = dom_stack.back();
        if (child_idx < nodes_[u].children.size()) {
            BlockId v = nodes_[u].children[child_idx++];
            nodes_[v].level = nodes_[u].level + 1;
            nodes_[v].dfs_in = ++timestamp;
            dom_stack.push_back({v, 0});
        } else {
            nodes_[u].dfs_out = ++timestamp;
            dom_stack.pop_back();
        }
    }
}

inline void MachineDominanceFrontier::compute(const MachineFunction& mf, const MachineDominatorTree& dom_tree) {
    clear();
    const auto n = mf.blocks().size();
    frontiers_.assign(n, {});
    if (n == 0 || dom_tree.empty()) return;

    for (const auto& bb : mf.blocks()) {
        if (bb.predecessors.size() < 2) continue;
        const BlockId b = bb.id;
        const BlockId idom_b = dom_tree.idom(b);

        for (BlockId p : bb.predecessors) {
            BlockId runner = p;
            while (runner != INVALID_BLOCK_ID && runner != idom_b && runner < n) {
                auto& df = frontiers_[runner];
                if (std::find(df.begin(), df.end(), b) == df.end()) {
                    df.push_back(b);
                }
                runner = dom_tree.idom(runner);
            }
        }
    }
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineDominanceFrontier::compute_iterated_dominance_frontier(
    std::span<const BlockId> def_blocks) const {
    std::vector<BlockId> idf;
    if (def_blocks.empty() || frontiers_.empty()) return idf;

    std::vector<bool> in_idf(frontiers_.size(), false);
    std::vector<bool> in_worklist(frontiers_.size(), false);
    std::vector<BlockId> worklist;
    worklist.reserve(def_blocks.size());

    for (BlockId b : def_blocks) {
        if (b < frontiers_.size()) {
            worklist.push_back(b);
            in_worklist[b] = true;
        }
    }

    std::size_t idx = 0;
    while (idx < worklist.size()) {
        BlockId node = worklist[idx++];
        if (node >= frontiers_.size()) continue;

        for (BlockId frontier_node : frontiers_[node]) {
            if (frontier_node < frontiers_.size() && !in_idf[frontier_node]) {
                in_idf[frontier_node] = true;
                idf.push_back(frontier_node);
                if (!in_worklist[frontier_node]) {
                    in_worklist[frontier_node] = true;
                    worklist.push_back(frontier_node);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return idf;
}

inline void MachineLoopInfo::compute(const MachineFunction& mf, const MachineDominatorTree& dom_tree) {
    clear();
    const auto block_count = mf.blocks().size();
    if (block_count == 0 || dom_tree.empty()) return;

    std::unordered_map<BlockId, std::vector<BlockId>> header_to_latches;
    for (const auto& bb : mf.blocks()) {
        const BlockId u = bb.id;
        for (BlockId v : bb.successors) {
            if (dom_tree.dominates(v, u)) {
                header_to_latches[v].push_back(u);
            }
        }
    }

    for (auto& [header, latches] : header_to_latches) {
        MachineLoop loop;
        loop.header = header;
        loop.latch = latches.front();
        loop.latches = latches;

        std::vector<bool> in_loop(block_count, false);
        loop.blocks.push_back(header);
        in_loop[header] = true;

        std::vector<BlockId> worklist;
        for (BlockId latch : latches) {
            if (!in_loop[latch]) {
                in_loop[latch] = true;
                loop.blocks.push_back(latch);
            }
            worklist.push_back(latch);
        }

        while (!worklist.empty()) {
            BlockId curr = worklist.back();
            worklist.pop_back();
            const auto* curr_bb = mf.block(curr);
            if (!curr_bb) continue;

            for (BlockId pred : curr_bb->predecessors) {
                if (pred < block_count && !in_loop[pred]) {
                    in_loop[pred] = true;
                    loop.blocks.push_back(pred);
                    worklist.push_back(pred);
                }
            }
        }

        const auto* header_bb = mf.block(header);
        if (header_bb) {
            BlockId candidate_preheader = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
            std::size_t outside_preds = 0;
            for (BlockId pred : header_bb->predecessors) {
                if (pred < block_count && !in_loop[pred]) {
                    outside_preds++;
                    candidate_preheader = pred;
                }
            }
            if (outside_preds == 1) {
                loop.preheader = candidate_preheader;
            }
        }

        for (BlockId b : loop.blocks) {
            const auto* b_bb = mf.block(b);
            if (!b_bb) continue;
            bool is_exiting = false;
            for (BlockId succ : b_bb->successors) {
                if (succ < block_count && !in_loop[succ]) {
                    is_exiting = true;
                    if (std::find(loop.exit_blocks.begin(), loop.exit_blocks.end(), succ) == loop.exit_blocks.end()) {
                        loop.exit_blocks.push_back(succ);
                    }
                }
            }
            if (is_exiting) {
                loop.exiting_blocks.push_back(b);
            }
        }

        loop.kind = MachineLoopKind::While;
        const auto* latch_bb = mf.block(loop.latch);
        if (latch_bb && !latch_bb->instructions.empty()) {
            const auto* last_inst = mf.instruction(latch_bb->instructions.back());
            if (last_inst && (last_inst->form == MachineForm::BranchCond || last_inst->form == MachineForm::BranchCompare)) {
                loop.kind = MachineLoopKind::DoWhile;
            }
        }

        add_loop(std::move(loop));
    }

    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < loops_.size(); ++i) {
        std::uint32_t depth = 1;
        for (std::size_t j = 0; j < loops_.size(); ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            if (loops_[j].blocks.size() > loops_[i].blocks.size() &&
                loops_[j].contains(loops_[i].header)) {
                depth++;
            }
        }
        loops_[i].depth = depth;
    }

    block_to_loop_.clear();
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < loops_.size(); ++i) {
        for (BlockId bb : loops_[i].blocks) {
            auto it = block_to_loop_.find(bb);
            if (it == block_to_loop_.end()) {
                block_to_loop_[bb] = i;
            } else {
                if (loops_[i].depth > loops_[it->second].depth) {
                    block_to_loop_[bb] = i;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Concrete bridge method implementations for MachineFunction
inline void MachineFunction::invalidate_analysis_caches() noexcept {
    dom_tree_valid_ = false;
    dom_frontier_valid_ = false;
    loop_info_valid_ = false;
}

inline void MachineFunction::insert_instruction_before(BlockId block_id, InstrId cursor_id, InstrId new_instr_id) {
    MachineBasicBlock* bb = block(block_id);
    MachineInstruction* new_inst = instruction(new_instr_id);
    if (!bb || !new_inst) return;
    if (new_inst->parent_block != INVALID_BLOCK_ID) return;

    if (cursor_id != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* cursor = instruction(cursor_id);
        if (!cursor || cursor->parent_block != block_id) return;
    }

    invalidate_analysis_caches();
    new_inst->parent_block = block_id;
    if (cursor_id == INVALID_INSTR_ID || bb->head_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        if (bb->tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            instruction(bb->tail_instr)->next_instr = new_instr_id;
            new_inst->prev_instr = bb->tail_instr;
            new_inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            bb->tail_instr = new_instr_id;
        } else {
            bb->head_instr = bb->tail_instr = new_instr_id;
            new_inst->prev_instr = new_inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        }
        bb->instructions.push_back(new_instr_id);
    } else {
        MachineInstruction* cursor = instruction(cursor_id);
        new_inst->next_instr = cursor_id;
        new_inst->prev_instr = cursor->prev_instr;
        if (cursor->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            instruction(cursor->prev_instr)->next_instr = new_instr_id;
        } else {
            bb->head_instr = new_instr_id;
        }
        cursor->prev_instr = new_instr_id;

        const auto it = std::find(bb->instructions.begin(), bb->instructions.end(), cursor_id);
        if (it == bb->instructions.end()) return;
        bb->instructions.insert(it, new_instr_id);
    }
    bb->instr_count = bb->instructions.size();
}

inline void MachineFunction::remove_instruction(InstrId instr_id) {
    MachineInstruction* inst = instruction(instr_id);
    if (!inst || inst->parent_block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID) return;
    MachineBasicBlock* bb = block(inst->parent_block);
    if (!bb) return;

    invalidate_analysis_caches();

    if (inst->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* prev = instruction(inst->prev_instr);
        if (prev) prev->next_instr = inst->next_instr;
    } else {
        bb->head_instr = inst->next_instr;
    }

    if (inst->next_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* next = instruction(inst->next_instr);
        if (next) next->prev_instr = inst->prev_instr;
    } else {
        bb->tail_instr = inst->prev_instr;
    }

    auto it = std::find(bb->instructions.begin(), bb->instructions.end(), instr_id);
    if (it != bb->instructions.end()) {
        bb->instructions.erase(it);
    }
    bb->instr_count = bb->instructions.size();

    inst->parent_block = INVALID_BLOCK_ID;
    inst->prev_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
    inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
}

inline void MachineFunction::splice_instructions(BlockId dest_block_id, InstrId cursor_id,
                                                BlockId src_block_id, InstrId first_instr_id, InstrId last_instr_id) {
    if (src_block_id == dest_block_id && first_instr_id == cursor_id) return;
    MachineBasicBlock* src_bb = block(src_block_id);
    MachineBasicBlock* dest_bb = block(dest_block_id);
    if (!src_bb || !dest_bb) return;

    MachineInstruction* first_inst = instruction(first_instr_id);
    MachineInstruction* last_inst = instruction(last_instr_id);
    if (!first_inst || !last_inst) return;
    if (first_inst->parent_block != src_block_id || last_inst->parent_block != src_block_id) return;

    invalidate_analysis_caches();

    std::vector<InstrId> range;
    InstrId cur = first_instr_id;
    while (cur != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        range.push_back(cur);
        MachineInstruction* ci = instruction(cur);
        if (!ci) break;
        ci->parent_block = dest_block_id;
        if (cur == last_instr_id) break;
        cur = ci->next_instr;
    }

    if (first_inst->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* prev = instruction(first_inst->prev_instr);
        if (prev) prev->next_instr = last_inst->next_instr;
    } else {
        src_bb->head_instr = last_inst->next_instr;
    }

    if (last_inst->next_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        MachineInstruction* next = instruction(last_inst->next_instr);
        if (next) next->prev_instr = first_inst->prev_instr;
    } else {
        src_bb->tail_instr = first_inst->prev_instr;
    }

    for (InstrId id : range) {
        auto it = std::find(src_bb->instructions.begin(), src_bb->instructions.end(), id);
        if (it != src_bb->instructions.end()) {
            src_bb->instructions.erase(it);
        }
    }
    src_bb->instr_count = src_bb->instructions.size();

    if (cursor_id == INVALID_INSTR_ID || dest_bb->head_instr == INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
        if (dest_bb->tail_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            instruction(dest_bb->tail_instr)->next_instr = first_instr_id;
            first_inst->prev_instr = dest_bb->tail_instr;
            last_inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            dest_bb->tail_instr = last_instr_id;
        } else {
            dest_bb->head_instr = first_instr_id;
            dest_bb->tail_instr = last_instr_id;
            first_inst->prev_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
            last_inst->next_instr = INVALID_INSTR_ID;
        }
        dest_bb->instructions.insert(dest_bb->instructions.end(), range.begin(), range.end());
    } else {
        MachineInstruction* cursor = instruction(cursor_id);
        first_inst->prev_instr = cursor->prev_instr;
        first_inst->next_instr = (first_instr_id == last_instr_id) ? cursor_id : first_inst->next_instr;
        last_inst->next_instr = cursor_id;

        if (cursor->prev_instr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            instruction(cursor->prev_instr)->next_instr = first_instr_id;
        } else {
            dest_bb->head_instr = first_instr_id;
        }
        cursor->prev_instr = last_instr_id;

        auto it = std::find(dest_bb->instructions.begin(), dest_bb->instructions.end(), cursor_id);
        if (it != dest_bb->instructions.end()) {
            dest_bb->instructions.insert(it, range.begin(), range.end());
        } else {
            dest_bb->instructions.insert(dest_bb->instructions.end(), range.begin(), range.end());
        }
    }
    dest_bb->instr_count = dest_bb->instructions.size();
}

inline void MachineFunction::recompute_dominators() const {
    if (!dom_tree_cache_ptr_) {
        dom_tree_cache_ptr_ = new MachineDominatorTree();
    }
    static_cast<MachineDominatorTree*>(dom_tree_cache_ptr_)->compute(*this);
    dom_tree_valid_ = true;
}

inline const MachineDominatorTree& MachineFunction::dominators() const {
    if (!dom_tree_valid_) {
        recompute_dominators();
    }
    return *static_cast<const MachineDominatorTree*>(dom_tree_cache_ptr_);
}

inline bool MachineFunction::dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const {
    return dominators().dominates(a, b);
}

inline bool MachineFunction::strictly_dominates(BlockId a, BlockId b) const {
    return dominators().strictly_dominates(a, b);
}

inline bool MachineFunction::instruction_dominates(InstrId a, InstrId b) const {
    if (a == b) return true;
    const auto* ia = instruction(a);
    const auto* ib = instruction(b);
    if (!ia || !ib) return false;
    if (ia->parent_block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || ib->parent_block == INVALID_BLOCK_ID) return false;

    if (ia->parent_block == ib->parent_block) {
        InstrId curr = ia->next_instr;
        while (curr != INVALID_INSTR_ID) {
            if (curr == b) return true;
            const auto* cinst = instruction(curr);
            if (!cinst) break;
            curr = cinst->next_instr;
        }
        return false;
    }

    return dominates(ia->parent_block, ib->parent_block);
}

inline bool MachineFunction::dominates(const MachineInstruction& a, const MachineInstruction& b) const {
    return instruction_dominates(a.id, b.id);
}

inline BlockId MachineFunction::nearest_common_dominator(BlockId a, BlockId b) const {
    return dominators().nearest_common_dominator(a, b);
}

inline void MachineFunction::recompute_dominance_frontier() const {
    if (!dom_frontier_cache_ptr_) {
        dom_frontier_cache_ptr_ = new MachineDominanceFrontier();
    }
    static_cast<MachineDominanceFrontier*>(dom_frontier_cache_ptr_)->compute(*this, dominators());
    dom_frontier_valid_ = true;
}

inline const MachineDominanceFrontier& MachineFunction::dominance_frontier() const {
    if (!dom_frontier_valid_) {
        recompute_dominance_frontier();
    }
    return *static_cast<const MachineDominanceFrontier*>(dom_frontier_cache_ptr_);
}

inline void MachineFunction::recompute_loop_info() const {
    if (!loop_info_cache_ptr_) {
        loop_info_cache_ptr_ = new MachineLoopInfo();
    }
    static_cast<MachineLoopInfo*>(loop_info_cache_ptr_)->compute(*this, dominators());
    loop_info_valid_ = true;
}

inline MachineLoopInfo& MachineFunction::loop_info() noexcept {
    if (!loop_info_valid_) {
        recompute_loop_info();
    }
    return *static_cast<MachineLoopInfo*>(loop_info_cache_ptr_);
}

inline const MachineLoopInfo& MachineFunction::loop_info() const noexcept {
    if (!loop_info_valid_) {
        recompute_loop_info();
    }
    return *static_cast<const MachineLoopInfo*>(loop_info_cache_ptr_);
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineFunction::traversal_preorder() const {
    std::vector<BlockId> order;
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || root >= blocks().size()) return order;
    std::vector<bool> visited(blocks().size(), false);
    std::vector<BlockId> stack;
    stack.push_back(root);
    visited[root] = true;

    while (!stack.empty()) {
        BlockId curr = stack.back();
        stack.pop_back();
        order.push_back(curr);
        const auto* bb = block(curr);
        if (!bb) continue;
        for (auto it = bb->successors.rbegin(); it != bb->successors.rend(); ++it) {
            BlockId succ = *it;
            if (succ < blocks().size() && !visited[succ]) {
                visited[succ] = true;
                stack.push_back(succ);
            }
        }
    }
    return order;
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineFunction::traversal_postorder() const {
    std::vector<BlockId> order;
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || root >= blocks().size()) return order;
    std::vector<bool> visited(blocks().size(), false);
    std::vector<std::pair<BlockId, std::size_t>> stack;
    visited[root] = true;
    stack.push_back({root, 0});

    while (!stack.empty()) {
        auto& [u, succ_idx] = stack.back();
        const auto* bb = block(u);
        if (bb && succ_idx < bb->successors.size()) {
            BlockId succ = bb->successors[succ_idx++];
            if (succ < blocks().size() && !visited[succ]) {
                visited[succ] = true;
                stack.push_back({succ, 0});
            }
        } else {
            order.push_back(u);
            stack.pop_back();
        }
    }
    return order;
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineFunction::traversal_reverse_postorder() const {
    auto order = traversal_postorder();
    std::reverse(order.begin(), order.end());
    return order;
}

inline std::vector<BlockId> MachineFunction::traversal_breadth_first() const {
    std::vector<BlockId> order;
    const BlockId root = entry_block();
    if (root == INVALID_BLOCK_ID || root >= blocks().size()) return order;
    std::vector<bool> visited(blocks().size(), false);
    std::queue<BlockId> q;
    visited[root] = true;
    q.push(root);

    while (!q.empty()) {
        BlockId curr = q.front();
        q.pop();
        order.push_back(curr);
        const auto* bb = block(curr);
        if (!bb) continue;
        for (BlockId succ : bb->successors) {
            if (succ < blocks().size() && !visited[succ]) {
                visited[succ] = true;
                q.push(succ);
            }
        }
    }
    return order;
}

} // namespace dgtl::machine
