module;

#include <cstdlib>
#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstddef>
#include <optional>
#include <limits>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

export module dgtl.mir.legalizer;

// Import native compiler base interfaces and sibling partitions
import dgtl.ir;

// Explicitly import target descriptions to resolve core::TargetMachine
import dgtl.target;
import dgtl.mir.runtime_policy;

/**
 * @brief Target-aware HIR legalizer.
 *
 * Canonical HIR -> target-aware legal HIR only.
 *
 * This stage:
 *   - may canonicalize/decompose target-illegal HIR;
 *   - may consult the selected TargetMachine for legality;
 *   - never creates target MachineOpcode values;
 *   - never assigns physical registers;
 *   - never constructs common MIR.
 *
 * Instruction selection owns the HIR -> common-MIR transition.
 */
export namespace dgtl::mir::lowering {

struct WideScalar128Operands {
    uint32_t lhs_lo = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
    uint32_t lhs_hi = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
    uint32_t rhs_lo = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
    uint32_t rhs_hi = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
    uint32_t result_lo = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
    uint32_t result_hi = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
};

struct LegalizationRemark {
    size_t node_index = 0;
    [[maybe_unused]] std::string_view detail; 
};

struct LegalizationResult {
    ir::IRStream stream;
    bool changed = false;
    std::vector<LegalizationRemark> remarks;

    [[nodiscard]] bool succeeded() const noexcept {
        return !stream.is_null();
    }
};

class MachineLegalizer {
public:
    static constexpr uint32_t kScalarWidth128TransportFlag = ir::ExtendedFlags::VEC_WIDTH_128;

    void register_scalar128_operands(size_t node_index, const WideScalar128Operands& operands) {
        if (node_index >= wide_scalar_nodes_.size()) {
            wide_scalar_nodes_.resize(node_index + 1);
        }
        wide_scalar_nodes_[node_index] = operands;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static bool requires_legalization(const ir::IRNode& node,
                                                    const core::TargetMachine& target) noexcept {
        if (is_branch_hint_opcode(node.opcode)) {
            return true;
        }

        // 128-bit scalar arithmetic is represented by the transport flag on the
        // semantic IR node. Legalization is target-driven: native 128-bit targets
        // keep the operation intact; narrower targets require decomposition.
        if ((node.logic_flags & kScalarWidth128TransportFlag) != 0 &&
            !target.description().supports_scalar_width(128)) {
            switch (node.opcode) {
                case ir::OpCode::ADD_I64:
                case ir::OpCode::SUB_I64:
                    return true;
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }

        // Half-precision promotion: targets without native FP16/BF16 promote to F32.
        if (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::ADD_BF16) {
            const bool native_fp16 = target.supports_scalar_width(16) ||
                                     target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                                     target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;
            if (!native_fp16) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        // Vector narrowing: 512-bit vector operations decompose to 256-bit on AVX2/narrow SIMD
        if ((node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VEC_ADD_F32_512 || node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VEC_MUL_F32_512) &&
            !target.description().supports_vector_width(512) && target.supports_vector_width(256)) {
            return true;
        }

        // Fixed 512-bit vector contract enforcement
        if (!target.description().supports_vector_width(512)) {
            switch (node.opcode) {
                case ir::OpCode::VEC_ADD_F32_512:
                case ir::OpCode::VEC_MUL_F32_512:
                case ir::OpCode::GATHER_F32_512:
                case ir::OpCode::STORE_V512:
                    return true;
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }

        if (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::FMA_F32) {
            const bool supports_fma = target.supports_fma() ||
                                      target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                                      target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;
            if (!supports_fma) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static bool requires_runtime_fallback(const ir::IRNode& node,
                                                        const core::TargetMachine& target) noexcept {
        if (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::FMA_F32) {
            const bool supports_fma = target.supports_fma() ||
                                      target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                                      target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;
            if (!supports_fma) {
                return runtime_policy::should_route_unhandled_to_runtime_call(target, node.opcode);
            }
        }

        if (!target.description().supports_vector_width(512)) {
            switch (node.opcode) {
                case ir::OpCode::VEC_ADD_F32_512:
                case ir::OpCode::VEC_MUL_F32_512:
                case ir::OpCode::GATHER_F32_512:
                case ir::OpCode::STORE_V512:
                    return runtime_policy::should_route_unhandled_to_runtime_call(target, node.opcode);
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] LegalizationResult legalize(const ir::IRStream& input, const core::TargetMachine& target) const {
        if (input.is_null()) {
            return {};
        }

        constexpr std::size_t kExpansionFactor = 6u;
        constexpr std::size_t kExpansionSlack = 32u;

        if (input.size() >
            (std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max() - kExpansionSlack) /
                kExpansionFactor) {
            LegalizationResult failed;
            failed.remarks.push_back(
                LegalizationRemark{0u, "legalization input is too large for bounded transactional capacity"});
            return failed;
        }

        LegalizationResult result;
        result.stream = ir::IRStream::allocate_transactional(
            input.size() * kExpansionFactor + kExpansionSlack);

        uint32_t next_temp = compute_next_virtual_id(input);

        result.remarks.reserve(input.size() / 4);

        auto propagate_node_metadata = [&](size_t in_idx, size_t out_idx) {
            const auto overflow = input.get_call_overflow_args(in_idx);
            if (!overflow.empty()) {
                result.stream.set_call_overflow_args(out_idx, overflow);
            }
            const auto words = input.get_call_abi_layout_words(in_idx);
            if (!words.empty()) {
                result.stream.set_call_abi_layout_words(out_idx, words);
            }
        };

        for (size_t i = 0; i < input.size(); ++i) {
            const ir::IRNode node = input.get_node(i);
            if (node.result_id != ir::INVALID_SLOT && node.result_id != 0u) {
                result.stream.set_result_type(node.result_id, input.get_result_type_code(node.result_id));
            }

            if (is_branch_hint_opcode(node.opcode)) {
                result.changed = true;
                ir::IRNode canonical = node;
                canonical.opcode = ir::OpCode::BRANCH_COND;
                canonical.flags |= ir::Flags::IS_BRANCH;
                canonical.refresh_intent_hash();
                result.stream.push_back(canonical);
                propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
                result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{i, "canonicalized BRANCH_LIKELY/BRANCH_UNLIKELY hint opcode to BRANCH_COND for backend-stable lowering"});
                continue;
            }

            const bool is_scalar128_expansion =
                (node.logic_flags & kScalarWidth128TransportFlag) != 0 &&
                !target.description().supports_scalar_width(128) &&
                (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::ADD_I64 || node.opcode == ir::OpCode::SUB_I64);

            if (is_scalar128_expansion) {
                if (i >= wide_scalar_nodes_.size() ||
                    wide_scalar_nodes_[i].lhs_lo == ir::INVALID_SLOT ||
                    wide_scalar_nodes_[i].lhs_hi == ir::INVALID_SLOT ||
                    wide_scalar_nodes_[i].rhs_lo == ir::INVALID_SLOT ||
                    wide_scalar_nodes_[i].rhs_hi == ir::INVALID_SLOT ||
                    wide_scalar_nodes_[i].result_lo == ir::INVALID_SLOT ||
                    wide_scalar_nodes_[i].result_hi == ir::INVALID_SLOT) {
                    result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                        i,
                        "128-bit scalar legalization requested without registered wide operand metadata; node preserved"
                    });
                    result.stream.push_back(node);
                    propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
                    continue;
                }

                if (next_temp == ir::INVALID_SLOT) {
                    result.stream.push_back(node);
                    propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
                    result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                        i,
                        "128-bit scalar legalization aborted: virtual-register ID space is exhausted; node preserved"
                    });
                    continue;
                }

                result.changed = true;
                expand_scalar128(node, wide_scalar_nodes_[i], next_temp, result.stream);
                result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                    i,
                    "expanded non-native 128-bit scalar arithmetic into low/high halves with carry chain"
                });
                continue;
            }

            const bool native_fp16 = target.supports_scalar_width(16) ||
                                     target.family() == core::TargetFamily::CUDA ||
                                     target.family() == core::TargetFamily::ROCm;
            const bool is_fp16_promotion =
                (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::ADD_BF16) && !native_fp16;

            if (is_fp16_promotion) {
                if (next_temp == ir::INVALID_SLOT) {
                    result.stream.push_back(node);
                    propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
                    result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                        i,
                        "FP16 promotion aborted: virtual-register ID space is exhausted; node preserved"
                    });
                    continue;
                }

                result.changed = true;
                expand_fp16_to_fp32_promotion(node, next_temp, result.stream);
                result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                    i,
                    "promoted ADD_BF16 to F32 arithmetic with convert-in/convert-out"
                });
                continue;
            }

            const bool is_vec512_narrowing =
                (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VEC_ADD_F32_512 || node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VEC_MUL_F32_512) &&
                !target.description().supports_vector_width(512) &&
                target.supports_vector_width(256);

            if (is_vec512_narrowing) {
                if (next_temp == ir::INVALID_SLOT) {
                    result.stream.push_back(node);
                    propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
                    continue;
                }

                result.changed = true;
                expand_vec512_to_vec256(node, next_temp, result.stream);
                result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                    i,
                    "narrowed 512-bit vector operation into dual 256-bit vector operations"
                });
                continue;
            }

            if (requires_legalization(node, target)) {
                result.stream.push_back(node);
                propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
                if (requires_runtime_fallback(node, target)) {
                    result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                        i,
                        "target-specific legality mismatch: generated form exists for the ISA family, but the selected TargetMachine requires runtime fallback"
                    });
                } else {
                    result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                        i,
                        "target requires legalization for this opcode, but no structural lowering recipe or runtime fallback exists; node preserved"
                    });
                }
                continue;
            }

            const runtime_policy::LegalizationRoute route =
                runtime_policy::classify(target, node.opcode);

            if (route == runtime_policy::LegalizationRoute::RuntimeCall) {
                result.stream.push_back(node);
                propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
                result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                    i,
                    "target-aware runtime-backed policy: no direct generated ISel mapping exists for this target; preserved for runtime-call lowering"
                });
                continue;
            }

            if (route == runtime_policy::LegalizationRoute::Unhandled) {
                result.stream.push_back(node);
                propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
                result.remarks.push_back(LegalizationRemark{
                    i,
                    "opcode has neither a target-specific generated ISel mapping nor a runtime contract; preserved for verifier/diagnostic handling"
                });
                continue;
            }

            result.stream.push_back(node);
            propagate_node_metadata(i, result.stream.size() - 1);
        }

        return result;
    }

private:
    std::vector<WideScalar128Operands> wide_scalar_nodes_;

    [[nodiscard]] static bool is_branch_hint_opcode(ir::OpCode opcode) noexcept {
        return opcode == ir::OpCode::BRANCH_LIKELY || opcode == ir::OpCode::BRANCH_UNLIKELY;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static uint32_t compute_next_virtual_id(
        const ir::IRStream& stream) noexcept {
        const uint32_t max_id = stream.get_max_id();

        if (max_id == ir::INVALID_SLOT || max_id >= 0xFFFFFFFEu) {
            return ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        }
        return max_id + 1u;
    }

    static void clear_width_transport_flag(ir::IRNode& node) noexcept {
        node.logic_flags &= ~kScalarWidth128TransportFlag;
    }

    static void expand_scalar128(const ir::IRNode& original,
                                 const WideScalar128Operands& pair,
                                 uint32_t& next_temp,
                                 ir::IRStream& out) {
        ir::IRNode low_op = original;
        low_op.result_id = next_temp++;
        low_op.op_a = pair.lhs_lo;
        low_op.op_b = pair.rhs_lo;
        low_op.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        clear_width_transport_flag(low_op);
        low_op.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(low_op);
        
        uint32_t low_result_temp = low_op.result_id;

        ir::IRNode carry_detect{};
        carry_detect.opcode = ir::OpCode::CMP_LT_I32;
        carry_detect.flags = ir::Flags::NONE;
        carry_detect.result_id = next_temp++;
        if (original.opcode == ir::OpCode::SUB_I64) {
            carry_detect.op_a = pair.lhs_lo;
            carry_detect.op_b = pair.rhs_lo;
        } else {
            carry_detect.op_a = low_result_temp;
            carry_detect.op_b = pair.lhs_lo;
        }
        carry_detect.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        carry_detect.logic_flags = ir::ExtendedFlags::LATENCY_CRITICAL;
        carry_detect.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(carry_detect);
        
        uint32_t carry_bit = carry_detect.result_id;

        ir::IRNode high_partial = original;
        high_partial.result_id = next_temp++;
        high_partial.op_a = pair.lhs_hi;
        high_partial.op_b = pair.rhs_hi;
        high_partial.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        clear_width_transport_flag(high_partial);
        high_partial.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(high_partial);
        
        uint32_t high_partial_result = high_partial.result_id;

        ir::IRNode high_with_carry = original;
        high_with_carry.opcode = (original.opcode == ir::OpCode::SUB_I64)
            ? ir::OpCode::SUB_I64
            : ir::OpCode::ADD_I64;
        high_with_carry.result_id = next_temp++;
        high_with_carry.op_a = high_partial_result;
        high_with_carry.op_b = carry_bit;
        high_with_carry.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        clear_width_transport_flag(high_with_carry);
        high_with_carry.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(high_with_carry);
        
        uint32_t high_result_temp = high_with_carry.result_id;

        ir::IRNode move_low{};
        move_low.opcode = ir::OpCode::REG_MOVE;
        move_low.flags = ir::Flags::NONE;
        move_low.result_id = pair.result_lo;
        move_low.op_a = low_result_temp;
        move_low.op_b = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        move_low.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        move_low.logic_flags = ir::ExtendedFlags::DETERMINISTIC;
        move_low.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(move_low);

        ir::IRNode move_high{};
        move_high.opcode = ir::OpCode::REG_MOVE;
        move_high.flags = ir::Flags::NONE;
        move_high.result_id = pair.result_hi;
        move_high.op_a = high_result_temp;
        move_high.op_b = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        move_high.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        move_high.logic_flags = ir::ExtendedFlags::DETERMINISTIC;
        move_high.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(move_high);
    }

    static void expand_fp16_to_fp32_promotion(const ir::IRNode& original,
                                              uint32_t& next_temp,
                                              ir::IRStream& out) {
        ir::IRNode cvt_a{};
        cvt_a.opcode = ir::OpCode::FCVTL_F16_TO_F32;
        cvt_a.result_id = next_temp++;
        cvt_a.op_a = original.op_a;
        cvt_a.op_b = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        cvt_a.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        cvt_a.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(cvt_a);

        ir::IRNode cvt_b{};
        cvt_b.opcode = ir::OpCode::FCVTL_F16_TO_F32;
        cvt_b.result_id = next_temp++;
        cvt_b.op_a = original.op_b;
        cvt_b.op_b = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        cvt_b.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        cvt_b.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(cvt_b);

        ir::IRNode add_f32{};
        add_f32.opcode = ir::OpCode::ADD_F32;
        add_f32.result_id = next_temp++;
        add_f32.op_a = cvt_a.result_id;
        add_f32.op_b = cvt_b.result_id;
        add_f32.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        add_f32.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(add_f32);

        ir::IRNode cvt_res{};
        cvt_res.opcode = ir::OpCode::FCVTN_F32_TO_F16;
        cvt_res.result_id = original.result_id;
        cvt_res.op_a = add_f32.result_id;
        cvt_res.op_b = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        cvt_res.op_c = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
        cvt_res.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(cvt_res);
    }

    static void expand_vec512_to_vec256(const ir::IRNode& original,
                                        uint32_t& next_temp,
                                        ir::IRStream& out) {
        ir::IRNode low_half = original;
        low_half.result_id = next_temp++;
        low_half.logic_flags = (low_half.logic_flags & ~ir::ExtendedFlags::VEC_WIDTH_512) | ir::ExtendedFlags::VEC_WIDTH_256;
        low_half.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(low_half);

        ir::IRNode high_half = original;
        high_half.result_id = original.result_id;
        high_half.logic_flags = (high_half.logic_flags & ~ir::ExtendedFlags::VEC_WIDTH_512) | ir::ExtendedFlags::VEC_WIDTH_256;
        high_half.refresh_intent_hash();
        out.push_back(high_half);
    }
};

using Legalizer = MachineLegalizer;

} // namespace dgtl::mir::lowering

module;

#include <cstdint>

export module dgtl.mir.runtime_policy;

import dgtl.ir_enums;
import dgtl.target;
import dgtl.isa.isel_dispatch;

// Generated target-aware MachineForm classification.
import dgtl.mir.isel_gen;

/**
 * @brief Target-aware legalization policy for DGTL's IR -> ISA boundary.
 *
 * This layer answers one policy question:
 *   "For this exact TargetMachine and IR opcode, is there a direct generated
 *    ISel path, should the operation be lowered through the DGTL runtime, or
 *    is it unsupported?"
 */
export namespace dgtl::mir::runtime_policy {

using dgtl::mir::MachineForm;

/**
 * @brief Backend disposition after target-aware ISel classification.
 */
enum class LegalizationRoute : std::uint8_t {
    DirectISel = 0,
    RuntimeCall,
    Unhandled
};

/**
 * @brief Classify one IR opcode for one concrete compilation target.
 *
 * Precedence is intentional:
 *   1. Target-aware generated ISel
 *   2. Target runtime-backed lowering provenance
 *   3. Fully unsupported
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline LegalizationRoute classify(
    const core::TargetMachine& target,
    ir::OpCode opcode) noexcept
{
    const MachineForm form =
        dgtl::isa::classify_machine_form(target, opcode);

    if (form != MachineForm::Unhandled) [[likely]] {
        return LegalizationRoute::DirectISel;
    }

    if (dgtl::isa::is_runtime_backed(target, opcode)) [[unlikely]] {
        return LegalizationRoute::RuntimeCall;
    }

    return LegalizationRoute::Unhandled;
}

/**
 * @brief True when generated target-specific ISel owns the lowering.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline bool has_direct_isel_mapping(
    const core::TargetMachine& target,
    ir::OpCode opcode) noexcept
{
    return classify(target, opcode) == LegalizationRoute::DirectISel;
}

/**
 * @brief True when no direct target ISel exists but a runtime fallback exists.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline bool should_route_unhandled_to_runtime_call(
    const core::TargetMachine& target,
    ir::OpCode opcode) noexcept
{
    return classify(target, opcode) == LegalizationRoute::RuntimeCall;
}

/**
 * @brief True when neither direct ISel nor a runtime fallback exists.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline bool is_fully_unhandled_opcode(
    const core::TargetMachine& target,
    ir::OpCode opcode) noexcept
{
    return classify(target, opcode) == LegalizationRoute::Unhandled;
}

/**
 * @brief Convenience predicate for callers that need the final disposition.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline bool is_direct_or_runtime_supported(
    const core::TargetMachine& target,
    ir::OpCode opcode) noexcept
{
    return classify(target, opcode) != LegalizationRoute::Unhandled;
}

/**
 * @brief Return the generated MachineForm for diagnostics and downstream policy.
 */
[[nodiscard]] inline MachineForm machine_form(
    const core::TargetMachine& target,
    ir::OpCode opcode) noexcept
{
    return dgtl::isa::classify_machine_form(target, opcode);
}

} // namespace dgtl::mir::runtime_policy

/**
 * @brief X64_Machine_Form_Lowering
 *
 * Full X64 lowering implementation.
 *
 * This file intentionally preserves the complete legacy encoding implementation
 * while the backend is migrated to the common-MIR ABI.  No encoder, frame,
 * allocation, CFG, or emission capability is deleted merely because its old
 * interface is being retired.
 *
 * Architectural ownership:
 *   IR/frontend -> legalization -> ISel -> common MIR
 *      -> X64TargetLoweringContract -> X64 machine opcodes
 *      -> register allocation/frame lowering -> machine encoding.
 *
 * The lowering layer queries generated kind tables and dispatches to encoder methods,
 * leaving the emitter as a thin register-state plus byte-encoding shell only.
 */
class X64FormLowering {
public:
    using ByteStream = std::vector<uint8_t>;
    using Emitter = void*; // Forward ref; actual type is X64StrikeEmitter

    // Encoder method type (void (X64StrikeEmitter::*)(const ir::IRNode&, ByteStream&))
    // We use std::function to store these
    using EncoderFunc = std::function<void(const ir::IRNode&, ByteStream&)>;

    struct EncoderCallbacks {
        EncoderFunc add_i32;
        EncoderFunc sub_i32;
        EncoderFunc mul_i32;
        EncoderFunc div_i32;
        EncoderFunc mod_i32;
        EncoderFunc and_i32;
        EncoderFunc or_i32;
        EncoderFunc xor_i32;
        EncoderFunc shl_i32;
        EncoderFunc shr_i32;

        EncoderFunc add_i64;
        EncoderFunc sub_i64;
        EncoderFunc mul_i64;
        EncoderFunc div_i64;
        EncoderFunc mod_i64;
        EncoderFunc and_i64;
        EncoderFunc or_i64;
        EncoderFunc xor_i64;
        EncoderFunc shl_i64;
        EncoderFunc shr_i64;

        EncoderFunc add_f32;
        EncoderFunc sub_f32;
        EncoderFunc mul_f32;
        EncoderFunc div_f32;

        EncoderFunc add_f64;
        EncoderFunc sub_f64;
        EncoderFunc mul_f64;
        EncoderFunc div_f64;

        EncoderFunc cmp_i32;

        EncoderFunc vec_add_f32_512;
        EncoderFunc vec_mul_f32_512;

        std::function<void(const char*, uint32_t)> fail_emission;
    };

    explicit X64FormLowering(const EncoderCallbacks& cbs) : callbacks_(cbs) {}

    inline void lower_integer_binary32(const SelectedInstruction& selected, ByteStream& out) {
        switch (generated::integer_binary32_kind(selected.semantic_opcode)) {
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Add:
                callbacks_.add_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Sub:
                callbacks_.sub_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Mul:
                callbacks_.mul_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Div:
                callbacks_.div_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Mod:
                callbacks_.mod_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::And:
                callbacks_.and_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Or:
                callbacks_.or_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Xor:
                callbacks_.xor_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Shl:
                callbacks_.shl_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::Shr:
                callbacks_.shr_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary32Kind::None:
            default:
                callbacks_.fail_emission("Unhandled MachineForm::IntegerBinary32 opcode",
                                         static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                return;
        }
    }

    inline void lower_integer_binary64(const SelectedInstruction& selected, ByteStream& out) {
        switch (generated::integer_binary64_kind(selected.semantic_opcode)) {
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Add:
                callbacks_.add_i64(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Sub:
                callbacks_.sub_i64(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Mul:
                callbacks_.mul_i64(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Div:
                callbacks_.div_i64(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Mod:
                callbacks_.mod_i64(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::And:
                if (callbacks_.and_i64) {
                    callbacks_.and_i64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for IntegerBinary64::And",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Or:
                if (callbacks_.or_i64) {
                    callbacks_.or_i64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for IntegerBinary64::Or",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Xor:
                if (callbacks_.xor_i64) {
                    callbacks_.xor_i64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for IntegerBinary64::Xor",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Shl:
                if (callbacks_.shl_i64) {
                    callbacks_.shl_i64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for IntegerBinary64::Shl",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::Shr:
                if (callbacks_.shr_i64) {
                    callbacks_.shr_i64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for IntegerBinary64::Shr",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::IntegerBinary64Kind::None:
            default:
                callbacks_.fail_emission("Unhandled MachineForm::IntegerBinary64 opcode",
                                         static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                return;
        }
    }

    inline void lower_float_binary32(const SelectedInstruction& selected, ByteStream& out) {
        switch (generated::float_binary32_kind(selected.semantic_opcode)) {
            case generated::FloatBinary32Kind::Add:
                callbacks_.add_f32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::FloatBinary32Kind::Sub:
                if (callbacks_.sub_f32) {
                    callbacks_.sub_f32(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for FloatBinary32::Sub",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::FloatBinary32Kind::Mul:
                callbacks_.mul_f32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::FloatBinary32Kind::Div:
                if (callbacks_.div_f32) {
                    callbacks_.div_f32(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for FloatBinary32::Div",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::FloatBinary32Kind::None:
            default:
                callbacks_.fail_emission("Unhandled MachineForm::FloatBinary32 opcode",
                                         static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                return;
        }
    }

    inline void lower_float_binary64(const SelectedInstruction& selected, ByteStream& out) {
        switch (generated::float_binary64_kind(selected.semantic_opcode)) {
            case generated::FloatBinary64Kind::Add:
                if (callbacks_.add_f64) {
                    callbacks_.add_f64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for FloatBinary64::Add",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::FloatBinary64Kind::Sub:
                if (callbacks_.sub_f64) {
                    callbacks_.sub_f64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for FloatBinary64::Sub",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::FloatBinary64Kind::Mul:
                if (callbacks_.mul_f64) {
                    callbacks_.mul_f64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for FloatBinary64::Mul",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::FloatBinary64Kind::Div:
                if (callbacks_.div_f64) {
                    callbacks_.div_f64(selected.node, out);
                } else {
                    callbacks_.fail_emission("Missing X64 encoder callback for FloatBinary64::Div",
                                             static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                }
                return;
            case generated::FloatBinary64Kind::None:
            default:
                callbacks_.fail_emission("Unhandled MachineForm::FloatBinary64 opcode",
                                         static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                return;
        }
    }

    inline void lower_compare32(const SelectedInstruction& selected, ByteStream& out) {
        switch (generated::compare32_kind(selected.semantic_opcode)) {
            case generated::Compare32Kind::Any:
                callbacks_.cmp_i32(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::Compare32Kind::None:
            default:
                callbacks_.fail_emission("Unhandled MachineForm::Compare32 opcode",
                                         static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                return;
        }
    }

    inline void lower_vector_binary512(const SelectedInstruction& selected, ByteStream& out) {
        switch (generated::vector_binary512_kind(selected.semantic_opcode)) {
            case generated::VectorBinary512Kind::Add:
                callbacks_.vec_add_f32_512(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::VectorBinary512Kind::Mul:
                callbacks_.vec_mul_f32_512(selected.node, out);
                return;
            case generated::VectorBinary512Kind::None:
            default:
                callbacks_.fail_emission("Unhandled MachineForm::VectorBinary512 opcode",
                                         static_cast<uint32_t>(selected.semantic_opcode));
                return;
        }
    }

private:
    EncoderCallbacks callbacks_;
};

} // namespace dgtl::isa::x64::lowering

export namespace dgtl::isa::x64 {

/*
 * ============================================================================
 * MIR construction / verification bridge
 *
 * P0 architecture rule:
 *   IR -> Legalize -> ISel -> common MIR -> target lowering -> emission.
 *
 * The byte emitter below still contains the legacy IR-oriented encoding
 * routines for compatibility. This bridge makes the common Machine IR an
 * explicit, verified artifact at the X64 boundary instead of allowing the
 * backend to silently bypass it.
 *
 * It deliberately does not assign physical registers or target opcodes.
 * Those remain target-lowering concerns.
 *
 * The byte encoder below remains a legacy IR-shaped compatibility path.
 * Therefore this bridge is not yet the final MIR-native encoder boundary;
 * target lowering and byte emission must still consume MachineInstruction
 * and MachineOperand directly in the next migration stage.
 * ============================================================================
 */
/*
 * ============================================================================
 * X64 target-lowering contract
 *
 * This is the ownership boundary for concrete X64 opcodes.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 *   - Common MIR owns MachineForm and semantic provenance.
 *   - This layer owns concrete X64 MachineOpcode assignment.
 *   - No IR OpCode is accepted by this API.
 *   - No IRNode is accepted by this API.
 *
 * The semantic operation kind needed to distinguish (for example) ADD from
 * SUB is deliberately NOT reconstructed from semantic_provenance here.
 * The common MIR contract must carry that information explicitly.  Until that
 * field is added to Machine_IR, this resolver rejects ambiguous forms instead
 * of silently reinterpreting provenance as an opcode.
 * ============================================================================
 */
class X64TargetLoweringContract {
public:
    using MachineOpcode = machine::MachineOpcode;

    struct Result {
        MachineOpcode opcode = machine::INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE;
        bool assigned = false;
        const char* diagnostic = nullptr;
    };

    [[nodiscard]] static Result assign_opcode(
        const machine::MachineInstruction& instruction) noexcept {
        /*
         * Target opcode assignment is intentionally centralized here.
         *
         * We cannot legally derive ADD/SUB/MUL/... from
         * semantic_provenance: that value is opaque provenance after ISel.
         * A future MachineSemanticKind field in Machine_IR is the required
         * input for this dispatch.
         */
        switch (instruction.form) {
            case machine::MachineForm::NoOp:
            case machine::MachineForm::Move:
            case machine::MachineForm::LoadImmediate:
            case machine::MachineForm::LoadStack:
            case machine::MachineForm::StoreStack:
            case machine::MachineForm::IntegerBinary32:
            case machine::MachineForm::IntegerBinary64:
            case machine::MachineForm::FloatBinary32:
            case machine::MachineForm::FloatBinary64:
            case machine::MachineForm::FloatTernaryFma32:
            case machine::MachineForm::Compare32:
            case machine::MachineForm::VectorBinary512:
            case machine::MachineForm::VectorGather512:
            case machine::MachineForm::VectorStore512:
            case machine::MachineForm::Call:
            case machine::MachineForm::Return:
            case machine::MachineForm::Goto:
            case machine::MachineForm::BranchCond:
            case machine::MachineForm::BranchCompare:
            case machine::MachineForm::Syscall:
            case machine::MachineForm::TensorOp:
            case machine::MachineForm::HardwareFence:
            case machine::MachineForm::AtomicOp:
            case machine::MachineForm::ConcurrentSync:
                return Result{
                    machine::INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE,
                    false,
                    "X64 target opcode assignment requires MachineSemanticKind in common MIR"};
            case machine::MachineForm::Unhandled:
            default:
                return Result{
                    machine::INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE,
                    false,
                    "X64 target lowering received MachineForm::Unhandled"};
        }
    }
};

class X64MIRBoundary {
public:
    using BlockMap = std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, machine::BlockId>;

    [[nodiscard]] static bool build_and_verify(
        const ir::IRStream& stream,
        const core::TargetMachine& target,
        const ir::IRStream& legalized_stream,
        lowering::InstructionSelectorProgram& selector,
        machine::MachineFunction& function,
        std::string& diagnostic) {
        diagnostic.clear();

        const auto arch = target.get_arch();
        if (arch != core::ArchType::X86_64_AVX2 &&
            arch != core::ArchType::X86_64_AVX512) {
            diagnostic = "X64 MIR boundary received a non-x86-64 target";
            return false;
        }

        const auto& ir_blocks = legalized_stream.get_basic_blocks();
        const auto& ir_edges = legalized_stream.get_cfg_edges();

        BlockMap block_map;
        block_map.reserve(ir_blocks.size() * 2u + 1u);

        if (ir_blocks.empty()) {
            function.create_block(true);
        } else {
            const std::uint32_t entry_id = legalized_stream.get_entry_block_id();

            for (const auto& src_block : ir_blocks) {
                const bool is_entry =
                    (src_block.id == entry_id) ||
                    (entry_id == ir::INVALID_SLOT && block_map.empty());

                const machine::BlockId machine_id =
                    function.create_block(is_entry).id;
                block_map.emplace(src_block.id, machine_id);
            }

            for (const auto& edge : ir_edges) {
                const auto from = block_map.find(edge.from);
                const auto to = block_map.find(edge.to);
                if (from == block_map.end() || to == block_map.end() ||
                    !function.connect_blocks(from->second, to->second)) {
                    diagnostic = "failed to construct MIR CFG edge";
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        /*
         * Resolve source labels to MIR block ids. The common MIR must carry
         * block references, never backend-specific label integers.
         */
        std::unordered_map<std::uint32_t, machine::BlockId> label_to_block;
        label_to_block.reserve(legalized_stream.size() / 4u + 4u);

        auto record_label = [&](const ir::IRNode& node,
                                machine::BlockId block) {
            if (node.opcode != ir::OpCode::LABEL) {
                return;
            }

            if (node.result_id != ir::INVALID_SLOT && node.result_id != 0u) {
                label_to_block[node.result_id] = block;
            }

            const bool function_header =
                (node.flags & (ir::Flags::HAS_SIDE_EFFECTS |
                               ir::Flags::IS_VOLATILE)) != 0u;

            if (!function_header) {
                if (node.op_a != ir::INVALID_SLOT && node.op_a != 0u) {
                    label_to_block[node.op_a] = block;
                }
                if (node.op_b != ir::INVALID_SLOT && node.op_b != 0u) {
                    label_to_block[node.op_b] = block;
                }
            }
        };

        /*
         * First pass: establish label ownership independently of instruction
         * selection so branch operands can become real MIR block references.
         */
        if (!ir_blocks.empty()) {
            for (const auto& src_block : ir_blocks) {
                const auto block_it = block_map.find(src_block.id);
                if (block_it == block_map.end()) {
                    diagnostic = "MIR block mapping is incomplete";
                    return false;
                }

                const std::size_t begin =
                    std::min<std::size_t>(src_block.begin, legalized_stream.size());
                const std::size_t end =
                    std::min<std::size_t>(src_block.end, legalized_stream.size());

                for (std::size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {
                    record_label(legalized_stream.get_node(i), block_it->second);
                }
            }
        } else {
            for (std::size_t i = 0; i < legalized_stream.size(); ++i) {
                record_label(legalized_stream.get_node(i), 0u);
            }
        }

        machine::MachineFunctionBuilder builder(function);

        auto find_block_for_node = [&](std::size_t node_index)
            -> machine::BlockId {
            for (const auto& src_block : ir_blocks) {
                if (node_index >= src_block.begin &&
                    node_index < src_block.end) {
                    const auto it = block_map.find(src_block.id);
                    if (it != block_map.end()) {
                        return it->second;
                    }
                }
            }
            return function.entry_block() == machine::INVALID_BLOCK_ID
                ? 0u
                : function.entry_block();
        };

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < legalized_stream.size(); ++i) {
            const ir::IRNode node = legalized_stream.get_node(i);
            const auto selected =
                selector.select_instruction(
                    legalized_stream, i, node, target);

            if (selected.form == lowering::MachineForm::Unhandled) {
                diagnostic = "ISel produced MachineForm::Unhandled";
                return false;
            }

            const machine::BlockId block = find_block_for_node(i);
            machine::MachineInstruction& instruction =
                selector.append_to_machine_function(
                    builder, block, selected);

            /*
             * HARD MIR CONTRACT:
             *
             * Instruction selection creates COMMON MIR only.  It must not
             * manufacture a target opcode by casting semantic provenance.
             *
             * Concrete X64 opcode assignment belongs to the dedicated target
             * lowering stage, after this common MIR has been constructed.
             * Machine_IR intentionally initializes opcode to INVALID.
             *
             * The previous implementation encoded IR OpCode directly into
             * MachineOpcode here.  That was an architectural violation:
             *
             *     static_cast<MachineOpcode>(semantic_opcode)
             *
             * is forbidden because it couples the common MIR ABI to the IR
             * opcode namespace.
             */
            if (instruction.opcode != machine::INVALID_MACHINE_OPCODE) {
                diagnostic = "common X64 MIR arrived with a preassigned target opcode";
                return false;
            }

            // The generic ISel bridge intentionally preserves source value ids.
            // LoadImmediate is the one form whose operand is not a value id, so
            // canonicalize its immediate here, before the MIR enters target byte
            // emission. This keeps the encoder independent of IRNode.
            if (selected.form == lowering::MachineForm::Move &&
                (selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::LOAD ||
                 selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VOLATILE_LOAD ||
                 selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::STORE ||
                 selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VOLATILE_STORE)) {
                const ir::TypeCode value_type =
                    legalized_stream.get_result_type_code(
                        selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::STORE ||
                        selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VOLATILE_STORE
                            ? selected.node.op_b
                            : selected.node.result_id);
                const std::uint32_t width =
                    value_type == ir::TypeCode::F32 ? 4u :
                    value_type == ir::TypeCode::F64 ? 8u :
                    value_type == ir::TypeCode::I32 ? 4u :
                    value_type == ir::TypeCode::I8 ? 1u :
                    value_type == ir::TypeCode::I16 ? 2u : 8u;

                if (selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::LOAD ||
                    selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VOLATILE_LOAD) {
                    if (instruction.operands.size() >= 2u &&
                        instruction.operands[1].kind == machine::MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                        const machine::MachineRegister pointer_reg =
                            instruction.operands[1].reg;
                        machine::MachineOperand memory{};
                        memory.kind = machine::MachineOperandKind::Memory;
                        memory.role = machine::OperandRole::Use;
                        memory.memory.base = pointer_reg;
                        memory.memory.size_bytes = width;
                        memory.memory.alignment = width;
                        memory.memory.load = true;
                        memory.memory.volatile_access =
                            selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VOLATILE_LOAD;
                        instruction.operands.erase(instruction.operands.begin() + 1);
                        instruction.add_operand(std::move(memory));
                    }
                } else {
                    if (instruction.operands.size() >= 3u &&
                        instruction.operands[1].kind == machine::MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                        instruction.operands[2].kind == machine::MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                        const machine::MachineRegister pointer_reg = instruction.operands[1].reg;
                        const machine::MachineRegister value_reg = instruction.operands[2].reg;
                        machine::MachineOperand memory{};
                        memory.kind = machine::MachineOperandKind::Memory;
                        memory.role = machine::OperandRole::Use;
                        memory.memory.base = pointer_reg;
                        memory.memory.size_bytes = width;
                        memory.memory.alignment = width;
                        memory.memory.store = true;
                        memory.memory.volatile_access =
                            selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VOLATILE_STORE;
                        instruction.operands.erase(instruction.operands.begin() + 1,
                                                   instruction.operands.begin() + 3);
                        instruction.add_operand(machine::MachineOperand::use(value_reg));
                        instruction.add_operand(std::move(memory));
                    }
                }

                // Carry scalar value class into MIR without importing IR types
                // into the encoder. Register class is already a first-class MIR
                // field and is sufficient to distinguish GPR vs XMM emission.
                if (value_type == ir::TypeCode::F32 || value_type == ir::TypeCode::F64) {
                    if (selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::LOAD ||
                        selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::VOLATILE_LOAD) {
                        if (!instruction.operands.empty() &&
                            instruction.operands.front().kind == machine::MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                            instruction.operands.front().reg.reg_class =
                                machine::RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint;
                        }
                    } else if (instruction.operands.size() >= 2u &&
                               instruction.operands[1].kind == machine::MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                        instruction.operands[1].reg.reg_class =
                            machine::RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (selected.form == lowering::MachineForm::Return &&
                !instruction.operands.empty() &&
                instruction.operands.front().kind == machine::MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                const std::uint32_t return_value =
                    selected.node.op_a != ir::INVALID_SLOT
                        ? selected.node.op_a
                        : selected.node.result_id;
                const ir::TypeCode return_type =
                    legalized_stream.get_result_type_code(return_value);
                if (return_type == ir::TypeCode::F32 || return_type == ir::TypeCode::F64) {
                    instruction.operands.front().reg.reg_class =
                        machine::RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint;
                    instruction.operands.front().subregister =
                        return_type == ir::TypeCode::F32 ? 32u : 64u;
                }
            }

            if (selected.form == lowering::MachineForm::LoadImmediate) {
                const std::uint64_t bits =
                    (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(node.op_b) << 32u) |
                    static_cast<std::uint64_t>(node.op_a);
                if (selected.node.opcode == ir::OpCode::CONST) {
                    const ir::TypeCode type =
                        legalized_stream.get_result_type_code(node.result_id);
                    if (type == ir::TypeCode::F32 || type == ir::TypeCode::F64) {
                        machine::MachineOperand immediate{};
                        immediate.kind = machine::MachineOperandKind::FloatingImmediate;
                        immediate.role = machine::OperandRole::Use;
                        immediate.floating_immediate = std::bit_cast<double>(bits);
                        instruction.operands.erase(
                            std::remove_if(
                                instruction.operands.begin(),
                                instruction.operands.end(),
                                [](const machine::MachineOperand& op) {
                                    return op.role == machine::OperandRole::Use;
                                }),
                            instruction.operands.end());
                        instruction.add_operand(std::move(immediate));
                    } else {
                        instruction.operands.erase(
                            std::remove_if(
                                instruction.operands.begin(),
                                instruction.operands.end(),
                                [](const machine::MachineOperand& op) {
                                    return op.role == machine::OperandRole::Use;
                                }),
                            instruction.operands.end());
                        instruction.add_operand(machine::MachineOperand::imm(
                            static_cast<std::int64_t>(bits)));
                    }
                } else {
                    instruction.operands.erase(
                        std::remove_if(
                            instruction.operands.begin(),
                            instruction.operands.end(),
                            [](const machine::MachineOperand& op) {
                                return op.role == machine::OperandRole::Use;
                            }),
                        instruction.operands.end());
                    instruction.add_operand(machine::MachineOperand::imm(
                        static_cast<std::int64_t>(bits)));
                }
            }

            /*
             * Branch targets are block references in MIR. The current
             * compatibility bridge in Instruction_Selector still initially
             * represents source operands as value ids, so repair only the
             * target operand here without changing the semantic source node.
             */
            auto rewrite_branch_target =
                [&](std::size_t operand_position, std::uint32_t label) -> bool {
                    const auto target_it = label_to_block.find(label);
                    if (target_it == label_to_block.end() ||
                        operand_position >= instruction.operands.size()) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    machine::MachineOperand& operand =
                        instruction.operands[operand_position];
                    operand = machine::MachineOperand::block_ref(
                        target_it->second);
                    return true;
                };

            switch (selected.form) {
                case lowering::MachineForm::Goto:
                    if (node.op_a != ir::INVALID_SLOT &&
                        node.op_a != 0u) {
                        if (!rewrite_branch_target(0u, node.op_a)) {
                            diagnostic = "unresolved GOTO MIR block target";
                            return false;
                        }
                    } else if (node.result_id != ir::INVALID_SLOT &&
                               node.result_id != 0u) {
                        if (!rewrite_branch_target(0u, node.result_id)) {
                            diagnostic = "unresolved GOTO MIR block target";
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                    break;

                case lowering::MachineForm::BranchCond:
                    if (node.op_b != ir::INVALID_SLOT &&
                        node.op_b != 0u) {
                        if (!rewrite_branch_target(1u, node.op_b)) {
                            diagnostic = "unresolved conditional branch MIR target";
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                    break;

                case lowering::MachineForm::BranchCompare:
                    if (node.op_c != ir::INVALID_SLOT &&
                        node.op_c != 0u) {
                        if (!rewrite_branch_target(2u, node.op_c)) {
                            diagnostic = "unresolved compare branch MIR target";
                            return false;
                        }
                    }
                    break;

                default:
                    break;
            }
        }

        const machine::MachineVerifyResult verification =
            machine::MachineVerifier::verify(function);
        if (!verification.ok()) {
            diagnostic = "MachineVerifier rejected X64 MIR";
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }
};

class X64StrikeEmitter {
public:
    struct PendingBranchPatch {
        size_t displacement_offset = 0;
        size_t instruction_end_offset = 0;
        uint32_t target_label = ir::INVALID_SLOT;
    };

    X64StrikeEmitter()
                : target_machine_(core::ArchType::X86_64_AVX2),
                  selector_(lowering::make_builtin_instruction_selector_program()) {}

    void set_target_machine(const core::TargetMachine& target) {
        target_machine_ = target;
    }

    void register_runtime_function(const std::string& name, uintptr_t address) {
        runtime_functions_[name] = address;
    }

    void register_runtime_function_slot(uint32_t slot, uintptr_t address) {
        runtime_slots_[slot] = address;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool had_emission_error() const noexcept {
        return had_error_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] const char* last_emission_error() const noexcept {
        return last_error_.c_str();
    }

    [[nodiscard]] ir::OpCode last_failed_opcode() const noexcept {
        return last_failed_opcode_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] size_t get_label_offset(uint32_t label_id) const noexcept {
        auto it = label_offsets_.find(label_id);
        return it != label_offsets_.end() ? it->second : 0;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool process_function(machine::MachineFunction& mir_function, std::vector<uint8_t>& out) const {
        out.clear();
        label_offsets_.clear();
        had_error_ = false;
        last_error_.clear();
        last_failed_opcode_ = ir::OpCode::NOP;

        const auto arch = target_machine_.get_arch();
        if (arch != core::ArchType::X86_64_AVX2 &&
            arch != core::ArchType::X86_64_AVX512) {
            mark_error("X64 emitter received a non-x86-64 target", ir::OpCode::NOP);
            return false;
        }

        /*
         * Target register allocation is a strict MIR pass.
         */
        mir_function.set_phase(machine::MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc);

        auto allocation =
            dgtl::isa::regalloc::X64MIRRegisterAllocator::run(mir_function);

        /*
         * Register pressure is a normal backend event, not an emission error.
         * Materialize spills into MIR stack slots and retry allocation.
         */
        for (std::uint32_t retry = 0u;
             !allocation.success && !allocation.spilled_virtuals.empty() && retry < 3u;
             ++retry) {
            const auto spill =
                dgtl::isa::regalloc::X64MIRSpillReload::lower(
                    mir_function, allocation.spilled_virtuals);
            if (!spill.success) {
                mark_error("X64 MIR spill/reload lowering failed", ir::OpCode::NOP);
                return false;
            }

            mir_function.set_phase(machine::MachinePhase::PreRegAlloc);
            allocation =
                dgtl::isa::regalloc::X64MIRRegisterAllocator::run(mir_function);
        }

        if (!allocation.success) {
            mark_error(
                allocation.spilled_virtuals.empty()
                    ? "X64 MIR register allocation failed"
                    : "X64 MIR register pressure remains after MIR spill/reload",
                ir::OpCode::NOP);
            return false;
        }

        /*
         * Frame lowering is likewise MIR-native.
         */
        std::vector<machine::MachineRegister> callee_saved;
        for (const auto& instruction : mir_function.instructions()) {
            for (const auto& operand : instruction.operands) {
                if (operand.kind != machine::MachineOperandKind::Register ||
                    !operand.reg.is_physical()) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (operand.reg.reg_class != machine::RegisterClassKind::General &&
                    operand.reg.reg_class != machine::RegisterClassKind::Integer) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (operand.reg.id == static_cast<std::uint32_t>(exec::X64Reg::RBX) ||
                    operand.reg.id == static_cast<std::uint32_t>(exec::X64Reg::R12) ||
                    operand.reg.id == static_cast<std::uint32_t>(exec::X64Reg::R13) ||
                    operand.reg.id == static_cast<std::uint32_t>(exec::X64Reg::R14) ||
                    operand.reg.id == static_cast<std::uint32_t>(exec::X64Reg::R15)) {
                    const bool seen = std::any_of(
                        callee_saved.begin(), callee_saved.end(),
                        [&](const machine::MachineRegister& reg) {
                            return reg.id == operand.reg.id;
                        });
                    if (!seen) callee_saved.push_back(operand.reg);
                }
            }
        }

        if (!dgtl::backend::isa::Win64FrameCalculator::apply_machine_function(
                mir_function, callee_saved)) {
            mark_error("X64 MIR frame lowering failed", ir::OpCode::NOP);
            return false;
        }

        const uint32_t frame_size = mir_function.frame_info().total_frame_bytes;
        if (frame_size == 0u && mir_function.frame_info().has_calls) {
            mark_error("X64 MIR frame layout produced no call frame", ir::OpCode::NOP);
            return false;
        }

        if (mir_function.has_virtual_registers()) {
            mark_error("X64 MIR emission received virtual registers after frame lowering",
                       ir::OpCode::NOP);
            return false;
        }

        const auto post_lowering_verify =
            machine::MachineVerifier::verify(mir_function);
        if (!post_lowering_verify.ok()) {
            mark_error("MachineVerifier rejected allocated/frame-lowered X64 MIR",
                       ir::OpCode::NOP);
            return false;
        }

        std::unordered_map<machine::BlockId, size_t> mir_block_offsets;
        std::vector<PendingBranchPatch> branch_patches;
        branch_patches.reserve(mir_function.instructions().size() / 8u + 4u);

        const bool mir_emitted = emit_mir_native_function(
            mir_function, frame_size, out, mir_block_offsets, branch_patches);
        if (!mir_emitted) {
            if (out.empty()) {
                out.push_back(0xC3);
            }
            return false;
        }

        // --- BACKPATCH MIR BLOCK BRANCHES ---
        for (const PendingBranchPatch& patch : branch_patches) {
            const auto target_it = mir_block_offsets.find(patch.target_label);
            if (target_it == mir_block_offsets.end()) {
                mark_error("Unresolved MIR basic-block branch target", ir::OpCode::GOTO);
                continue;
            }

            const int64_t target_offset = static_cast<int64_t>(target_it->second);
            const int64_t instr_end = static_cast<int64_t>(patch.instruction_end_offset);
            const int64_t rel64 = target_offset - instr_end;
            if (rel64 < static_cast<int64_t>(std::numeric_limits<int32_t>::min()) ||
                rel64 > static_cast<int64_t>(std::numeric_limits<int32_t>::max())) {
                mark_error("MIR branch target displacement out of range", ir::OpCode::GOTO);
                continue;
            }

            if (patch.displacement_offset + 4u > out.size()) {
                mark_error("Invalid MIR branch patch offset", ir::OpCode::GOTO);
                continue;
            }

            const int32_t rel32 = static_cast<int32_t>(rel64);
            out[patch.displacement_offset + 0u] = static_cast<uint8_t>((rel32 >> 0) & 0xFF);
            out[patch.displacement_offset + 1u] = static_cast<uint8_t>((rel32 >> 8) & 0xFF);
            out[patch.displacement_offset + 2u] = static_cast<uint8_t>((rel32 >> 16) & 0xFF);
            out[patch.displacement_offset + 3u] = static_cast<uint8_t>((rel32 >> 24) & 0xFF);
        }

        if (out.empty() || out.back() != 0xC3) {
            emit_add_rsp(out, frame_size);
            out.push_back(0xC3);
        }

        return !had_error_;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool process_stream(const ir::IRStream& stream, std::vector<uint8_t>& out) const {
        out.clear();
        label_offsets_.clear();
        had_error_ = false;
        last_error_.clear();
        last_failed_opcode_ = ir::OpCode::NOP;

        const auto arch = target_machine_.get_arch();
        if (arch != core::ArchType::X86_64_AVX2 &&
            arch != core::ArchType::X86_64_AVX512) {
            mark_error("X64 emitter received a non-x86-64 target", ir::OpCode::NOP);
            return false;
        }

        const auto legalized = legalizer_.legalize(stream, target_machine_);
        const ir::IRStream& machine_stream = legalized.stream;

        machine::MachineFunction mir_function("dgtl.x64");
        std::string mir_diagnostic;
        if (!X64MIRBoundary::build_and_verify(
                target_machine_,
                machine_stream,
                selector_,
                mir_function,
                mir_diagnostic)) {
            mark_error(
                mir_diagnostic.empty()
                    ? "X64 MIR construction/verification failed"
                    : mir_diagnostic.c_str(),
                ir::OpCode::NOP);
            return false;
        }

        return process_function(mir_function, out);
    }

private:
    static void append_u64(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint64_t value) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((value >> (i * 8)) & 0xFFu));
        }
    }

    static void emit_modrm_reg_reg(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint8_t reg, uint8_t rm) {
        const uint8_t modrm = static_cast<uint8_t>(0xC0u | ((reg & 0x7u) << 3u) | (rm & 0x7u));
        out.push_back(modrm);
    }

    static void emit_mov_imm32_signext(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint8_t dst, uint32_t imm32) {
        out.push_back(exec::X64Encoding::rex(/*w=*/true,
                                             /*r=*/false,
                                             /*x=*/false,
                                             /*b=*/requires_rex(dst)));
        out.push_back(0xC7); // MOV r/m64, imm32 (sign-extended)
        emit_modrm_reg_reg(out, 0x00, dst);
        out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(imm32 & 0xFFu));
        out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((imm32 >> 8u) & 0xFFu));
        out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((imm32 >> 16u) & 0xFFu));
        out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((imm32 >> 24u) & 0xFFu));
    }

    static void emit_prefetch_t0_from_base(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint8_t base) {
        out.push_back(exec::X64Encoding::rex(/*w=*/false,
                                             /*r=*/false,
                                             /*x=*/false,
                                             /*b=*/requires_rex(base)));
        out.push_back(0x0F);
        out.push_back(0x18); // PREFETCHh m8

        const uint8_t base_lo = static_cast<uint8_t>(base & 0x7u);
        if (base_lo == 0x4u) {
            out.push_back(0x0Cu); // mod=00, reg=001 (T0), rm=100 (SIB follows)
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x20u | base_lo));
            return;
        }
        if (base_lo == 0x5u) {
            out.push_back(0x4Du); // mod=01, reg=001 (T0), rm=101
            out.push_back(0x00u); // disp8 = 0
            return;
        }

        out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x08u | base_lo));
    }

    static void emit_clflush_from_base(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint8_t base) {
        out.push_back(exec::X64Encoding::rex(/*w=*/false,
                                             /*r=*/false,
                                             /*x=*/false,
                                             /*b=*/requires_rex(base)));
        out.push_back(0x0F);
        out.push_back(0xAE); // CLFLUSH m8 with /7

        const uint8_t base_lo = static_cast<uint8_t>(base & 0x7u);
        if (base_lo == 0x4u) {
            out.push_back(0x3Cu);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x20u | base_lo));
            return;
        }
        if (base_lo == 0x5u) {
            out.push_back(0x7Du);
            out.push_back(0x00u);
            return;
        }

        out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x38u | base_lo));
    }

    /*
     * The compatibility encoder uses signed 32-bit x86 displacement fields.
     * Keep virtual-value homes addressable without modulo aliasing.
     */
    static constexpr std::uint64_t MAX_ADDRESSABLE_VREG =
        (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::int32_t>::max()) - 40ull) / 8ull;

    /*
     * The old implementation used a fixed 131,112-byte frame and
     * `vreg % MAX_FRAME_SLOTS` addressing. That creates silent aliasing:
     * virtual registers separated by 16,384 names address the same stack
     * location. A backend must never trade correctness for a convenient
     * bounded hash.
     *
     * Until MachineFrameInfo stack slots become the sole source of offsets,
     * this compatibility path uses a deterministic one-to-one value-home
     * layout and computes the minimum frame required by the stream.
     */
    static uint32_t compute_frame_size(const ir::IRStream& stream) noexcept {
        std::uint64_t max_vreg = 0u;

        for (std::size_t i = 0; i < stream.size(); ++i) {
            const ir::IRNode node = stream.get_node(i);
            const std::uint32_t ids[] = {
                node.result_id, node.op_a, node.op_b, node.op_c
            };

            for (const std::uint32_t id : ids) {
                if (id != ir::INVALID_SLOT && id != 0u) {
                    max_vreg = std::max<std::uint64_t>(max_vreg, id);
                }
            }
        }

        /*
         * 40 bytes preserves the existing Win64 value-home convention used by
         * this compatibility encoder. Round the required storage to 16 bytes
         * because the prologue/epilogue maintain the ABI stack alignment.
         */
        const std::uint64_t required =
            40ull + ((max_vreg + 1ull) * 8ull);
        const std::uint64_t aligned =
            (required + 15ull) & ~15ull;

        if (aligned > std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max()) {
            return 0u;
        }
        return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(aligned);
    }

    static int32_t vreg_offset(uint32_t vreg) noexcept {
        if (vreg == ir::INVALID_SLOT || vreg == 0u ||
            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(vreg) > MAX_ADDRESSABLE_VREG) {
            return 40;
        }

        const std::uint64_t offset =
            40ull + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(vreg) * 8ull;

        if (offset > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(
                         std::numeric_limits<std::int32_t>::max())) {
            return 40;
        }

        return static_cast<int32_t>(offset);
    }

    static void emit_sub_rsp(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint32_t amount) {
        if (amount == 0) return;
#if defined(_WIN32)
        if (amount > 4096) {
            uint32_t remaining = amount;
            while (remaining > 4096) {
                out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x81); out.push_back(0xEC);
                out.push_back(0x00); out.push_back(0x10); out.push_back(0x00); out.push_back(0x00);
                out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x85); out.push_back(0x04); out.push_back(0x24);
                remaining -= 4096;
            }
            if (remaining > 0) {
                if (remaining <= 127) {
                    out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x83); out.push_back(0xEC);
                    out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(remaining & 0xFF));
                } else {
                    out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x81); out.push_back(0xEC);
                    out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((remaining >> 0) & 0xFF));
                    out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((remaining >> 8) & 0xFF));
                    out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((remaining >> 16) & 0xFF));
                    out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((remaining >> 24) & 0xFF));
                }
                out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x85); out.push_back(0x04); out.push_back(0x24);
            }
            return;
        }
#endif
        if (amount <= 127) {
            out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x83); out.push_back(0xEC);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(amount & 0xFF));
        } else {
            out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x81); out.push_back(0xEC);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((amount >> 0) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((amount >> 8) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((amount >> 16) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((amount >> 24) & 0xFF));
        }
    }

    static void emit_add_rsp(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint32_t amount) {
        if (amount == 0) return;
        if (amount <= 127) {
            out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x83); out.push_back(0xC4);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(amount & 0xFF));
        } else {
            out.push_back(0x48); out.push_back(0x81); out.push_back(0xC4);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((amount >> 0) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((amount >> 8) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((amount >> 16) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((amount >> 24) & 0xFF));
        }
    }

    static void emit_mov_reg_from_stack(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint8_t reg, int32_t offset) {
        out.push_back(exec::X64Encoding::rex(/*w=*/true,
                                             /*r=*/requires_rex(reg),
                                             /*x=*/false,
                                             /*b=*/false));
        out.push_back(0x8B); // MOV r64, [rsp + offset]
        if (offset >= -128 && offset <= 127) {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x44u | ((reg & 0x7u) << 3u)));
            out.push_back(0x24u);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(offset & 0xFF));
        } else {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x84u | ((reg & 0x7u) << 3u)));
            out.push_back(0x24u);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 0) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 8) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 16) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 24) & 0xFF));
        }
    }

    static void emit_mov_stack_from_reg(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, int32_t offset, uint8_t reg) {
        out.push_back(exec::X64Encoding::rex(/*w=*/true,
                                             /*r=*/requires_rex(reg),
                                             /*x=*/false,
                                             /*b=*/false));
        out.push_back(0x89); // MOV [rsp + offset], r64
        if (offset >= -128 && offset <= 127) {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x44u | ((reg & 0x7u) << 3u)));
            out.push_back(0x24u);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(offset & 0xFF));
        } else {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x84u | ((reg & 0x7u) << 3u)));
            out.push_back(0x24u);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 0) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 8) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 16) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 24) & 0xFF));
        }
    }

    static void emit_movsd_xmm_from_stack(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, uint8_t xmm_reg, int32_t offset) {
        out.push_back(0xF2);
        if (requires_rex(xmm_reg)) {
            out.push_back(exec::X64Encoding::rex(/*w=*/false,
                                                 /*r=*/true,
                                                 /*x=*/false,
                                                 /*b=*/false));
        }
        out.push_back(0x0F);
        out.push_back(0x10); // MOVSD xmm, [rsp + offset]
        if (offset >= -128 && offset <= 127) {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x44u | ((xmm_reg & 0x7u) << 3u)));
            out.push_back(0x24u);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(offset & 0xFF));
        } else {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x84u | ((xmm_reg & 0x7u) << 3u)));
            out.push_back(0x24u);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 0) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 8) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 16) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 24) & 0xFF));
        }
    }

    static void emit_movsd_stack_from_xmm(std::vector<uint8_t>& out, int32_t offset, uint8_t xmm_reg) {
        out.push_back(0xF2);
        if (requires_rex(xmm_reg)) {
            out.push_back(exec::X64Encoding::rex(/*w=*/false,
                                                 /*r=*/true,
                                                 /*x=*/false,
                                                 /*b=*/false));
        }
        out.push_back(0x0F);
        out.push_back(0x11); // MOVSD [rsp + offset], xmm
        if (offset >= -128 && offset <= 127) {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x44u | ((xmm_reg & 0x7u) << 3u)));
            out.push_back(0x24u);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(offset & 0xFF));
        } else {
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(0x84u | ((xmm_reg & 0x7u) << 3u)));
            out.push_back(0x24u);
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 0) & 0xFF));
            out.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>((offset >> 8) & 0xFF));
