module;

#include <cstdlib>

#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>
#include <format>
#include <cstdint>
#include <sstream>
#include <functional>
#include <unordered_map>
export module dgtl.isa.x64.nasm_emitter;
import dgtl.mir.runtime_contract;
import dgtl.mir.abi_lowering;
import dgtl.mir.machine_ir;
// Explicit namespace mapping to prevent ambiguity
using dgtl::ir::OpCode;
using dgtl::ir::IRNode;

export namespace dgtl::isa::x64 {

class DGTLNasmEmitter {
    std::string _asm_buffer;

public:
    DGTLNasmEmitter() { 
        _asm_buffer.reserve(1024 * 1024); 
    }

    /**
     * @brief Generates NASM x64 assembly directly from a dgtl::machine::MachineFunction.
     * Pure MIR emission: operates on machine blocks, physical/virtual registers, and machine opcodes.
     */
    void emit(const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function, const dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable* symbols = nullptr) {
        (void)symbols;
        this->clear();

        if (function.blocks().empty()) {
            append_line("; [WARNING] Empty MachineFunction provided. No code generated.");
            return;
        }

        std::string fn_name = function.name().empty() ? "DGTL_main" : std::string(function.name());
        append_line("; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------");
        append_line(std::format("; Function: {}", fn_name));
        append_line("; Generated by DGTL Machine Backend (MIR -> NASM x64)");
        append_line("; ----------------------------------------------------------------------------");
        append_line("default rel");
        append_line("global " + fn_name);
        append_line("section .text");
        append_line(fn_name + ":");

        // Prologue
        const auto& frame = function.frame_info();
        if (frame.total_frame_bytes > 0) {
            append_line("    push rbp");
            append_line("    mov rbp, rsp");
            append_line(std::format("    sub rsp, {}", frame.total_frame_bytes));
        }

        for (const auto& bb : function.blocks()) {
            if (!bb.entry) {
                append_line(std::format(".L{}:", bb.id));
            }

            for (dgtl::machine::InstrId iid : bb.instructions) {
                const auto* inst_ptr = function.instruction(iid);
                if (!inst_ptr) continue;
                const auto& inst = *inst_ptr;

                switch (inst.semantic_kind) {
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::LoadImmediate: {
                        if (!inst.operands.empty() && inst.operands[0].is_register()) {
                            const std::string dst = reg_name_x64(inst.operands[0].reg);
                            const int64_t imm = inst.operands.size() > 1 && inst.operands[1].kind == dgtl::machine::MachineOperandKind::Immediate
                                ? inst.operands[1].immediate : 0;
                            append_line(std::format("    mov {}, {}", dst, imm));
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::Move: {
                        if (inst.operands.size() >= 2 && inst.operands[0].is_register() && inst.operands[1].is_register()) {
                            const std::string dst = reg_name_x64(inst.operands[0].reg);
                            const std::string src = reg_name_x64(inst.operands[1].reg);
                            if (dst != src) {
                                append_line(std::format("    mov {}, {}", dst, src));
                            }
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::IntegerAdd:
                        emit_nasm_binop("add", inst);
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::IntegerSub:
                        emit_nasm_binop("sub", inst);
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::IntegerMul:
                        emit_nasm_binop("imul", inst);
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::IntegerAnd:
                        emit_nasm_binop("and", inst);
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::IntegerOr:
                        emit_nasm_binop("or", inst);
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::IntegerXor:
                        emit_nasm_binop("xor", inst);
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::IntegerShl:
                        emit_nasm_binop("shl", inst);
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::IntegerShr:
                        emit_nasm_binop("sar", inst);
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::Compare:
                        if (inst.operands.size() >= 2) {
                            std::string op0 = reg_name_x64(inst.operands[0].reg);
                            std::string op1 = inst.operands[1].is_register()
                                ? reg_name_x64(inst.operands[1].reg)
                                : std::to_string(inst.operands[1].immediate);
                            append_line(std::format("    cmp {}, {}", op0, op1));
                        }
                        break;
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::Goto: {
                        if (!inst.operands.empty() && inst.operands[0].kind == dgtl::machine::MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock) {
                            append_line(std::format("    jmp .L{}", inst.operands[0].block));
                        } else {
                            append_line("    jmp .Lend");
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::BranchCond: {
                        if (inst.operands.size() >= 2 && inst.operands[1].kind == dgtl::machine::MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock) {
                            append_line(std::format("    jne .L{}", inst.operands[1].block));
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                    case dgtl::machine::MachineSemanticKind::Return: {
                        if (frame.total_frame_bytes > 0) {
                            append_line(std::format("    add rsp, {}", frame.total_frame_bytes));
                            append_line("    pop rbp");
                        }
                        append_line("    ret");
                        break;
                    }
                    default:
                        break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * @brief Updated signature to utilize the dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable.
     * This matches the high-velocity identity resolution Pillar 2.
     */
    void emit(const ir::IRStream& stream, const dgtl::semantic::SymbolTable& symbols) {
        // PERMANENT FIX: Clear previous runs to ensure the assertion 
        // in baked_kernel.cpp sees fresh data.
        this->clear();

        if (stream.size() == 0) {
            append_line("; [WARNING] Empty IR Stream provided. No code generated.");
            return;
        }

        // --- FIX: Pre-scan LABEL nodes to build label_id → stream_index map ---
        // This resolves frontend label IDs in branch targets to the correct .L{n} positions.
        std::unordered_map<uint32_t, size_t> label_map;
        label_map.reserve(stream.size() / 4 + 4);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < stream.size(); ++i) {
            const auto& n = stream.get_node(i);
            if (n.opcode == ir::OpCode::LABEL) {
                if (n.result_id != ir::INVALID_SLOT && n.result_id != 0u) {
                    label_map[n.result_id] = i;
                }
                if (n.op_a != ir::INVALID_SLOT && n.op_a != 0u) {
                    label_map[n.op_a] = i;
                }
                if (n.op_b != ir::INVALID_SLOT && n.op_b != 0u) {
                    label_map[n.op_b] = i;
                }
                label_map[static_cast<uint32_t>(i)] = i;
            }
        }
        // Helper: resolve a branch target through label_map or return as-is
        auto resolve_target = [&](uint32_t raw) -> size_t {
            auto it = label_map.find(raw);
            if (it != label_map.end()) return it->second;
            return stream.size(); // fallback to epilogue
        };
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------------

        append_line("; -----------------------------------------------------------");
        append_line("; DGTL TIER-2 PERSISTENT KERNEL");
        append_line(std::format("; Logic Hash: 0x{:016X}", stream.generate_logic_hash()));
        append_line("; -----------------------------------------------------------");
        append_line("section .text");
        append_line("    global DGTL_kernel_entry");
        append_line("    extern __dgtl_rt_print_i64"); // runtime print hook
        append_line("");
        append_line("DGTL_kernel_entry:");
        
        // --- PROLOGUE: Save Windows x64 Non-Volatile Registers ---
        append_line("    push rbp");
        append_line("    mov  rbp, rsp");
        append_line("    push rbx");
        append_line("    push rsi");
        append_line("    push rdi");
        append_line("    push r12");
        append_line("    push r13");
        append_line("    push r14");
        append_line("    push r15");
        append_line("    sub  rsp, 32 ; Allocate Shadow Space");
        append_line("");

        (void)symbols;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < stream.size(); ++i) {
            const auto& node = stream.get_node(i);
            append_line(std::format(".L{}:", i));
            if (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::NOP) continue;
            if (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::LABEL) continue; // label pseudo-op, no code
            if ((node.opcode == ir::OpCode::LOAD_IMM || node.opcode == ir::OpCode::IMMEDIATE) &&
                is_foldable_ret_immediate(stream, i, node)) {
                continue;
            }

            std::string asm_code = translate_node(stream, node, i, resolve_target);
            
            // Format intent hash for debugging within the ASM
            std::string comment = std::format(" ; [Node {:03d}] Hash:0x{:08X}", i, node.intent_hash);
            append_line(std::format("    {:<45}{}", asm_code, comment));
        }

        // --- EPILOGUE: Restore Registers and Return ---
        append_line(".L_epilogue:");
        append_line("");
        append_line("    add  rsp, 32");
        append_line("    pop  r15");
        append_line("    pop  r14");
        append_line("    pop  r13");
        append_line("    pop  r12");
        append_line("    pop  rdi");
        append_line("    pop  rsi");
        append_line("    pop  rbx");
        append_line("    pop  rbp");
        append_line("    ret");
    }

    const std::string& get_output() const noexcept { return _asm_buffer; }

    void clear() noexcept { 
        _asm_buffer.clear(); 
    }

    /**
     * @brief Emit post-register-allocation MIR as NASM syntax.
     *
     * This is the MIR-native emission boundary.  Unlike the legacy
     * IRStream overload above, this path consumes only MachineFunction
     * state: physical MachineRegisters, MachineOperands, MachineForms,
     * basic-block edges, and MachineFrameInfo.
     *
     * The legacy IRStream overload is intentionally retained for source
     * compatibility.  New x64 backend paths should call this overload after
     * register allocation and frame lowering.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] bool emit_mir(
        const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function,
        std::string_view entry_symbol = "DGTL_kernel_entry") {

        clear();

        if (!function.is_phase_at_least(dgtl::machine::MachinePhase::FrameLowered) ||
            !function.properties().registers_allocated ||
            !function.properties().frame_lowered) {
            append_line("; [ERROR] NASM emission requires FrameLowered MIR");
            return false;
        }

        for (const auto& instruction : function.instructions()) {
            for (const auto& operand : instruction.operands) {
                if (operand.kind == dgtl::machine::MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                    operand.reg.is_virtual()) {
                    append_line("; [ERROR] Virtual register reached NASM emitter");
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        const auto& frame = function.frame_info();
        if (frame.callee_saved_bytes !=
            frame.callee_saved_registers.size() * 8u) {
            append_line("; [ERROR] Win64 frame callee-save byte count is inconsistent");
            return false;
        }
        for (const auto& reg : frame.callee_saved_registers) {
            if (!reg.is_physical() ||
                reg.reg_class != dgtl::machine::RegisterClassKind::General ||
                reg.id == 4u || reg.id == 5u) {
                append_line("; [ERROR] Invalid reserved register in Win64 callee-save plan");
                return false;
            }
        }

        if (frame.total_frame_bytes <
            frame.alignment_padding +
            (frame.has_calls ? 32u : 0u)) {
            append_line("; [ERROR] Win64 frame allocation is inconsistent");
            return false;
        }

        append_line("; -----------------------------------------------------------");
        append_line("; DGTL MIR-NATIVE X64 NASM EMISSION");
        append_line("; -----------------------------------------------------------");
        append_line("bits 64");
        append_line("section .text");
        append_line(std::format("global {}", entry_symbol));
        append_line("");
        append_line(std::format("{}:", entry_symbol));

        emit_mir_prologue(function);

        for (const auto& block : function.blocks()) {
            append_line(std::format(".Lbb{}:", block.id));

            for (const dgtl::machine::InstrId instruction_id : block.instructions) {
                const auto* instruction = function.instruction(instruction_id);
                if (instruction == nullptr) {
                    append_line("; [ERROR] Invalid MIR instruction reference");
                    return false;
                }

                if (!emit_mir_instruction(function, *instruction)) {
                    append_line(std::format(
                        "; [ERROR] Unsupported MIR instruction id={} form={} opcode={}",
                        instruction->id,
                        static_cast<unsigned>(instruction->form),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(instruction->opcode)));
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        // Return terminators branch to one canonical epilogue. Keep the label
        // materialized even when MIR already contains an explicit Return.
        append_line(".L_epilogue:");
        emit_mir_epilogue(function);

        return true;
    }

private:
    [[nodiscard]] static const char* mir_gpr_name(std::uint32_t id) noexcept {
        static constexpr const char* names[] = {
            "rax", "rcx", "rdx", "rbx", "rsp", "rbp", "rsi", "rdi",
            "r8", "r9", "r10", "r11", "r12", "r13", "r14", "r15"
        };
        return id < 16u ? names[id] : nullptr;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static const char* mir_gpr_name32(std::uint32_t id) noexcept {
        static constexpr const char* names[] = {
            "eax", "ecx", "edx", "ebx", "esp", "ebp", "esi", "edi",
            "r8d", "r9d", "r10d", "r11d", "r12d", "r13d", "r14d", "r15d"
        };
        return id < 16u ? names[id] : nullptr;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static std::string mir_register_name(
        const dgtl::machine::MachineRegister& reg) {

        using namespace dgtl::machine;

        if (!reg.valid() || reg.is_virtual()) return {};

        switch (reg.reg_class) {
        case RegisterClassKind::General:
        case RegisterClassKind::Integer:
        case RegisterClassKind::Address: {
            const char* name = mir_gpr_name(reg.id);
            return name != nullptr ? std::string(name) : std::string{};
        }

        case RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint:
            if (reg.id < 16u) {
                return std::format("xmm{}", reg.id);
            }
            return {};

        case RegisterClassKind::Vector:
            if (reg.id >= 16u && reg.id < 48u) {
                return std::format("zmm{}", reg.id - 16u);
            }
            if (reg.id < 32u) {
                return std::format("zmm{}", reg.id);
            }
            return {};

        default:
            return {};
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static std::string mir_memory_operand(
        const dgtl::machine::MachineMemoryOperand& memory) {

        std::string expression;
        const std::string base = mir_register_name(memory.base);
        const std::string index = mir_register_name(memory.index);

        if (!base.empty()) expression += base;

        if (!index.empty()) {
            if (!expression.empty()) expression += " + ";
            expression += index;
            if (memory.scale != 1) {
                expression += std::format("*{}", memory.scale);
            }
        }

        if (memory.displacement != 0 || expression.empty()) {
            if (!expression.empty() && memory.displacement > 0) {
                expression += std::format(" + {}", memory.displacement);
            } else if (!expression.empty() && memory.displacement < 0) {
                expression += std::format(" - {}", -memory.displacement);
            } else {
                expression += std::format("{}", memory.displacement);
            }
        }

        return std::format("[{}]", expression);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static std::string mir_operand_text(
        const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function,
        const dgtl::machine::MachineOperand& operand) {

        using namespace dgtl::machine;

        switch (operand.kind) {
        case MachineOperandKind::Register:
            return mir_register_name(operand.reg);

        case MachineOperandKind::Immediate:
            return std::format("{}", operand.immediate);

        case MachineOperandKind::FloatingImmediate:
            return std::format("{}", operand.floating_immediate);

        case MachineOperandKind::Memory:
            return mir_memory_operand(operand.memory);

        case MachineOperandKind::BasicBlock:
            return std::format(".Lbb{}", operand.block);

        case MachineOperandKind::Symbol:
            return std::format("_DGTL_symbol_{}", operand.symbol);

        case MachineOperandKind::StackSlot:
            if (operand.stack_slot < function.frame_info().slots.size()) {
                const auto& slot = function.frame_info().slots[operand.stack_slot];
                return std::format("[rbp{:+}]", slot.offset);
            }
            return {};

        default:
            return {};
        }
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static std::vector<std::string> mir_operands(
        const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function,
        const dgtl::machine::MachineInstruction& instruction) {

        std::vector<std::string> operands;
        for (const auto& operand : instruction.operands) {
            if (operand.kind == dgtl::machine::MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                operand.role == dgtl::machine::OperandRole::Def) {
                operands.push_back(mir_operand_text(function, operand));
            }
        }
        for (const auto& operand : instruction.operands) {
            if (operand.kind == dgtl::machine::MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                operand.role == dgtl::machine::OperandRole::Def) {
                continue;
            }
            const std::string text = mir_operand_text(function, operand);
            if (!text.empty()) operands.push_back(text);
        }
        return operands;
    }

    void emit_mir_prologue(
        const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function) {

        const auto& frame = function.frame_info();

        append_line("    push rbp");
        append_line("    mov rbp, rsp");

        // Frame lowering is authoritative: the emitter consumes the exact
        // physical non-volatile register list recorded in MachineFrameInfo.
        // It never rediscovers ABI state by scanning MIR instructions.
        for (const auto& reg : frame.callee_saved_registers) {
            const std::string name = mir_register_name(reg);
            if (name.empty()) {
                append_line("; [ERROR] Invalid Win64 callee-saved register in frame plan");
                return;
            }
            append_line(std::format("    push {}", name));
        }

        if (frame.total_frame_bytes != 0) {
            append_line(std::format("    sub rsp, {}", frame.total_frame_bytes));
        }
        append_line("");
    }

    void emit_mir_epilogue(
        const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function) {

        const auto& frame = function.frame_info();

        if (frame.total_frame_bytes != 0) {
            append_line(std::format("    add rsp, {}", frame.total_frame_bytes));
        }

        for (auto it = frame.callee_saved_registers.rbegin();
             it != frame.callee_saved_registers.rend(); ++it) {
            const std::string name = mir_register_name(*it);
            if (name.empty()) {
                append_line("; [ERROR] Invalid Win64 callee-saved register in frame plan");
                return;
            }
            append_line(std::format("    pop {}", name));
        }

        append_line("    pop rbp");
        append_line("    ret");
    }

    [[nodiscard]] bool emit_mir_instruction(
        const dgtl::machine::MachineFunction& function,
        const dgtl::machine::MachineInstruction& instruction) {

        using namespace dgtl::machine;

        const auto operands = mir_operands(function, instruction);
        const auto get = [&](std::size_t index) -> const std::string& {
            static const std::string empty;
            return index < operands.size() ? operands[index] : empty;
        };

        switch (instruction.form) {
        case MachineForm::NoOp:
            append_line("    nop");
            return true;

        case MachineForm::Move: {
            if (instruction.operands.size() < 2u) return false;
            const auto& dst = instruction.operands[0];
            const auto& src = instruction.operands[1];
            if (dst.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register) return false;

            const std::string dst_name = mir_register_name(dst.reg);
            const std::string src_name = mir_operand_text(function, src);
            if (dst_name.empty() || src_name.empty()) return false;

            if (dst.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint) {
                const bool f32 = dst.subregister == 32u;
                append_line(std::format("    {} {}, {}",
                    f32 ? "movss" : "movsd", dst_name, src_name));
                return true;
            }
            if (dst.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Vector) {
                return false; // Vector moves require width-aware AVX lowering.
            }
            append_line(std::format("    mov {}, {}", dst_name, src_name));
            return true;
        }

        case MachineForm::LoadImmediate:
            if (operands.size() >= 2) {
                append_line(std::format("    mov {}, {}", get(0), get(1)));
                return true;
            }
            return false;

        case MachineForm::IntegerBinary32:
        case MachineForm::IntegerBinary64: {
            if (instruction.operands.size() < 3u) return false;
            const ir::OpCode op = static_cast<ir::OpCode>(instruction.semantic_opcode);
            const char* mnemonic = nullptr;
            switch (op) {
            case ir::OpCode::ADD_I32: case ir::OpCode::ADD_I64: mnemonic = "add"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::SUB_I32: case ir::OpCode::SUB_I64: mnemonic = "sub"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::MUL_I32: case ir::OpCode::MUL_I64: mnemonic = "imul"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::AND_I32: case ir::OpCode::AND_I64: mnemonic = "and"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::OR_I32:  case ir::OpCode::OR_I64:  mnemonic = "or"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::XOR_I32: case ir::OpCode::XOR_I64: mnemonic = "xor"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::SHL_I32: case ir::OpCode::SHL_I64: mnemonic = "shl"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::SHR_I32: case ir::OpCode::SHR_I64: mnemonic = "shr"; break;
            default: return false;
            }
            const auto& dst = instruction.operands[0];
            const auto& lhs = instruction.operands[1];
            const auto& rhs = instruction.operands[2];
            if (dst.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register) return false;

            const bool is32 = instruction.form == MachineForm::IntegerBinary32;
            const char* dst32 = is32 ? mir_gpr_name32(dst.reg.id) : mir_gpr_name(dst.reg.id);
            const std::string dst_name = dst32 ? std::string(dst32) : std::string{};
            std::string lhs_name = mir_operand_text(function, lhs);
            std::string rhs_name = mir_operand_text(function, rhs);
            if (is32 && lhs.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                const char* n = mir_gpr_name32(lhs.reg.id);
                if (!n) return false;
                lhs_name = n;
            }
            if (is32 && rhs.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register) {
                const char* n = mir_gpr_name32(rhs.reg.id);
                if (!n) return false;
                rhs_name = n;
            }
            if (dst_name.empty() || lhs_name.empty() || rhs_name.empty()) return false;

            // DGTL MIR is SSA-shaped, so the result register is not assumed
            // to alias the first source. Materialize the two-address x86
            // destination explicitly.
            if (dst_name != lhs_name) {
                append_line(std::format("    mov {}, {}", dst_name, lhs_name));
            }

            if (op == ir::OpCode::SHL_I32 || op == ir::OpCode::SHL_I64 ||
                op == ir::OpCode::SHR_I32 || op == ir::OpCode::SHR_I64) {
                if (rhs.kind == MachineOperandKind::Immediate) {
                    append_line(std::format("    {} {}, {}", mnemonic, dst_name, rhs_name));
                    return true;
                }
                if (rhs.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register ||
                    rhs.reg.id != static_cast<std::uint32_t>(exec::X64Reg::RCX)) {
                    return false;
                }
                append_line(std::format("    {} {}, cl", mnemonic, dst_name));
                return true;
            }

            append_line(std::format("    {} {}, {}", mnemonic, dst_name, rhs_name));
            return true;
        }

        case MachineForm::FloatBinary32:
        case MachineForm::FloatBinary64: {
            if (operands.size() < 3) return false;
            const ir::OpCode op = static_cast<ir::OpCode>(instruction.semantic_opcode);
            const char* mnemonic = nullptr;
            switch (op) {
            case ir::OpCode::ADD_F32: mnemonic = "addss"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::SUB_F32: mnemonic = "subss"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::MUL_F32: mnemonic = "mulss"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::DIV_F32: mnemonic = "divss"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::ADD_F64: mnemonic = "addsd"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::SUB_F64: mnemonic = "subsd"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::MUL_F64: mnemonic = "mulsd"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::DIV_F64: mnemonic = "divsd"; break;
            default: return false;
            }
            append_line(std::format("    {} {}, {}", mnemonic, get(0), get(2)));
            return true;
        }

        case MachineForm::FloatTernaryFma32:
            if (operands.size() >= 4) {
                append_line(std::format(
                    "    vfmadd213ss {}, {}, {}",
                    get(0), get(2), get(3)));
                return true;
            }
            return false;

        case MachineForm::Compare32: {
            if (operands.size() < 3) return false;
            const ir::OpCode op = static_cast<ir::OpCode>(instruction.semantic_opcode);
            const char* condition = nullptr;
            switch (op) {
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_EQ_I32: condition = "e"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_NE_I32: condition = "ne"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_LT_I32: condition = "l"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_LE_I32: condition = "le"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_GT_I32: condition = "g"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_GE_I32: condition = "ge"; break;
            default: return false;
            }
            append_line(std::format("    cmp {}, {}", get(1), get(2)));
            append_line(std::format("    set{} al", condition));
            append_line(std::format("    movzx {}, al", get(0)));
            return true;
        }

        case MachineForm::LoadStack:
        case MachineForm::StoreStack: {
            if (instruction.operands.size() < 2u) return false;

            const auto& first = instruction.operands[0];
            const auto& second = instruction.operands[1];

            if (instruction.form == MachineForm::LoadStack) {
                if (first.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register) return false;
                const std::string dst = mir_register_name(first.reg);
                const std::string src = mir_operand_text(function, second);
                if (dst.empty() || src.empty()) return false;

                if (first.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint) {
                    const bool f32 = first.subregister == 32u;
                    append_line(std::format("    {} {}, {}",
                        f32 ? "movss" : "movsd", dst, src));
                    return true;
                }
                if (first.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Vector) {
                    return false;
                }
                append_line(std::format("    mov {}, {}", dst, src));
                return true;
            }

            // StoreStack: source register -> stack slot. The old emitter
            // accidentally reversed this direction.
            if (first.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register) return false;
            const std::string src = mir_register_name(first.reg);
            const std::string dst = mir_operand_text(function, second);
            if (src.empty() || dst.empty()) return false;

            if (first.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint) {
                const bool f32 = first.subregister == 32u;
                append_line(std::format("    {} {}, {}",
                    f32 ? "movss" : "movsd", dst, src));
                return true;
            }
            if (first.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Vector) {
                return false;
            }
            append_line(std::format("    mov {}, {}", dst, src));
            return true;
        }

        case MachineForm::Goto:
            if (!operands.empty()) {
                append_line(std::format("    jmp {}", get(0)));
                return true;
            }
            return false;

        case MachineForm::BranchCond:
            if (operands.size() >= 2) {
                append_line(std::format("    test {0}, {0}", get(0)));
                append_line(std::format("    jnz {}", get(1)));
                return true;
            }
            return false;

        case MachineForm::BranchCompare: {
            if (operands.size() < 3u) return false;
            const ir::OpCode op = static_cast<ir::OpCode>(instruction.semantic_opcode);
            const char* cc = nullptr;
            switch (op) {
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_EQ_I32: cc = "e"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_NE_I32: cc = "ne"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_LT_I32: cc = "l"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_LE_I32: cc = "le"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_GT_I32: cc = "g"; break;
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_GE_I32: cc = "ge"; break;
            default:
                return false;
            }
            append_line(std::format("    cmp {}, {}", get(0), get(1)));
            append_line(std::format("    j{} {}", cc, get(2)));
            return true;
        }

        case MachineForm::Return: {
            // Win64 ABI return registers are RAX for integer/pointer values
            // and XMM0 for scalar FP values. ABI placement is finalized here
            // only as a last-mile encoding of the already physical MIR.
            if (!instruction.operands.empty()) {
                const auto& value = instruction.operands[0];
                if (value.kind != MachineOperandKind::Register ||
                    !value.reg.is_physical()) {
                    return false;
                }
                const std::string src = mir_register_name(value.reg);
                if (src.empty()) return false;

                if (value.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::FloatingPoint) {
                    const bool f32 = value.subregister == 32u;
                    const char* move = f32 ? "movss" : "movsd";
                    if (src != "xmm0") {
                        append_line(std::format("    {} xmm0, {}", move, src));
                    }
                } else if (value.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::General ||
                           value.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Integer ||
                           value.reg.reg_class == RegisterClassKind::Address) {
                    if (src != "rax") {
                        append_line(std::format("    mov rax, {}", src));
                    }
                } else {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            append_line("    jmp .L_epilogue");
            return true;
        }

        case MachineForm::Call: {
            // ABI lowering is responsible for argument placement. The NASM
            // emitter only encodes the already-lowered call target.
            for (const auto& operand : instruction.operands) {
                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::Symbol) {
                    const std::string target = mir_operand_text(function, operand);
                    if (target.empty()) return false;
                    append_line(std::format("    call {}", target));
                    return true;
                }
                if (operand.kind == MachineOperandKind::Register &&
                    !operand.reg.is_virtual()) {
                    const std::string target = mir_register_name(operand.reg);
                    if (target.empty()) return false;
                    append_line(std::format("    call {}", target));
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        case MachineForm::HardwareFence:
            append_line("    mfence");
            return true;

        case MachineForm::Syscall:
            append_line("    syscall");
            return true;

        default:
            // No fabricated instruction text: unsupported MIR forms fail
            // closed and remain visible to the backend diagnostics.
            return false;
        }
    }

    static bool is_foldable_ret_immediate(const ir::IRStream& stream, size_t load_idx, const ir::IRNode& load_node) {
        if (load_node.result_id == ir::INVALID_SLOT) return false;

        bool used_by_ret = false;
        for (size_t i = load_idx + 1; i < stream.size(); ++i) {
            const auto& node = stream.get_node(i);
            if (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::NOP) continue;

            const bool references_reg =
                node.op_a == load_node.result_id ||
                node.op_b == load_node.result_id ||
                node.op_c == load_node.result_id;

            if (!references_reg) continue;

            if (node.opcode == ir::OpCode::RET && node.op_a == load_node.result_id && !used_by_ret) {
                used_by_ret = true;
                continue;
            }

            return false;
        }

        return used_by_ret;
    }

    static bool resolve_ret_immediate(const ir::IRStream& stream, uint32_t reg, uint64_t& imm_out) {
        for (size_t i = stream.size(); i-- > 0;) {
            const auto& node = stream.get_node(i);
            if (node.opcode != ir::OpCode::LOAD_IMM && node.opcode != ir::OpCode::IMMEDIATE &&
                node.opcode != ir::OpCode::STRING_CONST) continue;
            if (node.result_id != reg) continue;
            if (node.intent_hash != 0) {
                imm_out = node.intent_hash;
            } else {
                const uint32_t high = (node.op_a == ir::INVALID_SLOT) ? 0u : node.op_a;
                imm_out = (static_cast<uint64_t>(high) << 32) | (node.op_b & 0xFFFFFFFFULL);
            }
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    void append_line(std::string_view line) {
        _asm_buffer.append(line);
        _asm_buffer.append("\n");
    }

    std::string translate_node(const ir::IRStream& stream,
                               const ir::IRNode& node,
                               size_t node_idx,
                               const std::function<size_t(uint32_t)>& resolve_target) {
        auto r = [](uint32_t idx) -> const char* {
            static constexpr const char* regs[] = { 
                "rax", "rbx", "rcx", "rdx", "rsi", "rdi", "r8", "r9",
                "r10", "r11", "r12", "r13", "r14", "r15", "rbx", "rcx"
            };
            return regs[idx & 0xF];
        };

        auto xr = [](uint32_t idx) -> const char* {
            static constexpr const char* xregs[] = {
                "xmm0", "xmm1", "xmm2", "xmm3", "xmm4", "xmm5", "xmm6", "xmm7",
                "xmm8", "xmm9", "xmm10", "xmm11", "xmm12", "xmm13", "xmm14", "xmm15"
            };
            return xregs[idx & 0xF];
        };

        auto emit_runtime_call_text = [&](const char* symbol) -> std::string {
            std::string code = "sub rsp, 32";
            code += std::format(" \n    mov rcx, {}", r(node.op_a));
            code += std::format(" \n    mov rdx, {}", r(node.op_b));
            code += std::format(" \n    mov r8, {}", r(node.op_c));
            code += std::format(" \n    call {}", symbol);
            code += " \n    add rsp, 32";
            if (node.result_id != ir::INVALID_SLOT) {
                code += std::format(" \n    mov {}, rax", r(node.result_id));
            }
            return code;
        };

        switch (node.opcode) {
            case ir::OpCode::LOAD_IMM:
            case ir::OpCode::IMMEDIATE:
            case ir::OpCode::STRING_CONST: {
                uint64_t imm = node.intent_hash;
                if (imm == 0) {
                    const uint32_t high = (node.op_a == ir::INVALID_SLOT) ? 0u : node.op_a;
                    imm = (static_cast<uint64_t>(high) << 32) | (node.op_b & 0xFFFFFFFF);
                }
                return std::format("mov {}, {}", r(node.result_id), imm);
            }

            case ir::OpCode::ADD_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::ADD_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    add {0}, {2}", 
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("add {}, {}", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::SUB_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::SUB_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    sub {0}, {2}",
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("sub {}, {}", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::MUL_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::MUL_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    imul {0}, {2}",
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("imul {}, {}", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::ADD_F32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movss {0}, {1} \n    addss {0}, {2}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("addss {}, {}", xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::SUB_F32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movss {0}, {1} \n    subss {0}, {2}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("subss {}, {}", xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::MUL_F32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movss {0}, {1} \n    mulss {0}, {2}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("mulss {}, {}", xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::DIV_F32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movss {0}, {1} \n    divss {0}, {2}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("divss {}, {}", xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::ADD_F64: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movsd {0}, {1} \n    addsd {0}, {2}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("addsd {}, {}", xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::SUB_F64: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movsd {0}, {1} \n    subsd {0}, {2}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("subsd {}, {}", xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::MUL_F64: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movsd {0}, {1} \n    mulsd {0}, {2}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("mulsd {}, {}", xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::DIV_F64: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movsd {0}, {1} \n    divsd {0}, {2}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("divsd {}, {}", xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::FMA_F32: {
                // Preserve fused lowering in text backend: dst = (a * b) + c
                // using scalar FMA when available.
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movaps {0}, {1} \n    vfmadd213ss {0}, {2}, {3}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b), xr(node.op_c));
                }
                return std::format("vfmadd213ss {0}, {1}, {2}",
                                   xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b), xr(node.op_c));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::DGTL_FAST_FMA: {
                // DGTL fast f64 FMA path.
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("movapd {0}, {1} \n    vfmadd213sd {0}, {2}, {3}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b), xr(node.op_c));
                }
                return std::format("vfmadd213sd {0}, {1}, {2}",
                                   xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_b), xr(node.op_c));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::SIMD_COMPLEX_FMA: {
                // Conservative vectorized complex-FMA lowering: dst = c + a*b over packed f32.
                if (node.result_id != node.op_c) {
                    return std::format("vmovaps {0}, {1} \n    vfmadd213ps {0}, {2}, {3}",
                                       xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_c), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("vfmadd231ps {0}, {1}, {2}",
                                   xr(node.result_id), xr(node.op_a), xr(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::DIV_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::DIV_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != ir::INVALID_SLOT && std::string_view(r(node.result_id)) != "rax") {
                    return std::format("mov rax, {} \n    cqo \n    idiv {} \n    mov {}, rax",
                                       r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
                }
                return std::format("mov rax, {} \n    cqo \n    idiv {}", r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::MOD_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::MOD_I32: {
                return std::format("mov rax, {} \n    cqo \n    idiv {} \n    mov {}, rdx",
                                   r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::CMP_EQ_I32: {
                return std::format("cmp {}, {} \n    sete al \n    movzx {}, al",
                                   r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_NE_I32: {
                return std::format("cmp {}, {} \n    setne al \n    movzx {}, al",
                                   r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_LT_I32: {
                return std::format("cmp {}, {} \n    setl al \n    movzx {}, al",
                                   r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_LE_I32: {
                return std::format("cmp {}, {} \n    setle al \n    movzx {}, al",
                                   r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_GT_I32: {
                return std::format("cmp {}, {} \n    setg al \n    movzx {}, al",
                                   r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }
            case ir::OpCode::CMP_GE_I32: {
                return std::format("cmp {}, {} \n    setge al \n    movzx {}, al",
                                   r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::AND_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::AND_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    and {0}, {2}",
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("and {}, {}", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::OR_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::OR_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    or {0}, {2}",
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("or {}, {}", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::XOR_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::XOR_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    xor {0}, {2}",
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("xor {}, {}", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::SHL_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::SHL_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    mov rcx, {2} \n    shl {0}, cl",
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("mov rcx, {} \n    shl {}, cl", r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::SHR_I64:
            case ir::OpCode::SHR_I32: {
                if (node.result_id != node.op_a) {
                    return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    mov rcx, {2} \n    shr {0}, cl",
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));
                }
                return std::format("mov rcx, {} \n    shr {}, cl", r(node.op_b), r(node.result_id));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::LOAD: {
                return std::format("mov {}, [{} + {}]", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), node.op_c);
            }

            case ir::OpCode::STORE: {
                return std::format("mov [{} + {}], {}", r(node.op_a), node.op_c, r(node.op_b));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::REG_MOVE: {
                return std::format("mov {}, {}", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::RET: {
                if (node.op_a != ir::INVALID_SLOT) {
                    uint64_t imm = 0;
                    if (resolve_ret_immediate(stream, node.op_a, imm)) {
                        return std::format("mov rax, {} \n    jmp .L_epilogue", imm);
                    }
                    return std::format("mov rax, {} \n    jmp .L_epilogue", r(node.op_a));
                }
                return "jmp .L_epilogue";
            }

            case ir::OpCode::GATHER_F32_512: {
                return std::format("vmovups zmm{}, [{} + {}]", 
                                   node.result_id % 32, r(node.op_a), node.op_c);
            }

            case ir::OpCode::BRANCH:
            case ir::OpCode::GOTO:
                return std::format("jmp .L{}", resolve_target(node.op_a));

            case ir::OpCode::BRANCH_COND:
                // op_a = condition register, op_b = target block label
                return std::format("test {0}, {0} \n    jnz .L{1}",
                                   r(node.op_a), resolve_target(node.op_b));

            case ir::OpCode::BRANCH_LIKELY:
            case ir::OpCode::BRANCH_UNLIKELY:
                // Keep textual emitter semantically aligned with branch-cond lowering.
                return std::format("test {0}, {0} \n    jnz .L{1}",
                                   r(node.op_a), resolve_target(node.op_b));

            case ir::OpCode::BRANCH_COMPARE_FUSED:
                // op_a = lhs register, op_b = rhs register, op_c = target block label
                return std::format("cmp {}, {} \n    jne .L{}", r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), resolve_target(node.op_c));

            case ir::OpCode::PHI_NODE:
                return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    test {2}, {2} \n    cmovne {0}, {3}",
                                   r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_c), r(node.op_b));

            case ir::OpCode::BOOL_SELECT:
            case ir::OpCode::SELECT:
                return std::format("mov {0}, {2} \n    test {1}, {1} \n    cmovne {0}, {3}",
                                   r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_c), r(node.op_b));

            case ir::OpCode::GEP:
                return std::format("lea {0}, [{1} + {2}]",
                                   r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));

            case ir::OpCode::PTR_TO_INT:
            case ir::OpCode::INT_TO_PTR:
                return std::format("mov {0}, {1}", r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a));

            case ir::OpCode::GUARD_VALUE:
                return std::format("mov {0}, {3} \n    cmp {1}, {2} \n    cmove {0}, {1}",
                                   r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.op_c));

            case ir::OpCode::GUARD_ALIAS:
                return std::format("mov {0}, 1 \n    cmp {1}, {2} \n    cmovne {0}, {3}",
                                   r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), r(node.op_c));

            case ir::OpCode::PREFETCH:
            case ir::OpCode::PREFETCH_T0:
                return std::format("prefetcht0 [{}]", r(node.op_a));

            case ir::OpCode::CLFLUSH:
                return std::format("clflush [{}]", r(node.op_a));

            case ir::OpCode::READ_TSC:
                return std::format("rdtsc \n    shl rdx, 32 \n    or rax, rdx \n    mov {0}, rax", r(node.result_id));

            case ir::OpCode::PTR_DIFF:
                return std::format("mov {0}, {1} \n    sub {0}, {2}",
                                   r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b));

            case ir::OpCode::LEA: {
                const uint32_t scale = node.op_c;
                if (scale == 1u || scale == 2u || scale == 4u || scale == 8u) {
                    return std::format("lea {0}, [{1} + {2}*{3}]",
                                       r(node.result_id), r(node.op_a), r(node.op_b), scale);
                }
                if (const char* symbol = dgtl::mir::runtime_contract::runtime_symbol_for_opcode(node.opcode); symbol != nullptr) {
                    return emit_runtime_call_text(symbol);
                }
                return std::format("nop ; Unsupported Op: 0x{:X}", static_cast<int>(node.opcode));
            }

            case ir::OpCode::ACQUIRE_FENCE:
                return "lfence";

            case ir::OpCode::RELEASE_FENCE:
                return "sfence";

            case ir::OpCode::ATOMIC_FENCE:
            case ir::OpCode::MEMORY_FENCE:
            case ir::OpCode::BARRIER_SYNC:
            case ir::OpCode::SYNC_BARRIER:
                return "mfence";

            case ir::OpCode::CALL: {
                std::vector<uint32_t> args;
                args.reserve(8);
                if (node.op_b != ir::INVALID_SLOT) args.push_back(node.op_b);
                if (node.op_c != ir::INVALID_SLOT) args.push_back(node.op_c);
                if (node.logic_flags != ir::INVALID_SLOT) args.push_back(node.logic_flags);
                const auto extra_args = stream.get_call_overflow_args(node_idx);
                std::copy_if(extra_args.begin(), extra_args.end(), std::back_inserter(args),
                             [](uint32_t extra) { return extra != ir::INVALID_SLOT; });

                auto map_reg_name = [](uint32_t reg_id) -> const char* {
                    switch (reg_id) {
                        case 0u: return "rdi";
                        case 1u: return "rsi";
                        case 2u: return "rdx";
                        case 3u: return "rcx";
                        case 4u: return "r8";
                        case 5u: return "r9";
                        // Microsoft x64 canonical integer arg order IDs from ABI_Lowering.
                        case 6u: return "rax";
                        case 7u: return "rbx";
                        case 8u: return "rsp";
                        case 9u: return "rbp";
                        case 10u: return "rsi";
                        case 11u: return "rdi";
                        case 12u: return "r8";
                        case 13u: return "r9";
                        case 14u: return "r10";
                        case 15u: return "r11";
                        default: return nullptr;
                    }
                };

                auto map_xmm_index = [](uint32_t reg_id) -> int {
                    if (reg_id >= 16u && reg_id <= 31u) {
                        return static_cast<int>(reg_id - 16u);
                    }
                    return -1;
                };

                auto is_ms_integer_arg_id = [](uint32_t reg_id) -> bool {
                    return reg_id == 2u || reg_id == 3u || reg_id == 4u || reg_id == 5u;
                };

                auto map_ms_arg_reg_name = [](uint32_t reg_id) -> const char* {
                    switch (reg_id) {
                        case 3u: return "rcx";
                        case 0u: return "rdi";
                        case 2u: return "rdx";
                        case 4u: return "r8";
                        case 5u: return "r9";
                        default: return nullptr;
                    }
                };

                const std::vector<uint32_t> layout_words = stream.get_call_abi_layout_words(node_idx);
                std::string cs;
                if (layout_words.size() >= 4) {
                    const uint32_t param_count = layout_words[0];
                    const uint32_t stack_size = layout_words[1];
                    const size_t required_words = 4u + static_cast<size_t>(param_count) * 4u;

                    if (layout_words.size() >= required_words && stack_size > 0u) {
                        cs += std::format("sub rsp, {}", stack_size);
                    }

                    if (layout_words.size() >= required_words) {
                        size_t w = 4u;
                        const size_t count = std::min<size_t>(param_count, args.size());
                        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i, w += 4u) {
                            const bool in_register = (layout_words[w] != 0u);
                            const uint32_t reg_id = layout_words[w + 1u];
                            const uint32_t stack_offset = layout_words[w + 2u];

                            if (in_register) {
                                const int xmm_idx = map_xmm_index(reg_id);
                                if (xmm_idx >= 0) {
                                    if (!cs.empty()) cs += " \n    ";
                                    cs += std::format("movq xmm{}, {}", xmm_idx, r(args[i]));
                                } else {
                                    const char* dst = is_ms_integer_arg_id(reg_id)
                                        ? map_ms_arg_reg_name(reg_id)
                                        : map_reg_name(reg_id);
                                    if (dst != nullptr) {
                                        if (!cs.empty()) cs += " \n    ";
                                        cs += std::format("mov {}, {}", dst, r(args[i]));
                                    }
                                }
                            } else {
                                if (!cs.empty()) cs += " \n    ";
                                cs += std::format("mov rax, {}", r(args[i]));
                                cs += std::format(" \n    mov [rsp + {}], rax", stack_offset);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    if (!cs.empty()) cs += " \n    ";
                    cs += std::format("call _DGTL_fn_{}", node.op_a);
                    if (layout_words.size() >= required_words && stack_size > 0u) {
                        cs += std::format(" \n    add rsp, {}", stack_size);
                    }
                } else {
                    cs = "sub rsp, 32";
                    if (node.op_b != ir::INVALID_SLOT)
                        cs += std::format(" \n    mov rcx, {}", r(node.op_b));
                    if (node.op_c != ir::INVALID_SLOT)
                        cs += std::format(" \n    mov rdx, {}", r(node.op_c));
                    cs += std::format(" \n    call _DGTL_fn_{}", node.op_a);
                    cs += " \n    add rsp, 32";
                }
                return cs;
            }

            case ir::OpCode::SYSCALL:
                return "syscall";

            default:
                if (const char* symbol = dgtl::mir::runtime_contract::runtime_symbol_for_opcode(node.opcode); symbol != nullptr) {
                    return emit_runtime_call_text(symbol);
                }
                // Stable fallback to keep output buildable.
                return std::format("nop ; Unsupported Op: 0x{:X}", static_cast<int>(node.opcode));
        }
    }

    static std::string reg_name_x64(dgtl::machine::MachineRegister reg) {
        static constexpr const char* kRegNames[] = {
            "rax", "rcx", "rdx", "rbx", "rsp", "rbp", "rsi", "rdi",
            "r8",  "r9",  "r10", "r11", "r12", "r13", "r14", "r15"
        };
        if (reg.id < 16) {
            return kRegNames[reg.id];
        }
        return "r" + std::to_string(reg.id);
    }

    void emit_nasm_binop(std::string_view op, const dgtl::machine::MachineInstruction& inst) {
        if (inst.operands.empty()) return;
        std::string dst = reg_name_x64(inst.operands[0].reg);
        if (inst.operands.size() >= 3 && inst.operands[1].is_register() && inst.operands[2].is_register()) {
            std::string src1 = reg_name_x64(inst.operands[1].reg);
            std::string src2 = reg_name_x64(inst.operands[2].reg);
            if (dst != src1) {
                append_line(std::format("    mov {}, {}", dst, src1));
            }
            append_line(std::format("    {} {}, {}", op, dst, src2));
        } else if (inst.operands.size() >= 2) {
            std::string src = inst.operands[1].is_register()
                ? reg_name_x64(inst.operands[1].reg)
                : std::to_string(inst.operands[1].immediate);
            append_line(std::format("    {} {}, {}", op, dst, src));
        }
    }
};

} // namespace dgtl::isa::x64

module;

#include <cstdlib>

// Global Module Fragment: Capture core memory layout requirements safely
#include <bit>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <type_traits>

export module dgtl.ir_node;

// Dependency Ordered Named Module Imports
export import dgtl.ir_enums;
import dgtl.ir_schema;
import dgtl.ir.gen;

// HIR representation only: target lowering/MIR state must not be stored here.
// Semantic classification is derived exclusively from canonical schema metadata.

export namespace dgtl::ir {

struct IRCore {
    std::uint32_t result_id = 0u;

    std::uint32_t op_a = 0u;
    std::uint32_t op_b = 0u;
    std::uint32_t op_c = 0u;

    std::uint64_t intent_hash = 0ull;

    std::uint32_t logic_flags = 0u;
    std::uint32_t aux_flags   = 0u;

    std::uint32_t debug_id    = 0u;
    std::uint32_t epoch       = 0u;
};

#pragma pack(push, 2)
struct IRAtom {
    OpCode opcode = OpCode::NOP;
    std::uint16_t flags = 0;

    constexpr IRAtom() = default;
    constexpr IRAtom(OpCode op, std::uint16_t fl) noexcept : opcode(op), flags(fl) {}

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr operator std::uint32_t() const noexcept {
        return (static_cast<std::uint32_t>(opcode) << 16) | flags;
    }

    constexpr IRAtom& operator=(std::uint32_t value) noexcept {
        opcode = static_cast<OpCode>(value >> 16);
        flags = static_cast<std::uint16_t>(value & 0xFFFFu);
        return *this;
    }
};
#pragma pack(pop)

struct AtomSplitter {
    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr OpCode get_opcode(IRAtom atom) noexcept {
        return atom.opcode;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::uint16_t get_flags(IRAtom atom) noexcept {
        return atom.flags;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr IRAtom pack(OpCode op, std::uint16_t flags) noexcept {
        return IRAtom(op, flags);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr IRAtom pack(std::uint32_t value) noexcept {
        return IRAtom(
            static_cast<OpCode>(value >> 16),
            static_cast<std::uint16_t>(value & 0xFFFFu)
        );
    }
};

namespace detail {

[[nodiscard]] inline constexpr bool irnode_is_branch(OpCode op) noexcept {
    return gen::op_has_trait(op, gen::GenTrait::IsTerminator) &&
           gen::op_implements_interface(op, gen::GenInterface::ControlFlow);
}

[[nodiscard]] inline constexpr bool irnode_has_side_effects(
    OpCode op, std::uint16_t flags) noexcept {
    if ((flags & Flags::HAS_SIDE_EFFECTS) != 0u) return true;
    if ((flags & Flags::IS_VOLATILE) != 0u) return true;
    if (!schema::is_canonical(op)) return true;
    return !gen::op_has_trait(op, gen::GenTrait::HasNoSideEffects);
}

[[nodiscard]] inline constexpr double irnode_inline_weight(OpCode op) noexcept {
    if (irnode_is_branch(op)) return 5.0;
    if (gen::op_implements_interface(op, gen::GenInterface::SIMD) ||
        gen::op_has_trait(op, gen::GenTrait::IsVectorizable)) return 3.0;
    return 1.0;
}

} // namespace detail

#pragma pack(push, 1)

struct alignas(32) IRNode {
    OpCode        opcode = OpCode::NOP;
    std::uint16_t flags = 0;

    std::uint32_t result_id = 0;
    std::uint32_t op_a = 0;
    std::uint32_t op_b = 0;
    std::uint32_t op_c = 0;
    std::uint64_t intent_hash = 0;
    std::uint32_t logic_flags = 0;

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_terminator() const noexcept {
        return (flags & Flags::IS_RETURN) != 0u ||
               gen::op_has_trait(opcode, gen::GenTrait::IsTerminator);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_volatile() const noexcept {
        return (flags & Flags::IS_VOLATILE) != 0u;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_side_effects() const noexcept {
        return detail::irnode_has_side_effects(opcode, flags);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_valid() const noexcept {
        return opcode != OpCode::NOP;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint64_t logic_hash() const noexcept {
        return intent_hash ^ (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(result_id) << 32);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr double predict_inlined_benefit() const noexcept {
        return detail::irnode_inline_weight(opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_memory_read() const noexcept {
        return gen::op_has_trait(opcode, gen::GenTrait::IsMemoryRead);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_memory_write() const noexcept {
        return gen::op_has_trait(opcode, gen::GenTrait::IsMemoryWrite);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_constant_foldable() const noexcept {
        return gen::op_has_trait(opcode, gen::GenTrait::ConstantFoldable);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr bool has_result() const noexcept {
        if (const auto* def = gen::find_generated_op_def(opcode)) return def->has_result;
        return false;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr std::uint16_t stable_opcode_id() const noexcept {
        return schema::stable_id(opcode);
    }

    [[nodiscard]] constexpr IRNode clone() const noexcept { return *this; }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::uint64_t calculate_hash(OpCode op,
                                                                 std::uint32_t res,
                                                                 std::uint32_t a,
                                                                 std::uint32_t b,
                                                                 std::uint32_t c) noexcept {
        std::uint64_t h = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(op) ^ 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        auto mix = [&](std::uint32_t val) {
            h ^= (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(val) + 0x517cc1b727220a95ULL + (h << 6) + (h >> 2));
        };
        mix(res); mix(a); mix(b); mix(c);
        return h;
    }

    [[nodiscard]] static constexpr std::uint64_t calculate_semantic_hash(
        OpCode op, std::uint32_t a, std::uint32_t b, std::uint32_t c,
        std::uint16_t flags = Flags::NONE) noexcept {
        std::uint64_t h = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(op) ^ 0xD6E8FEB86659FD93ULL;
        auto mix = [&](std::uint64_t val) {
            h ^= val + 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ULL + (h << 6) + (h >> 2);
        };
        mix(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(flags));
        mix(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a));
        mix(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b));
        mix(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(c));
        return h;
    }

    constexpr void refresh_intent_hash() noexcept {
        intent_hash = calculate_hash(opcode, result_id, op_a, op_b, op_c);
    }

    // Constant folding is intentionally implemented by HIR transformation passes.
    // IRNode is a passive representation and never owns rewrite semantics.


};

#pragma pack(pop)

static_assert(sizeof(IRNode) == 32,      "IRNode must be exactly 32 bytes for cache-line aligned HIR storage contract");
static_assert(alignof(IRNode) == 32,     "IRNode must have 32-byte alignment for cache-line-aligned canonical HIR storage");
static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<IRNode>,  "IRNode must be trivially copyable for transactional HIR storage");
static_assert(std::is_standard_layout_v<IRNode>,     "IRNode must be standard-layout for deterministic HIR object layout");
static_assert(offsetof(IRNode, opcode)      ==  0,   "IRNode::opcode must be at offset 0 - stable HIR layout");
static_assert(offsetof(IRNode, flags)       ==  2,   "IRNode::flags must be at offset 2 - stable HIR layout");
static_assert(offsetof(IRNode, result_id)   ==  4,   "IRNode::result_id must be at offset 4 - stable HIR layout");
static_assert(offsetof(IRNode, op_a)        ==  8,   "IRNode::op_a must be at offset 8 - stable HIR layout");
static_assert(offsetof(IRNode, op_b)        == 12,   "IRNode::op_b must be at offset 12 - stable HIR layout");
static_assert(offsetof(IRNode, op_c)        == 16,   "IRNode::op_c must be at offset 16 - stable HIR layout");
static_assert(offsetof(IRNode, intent_hash) == 20,   "IRNode::intent_hash must be at offset 20 - stable HIR layout");
static_assert(offsetof(IRNode, logic_flags) == 28,   "IRNode::logic_flags must be at offset 28 - stable HIR layout");
static_assert(sizeof(float)  == 4, "float must be 32-bit IEEE 754 on conforming IEEE-754 C++ implementations");
static_assert(sizeof(double) == 8, "double must be 64-bit IEEE 754 on conforming IEEE-754 C++ implementations");
static_assert(sizeof(int)    == 4, "int must be 32-bit on all DGTL-supported platforms");

static_assert(sizeof(IRAtom)  == 4,  "IRAtom must be exactly 4 bytes - SoA stream width contract");
static_assert(alignof(IRAtom) == 2,  "IRAtom must be 2-byte aligned for packed stream access");
static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<IRAtom>, "IRAtom must be trivially copyable");

static_assert(sizeof(IRCore)  == 40, "IRCore must be exactly 40 bytes - tier-2 SoA stride contract");
static_assert(std::is_trivially_copyable_v<IRCore>, "IRCore must be trivially copyable for shadow-buffer streaming");
static_assert(std::is_standard_layout_v<IRCore>,    "IRCore must be standard-layout for stable HIR layout");

} // namespace dgtl::ir
